Deliberately permissive regex — this only screens // out addresses with no @ or no dot in the domain, not a full RFC 5322 // check, since rejecting a real-but-unusual address is worse than letting // Resend's own delivery failure surface a truly bad one. const EMAIL_PATTERN = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/; function isValidEmail(email) { return EMAIL_PATTERN.test(email); } async function upsertBudgetCell(env, table, { regionId, lineOfBusiness, fiscalYear, amount }, actorUserId) { assertValidBudgetTable(table); if (!VALID_LOBS.includes(lineOfBusiness)) return { error: 'Invalid line of business.', status: 400 }; if (!isValidFiscalYear(fiscalYear)) return { error: 'Invalid fiscal year.', status: 400 }; const unlockCheck = await assertFiscalYearUnlocked(env, fiscalYear); if (unlockCheck) return unlockCheck; const region = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM regions WHERE id = ?').bind(regionId).first(); if (!region) return { error: 'Region not found.', status: 404 }; const id = randomToken(16); await env.DB.prepare( `INSERT INTO ${table} (id, region_id, line_of_business, fiscal_year, amount, updated_by, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?) ON CONFLICT(region_id, line_of_business, fiscal_year) DO UPDATE SET amount = excluded.amount, updated_by = excluded.updated_by, updated_at = excluded.updated_at` ).bind(id, regionId, lineOfBusiness, fiscalYear, amount, actorUserId, new Date().toISOString()).run(); return { ok: true }; } // Batch-clear version of the above (v1o, 2026-09-06): range-select + // Delete on the Sales Budgets annual grid. Same shape as // upsertRevenueBudgetMonthlyCellsBatch below (one D1 batch() call, atomic, // every cell shares fiscalYear/table and validation or none land) but for // the region x LOB annual grid instead of one region/LOB's 12 months — // cells here are {regionId, lineOfBusiness} pairs, always writing amount:0. // Kept generic over table (VALID_BUDGET_TABLES) rather than hardcoded to // sales_budgets, matching upsertBudgetCell's own shape, but in practice // only sales_budgets is reachable through this: Revenue's annual grid is // derived/read-only (see the GET revenue-budgets route's comment) so a // call here for revenue_budgets is rejected the same way a single-cell // POST to /api/baseline/revenue-budgets already is. async function clearBudgetCellsBatch(env, table, { fiscalYear, cells }, actorUserId) { if (!VALID_BUDGET_TABLES.has(table)) return { error: 'Invalid budget table.', status: 400 }; if (table === 'revenue_budgets') return { error: 'Revenue Budget figures are derived from Monthly Detail and cannot be edited here.', status: 400 }; if (!isValidFiscalYear(fiscalYear)) return { error: 'Invalid fiscal year.', status: 400 }; if (!Array.isArray(cells) || cells.length === 0) return { error: 'At least one cell is required.', status: 400 }; if (cells.length > 100) return { error: 'A single clear can cover at most 100 cells.', status: 400 }; for (const c of cells) { if (!VALID_LOBS.includes(c.lineOfBusiness)) return { error: 'Invalid line of business.', status: 400 }; if (!c.regionId) return { error: 'Invalid region.', status: 400 }; } const unlockCheck = await assertFiscalYearUnlocked(env, fiscalYear); if (unlockCheck) return unlockCheck; const regionIds = [...new Set(cells.map(c => c.regionId))]; const placeholders = regionIds.map(() => '?').join(','); const foundRegions = await env.DB.prepare(`SELECT id FROM regions WHERE id IN (${placeholders})`).bind(...regionIds).all(); const foundIds = new Set((foundRegions.results || []).map(r => r.id)); for (const id of regionIds) { if (!foundIds.has(id)) return { error: 'Region not found.', status: 404 }; } const now = new Date().toISOString(); const stmts = cells.map(c => env.DB.prepare( `INSERT INTO ${table} (id, region_id, line_of_business, fiscal_year, amount, updated_by, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, 0, ?, ?) ON CONFLICT(region_id, line_of_business, fiscal_year) DO UPDATE SET amount = 0, updated_by = excluded.updated_by, updated_at = excluded.updated_at` ).bind(randomToken(16), c.regionId, c.lineOfBusiness, fiscalYear, actorUserId, now)); await env.DB.batch(stmts); return { ok: true, cellsCleared: cells.length }; } // ---- Baseline: Setup Baseline wizard, fiscal year lock (Worker batch // C, 2026-09-04) ---- // // One row per fiscal year. A year with no row is treated as unlocked // (the common case pre-launch and for the current working year), so // this table only ever needs a row written the moment a year is first // locked, never seeded up front. Unlock is a real admin action (clears // locked_at/locked_by rather than deleting the row, so "who locked this // originally" and "who last unlocked it" both stay visible via // unlocked_at/unlocked_by), matches the mockup's own copy: "an admin // has to explicitly unlock the year first, this isn't reversible by // accident." // // Every revenue_budgets / sales_budgets / revenue_budgets_monthly write // route below now checks this before writing (assertFiscalYearUnlocked) // so a lock is real enforcement, not just a wizard-screen visual state, // closing the gap the mockup's own callout flagged (a locked year must // be genuinely read-only everywhere, not just inside the wizard). async function getFiscalYearLock(env, fiscalYear) { const row = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT * FROM fiscal_year_locks WHERE fiscal_year = ?').bind(fiscalYear).first(); return row || null; } async function assertFiscalYearUnlocked(env, fiscalYear) { const lock = await getFiscalYearLock(env, fiscalYear); if (lock && lock.locked_at) { return { error: `Fiscal year ${fiscalYear} is locked. An admin must unlock it before this can be edited.`, status: 409 }; } return null; } // Reconciliation check backing Step 4's hard block (mockup: "the totals // need to reconcile before you can lock"). Compares each LOB's summed // regional entries (active regions only) against that LOB's company // target row, for both revenue_budgets and sales_budgets independently. // Returns the exact dollar gaps, not just a pass/fail flag, since the // mockup's recon-banner shows the real numbers, not a generic message. async function checkFiscalYearReconciliation(env, fiscalYear) { const gaps = []; for (const table of ['revenue_budgets', 'sales_budgets']) { const regionSums = await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT b.line_of_business AS lob, SUM(b.amount) AS region_total FROM ${table} b JOIN regions r ON r.id = b.region_id WHERE b.fiscal_year = ? AND r.active = 1 GROUP BY b.line_of_business` ).bind(fiscalYear).all(); const sums = Object.fromEntries((regionSums.results || []).map(r => [r.lob, r.region_total || 0])); // Company target per LOB isn't its own row today, Step 2's applied // total is what's stored as each region's target implicitly via the // scenario, so until that's resolved (see Apply Scenario note below) // the target used here is the sum of all regions' entries at the // moment Step 2 was last saved, tracked via company_targets below. const targetRows = await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT line_of_business AS lob, amount FROM budget_company_targets WHERE table_name = ? AND fiscal_year = ?` ).bind(table, fiscalYear).all(); const targets = Object.fromEntries((targetRows.results || []).map(r => [r.lob, r.amount || 0])); for (const lob of VALID_LOBS) { const regionTotal = sums[lob] || 0; const target = targets[lob]; if (target === undefined) continue; // no target set yet for this LOB this year, nothing to reconcile against const gap = Math.round((regionTotal - target) * 100) / 100; if (Math.abs(gap) > 0.01) { gaps.push({ table, lineOfBusiness: lob, regionTotal, target, gap }); } } } return gaps; } async function lockFiscalYear(env, fiscalYear, actorUserId, { acknowledgeGaps = false } = {}) { if (!isValidFiscalYear(fiscalYear)) return { error: 'Invalid fiscal year.', status: 400 }; const existing = await getFiscalYearLock(env, fiscalYear); if (existing && existing.locked_at) return { error: `Fiscal year ${fiscalYear} is already locked.`, status: 409 }; const gaps = await checkFiscalYearReconciliation(env, fiscalYear); if (gaps.length > 0 && !acknowledgeGaps) { return { error: 'Reconciliation gaps must be resolved or explicitly acknowledged before locking.', status: 409, gaps }; } const now = new Date().toISOString(); await env.DB.prepare( `INSERT INTO fiscal_year_locks (fiscal_year, locked_at, locked_by, unlocked_at, unlocked_by, acknowledged_gaps) VALUES (?, ?, ?, NULL, NULL, ?) ON CONFLICT(fiscal_year) DO UPDATE SET locked_at = excluded.locked_at, locked_by = excluded.locked_by, unlocked_at = NULL, unlocked_by = NULL, acknowledged_gaps = excluded.acknowledged_gaps` ).bind(fiscalYear, now, actorUserId, gaps.length > 0 ? JSON.stringify(gaps) : null).run(); return { ok: true, gapsAcknowledged: gaps.length > 0 ? gaps : undefined }; } async function unlockFiscalYear(env, fiscalYear, actorUserId) { if (!isValidFiscalYear(fiscalYear)) return { error: 'Invalid fiscal year.', status: 400 }; const existing = await getFiscalYearLock(env, fiscalYear); if (!existing || !existing.locked_at) return { error: `Fiscal year ${fiscalYear} is not locked.`, status: 409 }; await env.DB.prepare( `UPDATE fiscal_year_locks SET locked_at = NULL, locked_by = NULL, unlocked_at = ?, unlocked_by = ? WHERE fiscal_year = ?` ).bind(new Date().toISOString(), actorUserId, fiscalYear).run(); return { ok: true }; } // ---- Baseline: Setup Baseline wizard, company-wide LOB targets // (Worker batch C, 2026-09-04) ---- // // Backs Step 2 (Line of Business Totals) and the Step 3 reconciliation // check above. Deliberately its own table rather than a derived SUM of // regional entries: Step 2's number is a real, directly-entered target // (pre-filled from Step 1's applied scenario, but editable independent // of it, same "adjust by hand" pattern as revenue_budgets_monthly vs. // revenue_budgets), and Step 3's regions are allocated against it, not // the other way around. // // Apply Scenario mechanics (resolved 2026-09-04, was an open question // in the mockup): applying a scenario OVERWRITES this table's row for // each LOB outright. It does not save a separate comparison reference. // Rationale: Growth Scenario is explicitly framed in-product as a // sandbox ("nothing here is saved... until you explicitly apply it"), // the apply action is the commit point, matching the mockup's own // wording ("Adjust either total by hand if you don't want the // scenario's number to stick"). Revisit if Misty wants a comparison // mode later; nothing here blocks adding one. async function listCompanyTargets(env, table, fiscalYear) { assertValidBudgetTable(table); const rows = await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT * FROM budget_company_targets WHERE table_name = ? AND fiscal_year = ?` ).bind(table, fiscalYear).all(); return rows.results || []; } async function upsertCompanyTarget(env, table, { lineOfBusiness, fiscalYear, amount }, actorUserId) { assertValidBudgetTable(table); if (!VALID_LOBS.includes(lineOfBusiness)) return { error: 'Invalid line of business.', status: 400 }; if (!isValidFiscalYear(fiscalYear)) return { error: 'Invalid fiscal year.', status: 400 }; const unlockCheck = await assertFiscalYearUnlocked(env, fiscalYear); if (unlockCheck) return unlockCheck; await env.DB.prepare( `INSERT INTO budget_company_targets (table_name, line_of_business, fiscal_year, amount, updated_by, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?) ON CONFLICT(table_name, line_of_business, fiscal_year) DO UPDATE SET amount = excluded.amount, updated_by = excluded.updated_by, updated_at = excluded.updated_at` ).bind(table, lineOfBusiness, fiscalYear, Number(amount) || 0, actorUserId, new Date().toISOString()).run(); return { ok: true }; } // Applies a Growth Scenario's per-LOB results directly to // budget_company_targets for the given table (revenue_budgets or // sales_budgets), overwriting any existing target row for that // LOB/year, see the mechanics note above. `lobAmounts` is // { program_advisory: number, design_services: number, inspections: number }. async function applyGrowthScenario(env, table, fiscalYear, lobAmounts, actorUserId) { assertValidBudgetTable(table); if (!isValidFiscalYear(fiscalYear)) return { error: 'Invalid fiscal year.', status: 400 }; const unlockCheck = await assertFiscalYearUnlocked(env, fiscalYear); if (unlockCheck) return unlockCheck; for (const lob of VALID_LOBS) { if (lobAmounts[lob] === undefined) continue; const result = await upsertCompanyTarget(env, table, { lineOfBusiness: lob, fiscalYear, amount: lobAmounts[lob] }, actorUserId); if (result.error) return result; } return { ok: true }; } // ---- Baseline: Revenue Budgets monthly seasonality (§55.2, Worker // batch A, 2026-09-04; reversed 2026-09-06, see below) ---- // // Sales Budgets has NO equivalent — it stays annual-only by design, and // its "monthly" pace anywhere in this app (Month Pace, Regional Drivers) // is always computed as annual÷12 at query time, never stored. Only // Revenue Budgets gets a real monthly table, since that's the only // budget type with real seasonal source data (the workbook's "Final 26' // Budget Seasonality" block). // // REVERSED 2026-09-06, per Misty's explicit instruction: originally // deliberately independent from revenue_budgets.amount (§55.2, "keep // them independent") — that decision no longer holds. The annual // Revenue Budget is now DERIVED as the sum of these monthly rows (see // listRevenueBudgetsAnnualDerived below and the revenue-budgets GET // route); revenue_budgets.amount is no longer written to or read as a // source of truth anywhere. A region/LOB with zero monthly rows sums to // $0 on purpose (confirmed: "some are entered as 0 meaning they didn't // have a budget" — there is no fallback to an old typed-in annual // value). async function listRevenueBudgetsMonthly(env, fiscalYear) { const rows = await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT * FROM revenue_budgets_monthly WHERE fiscal_year = ?` ).bind(fiscalYear).all(); return rows.results || []; } // The Revenue Budget annual grid, derived: sums revenue_budgets_monthly // per region/LOB. Single shared implementation for the GET route (Baseline // screen) and Forecast's Month Pace / Regional Drivers, which both used to // read the old, now-unused revenue_budgets table directly as their // annual-total fallback — kept in this one place so the definition of // "annual total" can't drift between the screen you edit it on and the // screens that just display it. async function listRevenueBudgetsAnnualDerived(env, fiscalYear) { const monthlyRows = await listRevenueBudgetsMonthly(env, fiscalYear); const sums = new Map(); // "regionId|lob" -> amount for (const r of monthlyRows) { const key = `${r.region_id}|${r.line_of_business}`; sums.set(key, (sums.get(key) || 0) + (r.amount || 0)); } return [...sums.entries()].map(([key, amount]) => { const [regionId, lineOfBusiness] = key.split('|'); return { region_id: regionId, line_of_business: lineOfBusiness, fiscal_year: fiscalYear, amount }; }); } async function upsertRevenueBudgetMonthlyCell(env, { regionId, lineOfBusiness, fiscalYear, month, amount }, actorUserId) { if (!VALID_LOBS.includes(lineOfBusiness)) return { error: 'Invalid line of business.', status: 400 }; if (!isValidFiscalYear(fiscalYear)) return { error: 'Invalid fiscal year.', status: 400 }; if (!isValidMonth(month)) return { error: 'Invalid month.', status: 400 }; const unlockCheck = await assertFiscalYearUnlocked(env, fiscalYear); if (unlockCheck) return unlockCheck; const region = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM regions WHERE id = ?').bind(regionId).first(); if (!region) return { error: 'Region not found.', status: 404 }; const id = randomToken(16); await env.DB.prepare( `INSERT INTO revenue_budgets_monthly (id, region_id, line_of_business, fiscal_year, month, amount, updated_by, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?) ON CONFLICT(region_id, line_of_business, fiscal_year, month) DO UPDATE SET amount = excluded.amount, updated_by = excluded.updated_by, updated_at = excluded.updated_at` ).bind(id, regionId, lineOfBusiness, fiscalYear, month, amount, actorUserId, new Date().toISOString()).run(); return { ok: true }; } // Batch version of the above, for pasting a row of months at once (e.g. // copying Jan-Dec straight out of Misty's real Excel gating workbook and // pasting into one Monthly Detail row) — one D1 batch() call, atomic, all // cells share the same region/LOB/fiscalYear and validation, or land // together. Same per-cell shape ({month, amount}) repeated, not a // different endpoint contract from the single-cell version, so the // frontend can fall back to the single-cell POST unchanged for a normal // one-cell edit. async function upsertRevenueBudgetMonthlyCellsBatch(env, { regionId, lineOfBusiness, fiscalYear, cells }, actorUserId) { if (!VALID_LOBS.includes(lineOfBusiness)) return { error: 'Invalid line of business.', status: 400 }; if (!isValidFiscalYear(fiscalYear)) return { error: 'Invalid fiscal year.', status: 400 }; if (!Array.isArray(cells) || cells.length === 0) return { error: 'At least one cell is required.', status: 400 }; if (cells.length > 12) return { error: 'A single paste can cover at most 12 months.', status: 400 }; for (const c of cells) { if (!isValidMonth(c.month)) return { error: 'Invalid month.', status: 400 }; } const unlockCheck = await assertFiscalYearUnlocked(env, fiscalYear); if (unlockCheck) return unlockCheck; const region = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM regions WHERE id = ?').bind(regionId).first(); if (!region) return { error: 'Region not found.', status: 404 }; const now = new Date().toISOString(); const stmts = cells.map(c => env.DB.prepare( `INSERT INTO revenue_budgets_monthly (id, region_id, line_of_business, fiscal_year, month, amount, updated_by, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?) ON CONFLICT(region_id, line_of_business, fiscal_year, month) DO UPDATE SET amount = excluded.amount, updated_by = excluded.updated_by, updated_at = excluded.updated_at` ).bind(randomToken(16), regionId, lineOfBusiness, fiscalYear, c.month, Number(c.amount) || 0, actorUserId, now)); await env.DB.batch(stmts); return { ok: true, cellsSaved: cells.length }; } // Clear version for Monthly Detail's range-select + Delete (v1o, // 2026-09-06). Distinct from upsertRevenueBudgetMonthlyCellsBatch above // rather than reusing it: that function is scoped to one region/LOB row // (matching a single paste), capped at 12 cells, but a selected range here // can span multiple LOB rows within the one region currently open on // Monthly Detail (the screen only ever shows one region's rows at a time, // so regionId is a single shared value, not per-cell). Every cleared cell // still shares fiscalYear/regionId; only lineOfBusiness and month vary // per cell. async function clearRevenueBudgetMonthlyCellsBatch(env, { regionId, fiscalYear, cells }, actorUserId) { if (!isValidFiscalYear(fiscalYear)) return { error: 'Invalid fiscal year.', status: 400 }; if (!Array.isArray(cells) || cells.length === 0) return { error: 'At least one cell is required.', status: 400 }; if (cells.length > 36) return { error: 'A single clear can cover at most 36 cells (all LOBs, all months).', status: 400 }; for (const c of cells) { if (!VALID_LOBS.includes(c.lineOfBusiness)) return { error: 'Invalid line of business.', status: 400 }; if (!isValidMonth(c.month)) return { error: 'Invalid month.', status: 400 }; } const unlockCheck = await assertFiscalYearUnlocked(env, fiscalYear); if (unlockCheck) return unlockCheck; const region = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM regions WHERE id = ?').bind(regionId).first(); if (!region) return { error: 'Region not found.', status: 404 }; const now = new Date().toISOString(); const stmts = cells.map(c => env.DB.prepare( `INSERT INTO revenue_budgets_monthly (id, region_id, line_of_business, fiscal_year, month, amount, updated_by, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 0, ?, ?) ON CONFLICT(region_id, line_of_business, fiscal_year, month) DO UPDATE SET amount = 0, updated_by = excluded.updated_by, updated_at = excluded.updated_at` ).bind(randomToken(16), regionId, c.lineOfBusiness, fiscalYear, c.month, actorUserId, now)); await env.DB.batch(stmts); return { ok: true, cellsCleared: cells.length }; } // "Suggest from history" (v1p, 2026-09-06): a read-only computation, not // a write — nothing is saved here, it just returns suggested monthly // figures for the frontend to fill into blank cells, which are then // saved (or edited first) exactly like any other Monthly Detail entry. // Per Misty's direct correction: this is for filling in months that // haven't been entered yet for the year being built, never for // overwriting real data entry, so it deliberately takes the CURRENT // monthly rows for fiscalYear as an input and only returns suggestions // for months missing from them (isValidMonth(month) with no matching row, // or an explicit amount of 0 — both read the same way Monthly Detail // already treats an unentered month, see listRevenueBudgetsAnnualDerived's // own "$0 and absent mean the same thing" comment above). // // Shape, confirmed with Misty: for one region, all 3 LOBs at once (the // screen is already scoped to one region via its picker) — // 1. Prior fiscal year's monthly BUDGET shape, from // revenue_budgets_monthly (same table this screen reads/writes, // just fiscalYear - 1): each month's % of that year's total. // 2. Prior fiscal year's monthly ACTUALS shape, from billed_fact, // joined to line_of_business via the existing // mapRevenueTypeToLob/REVENUE_TYPE_TO_LOB helper (billed_fact has no // line_of_business column, only revenue_type). // 3. Blend the two shapes 50/50 per month. If only one of the two // exists for a given region/LOB (the other has zero prior-year // rows), use that one at 100% rather than failing or defaulting to a // flat split — confirmed directly with Misty. // 4. Apply the blended shape to prior fiscal year's ACTUAL annual total // for that region/LOB (sum of billed_fact, not a wizard/company // target — confirmed directly with Misty: there is no per-region // "new year target" until Monthly Detail itself is filled in, which // is exactly what this feature is helping build). // If NEITHER prior-year budget nor prior-year actuals exist for a // region/LOB, that LOB is simply omitted from the response (nothing to // base a suggestion on) rather than inventing a number — same principle // as standing rule 7 (never invent a value to fill a gap). async function suggestRevenueBudgetMonthlyFromHistory(env, { regionId, fiscalYear }) { if (!isValidFiscalYear(fiscalYear)) return { error: 'Invalid fiscal year.', status: 400 }; const region = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM regions WHERE id = ?').bind(regionId).first(); if (!region) return { error: 'Region not found.', status: 404 }; const priorYear = fiscalYear - 1; // Prior-year monthly budget, this region, all LOBs. const priorBudgetRows = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT line_of_business, month, amount FROM revenue_budgets_monthly WHERE region_id = ? AND fiscal_year = ?` ).bind(regionId, priorYear).all()).results || []; const priorBudgetByLob = {}; // lob -> { month: amount } for (const r of priorBudgetRows) { if (!priorBudgetByLob[r.line_of_business]) priorBudgetByLob[r.line_of_business] = {}; priorBudgetByLob[r.line_of_business][r.month] = (priorBudgetByLob[r.line_of_business][r.month] || 0) + (r.amount || 0); } // Prior-year monthly actuals, this region, all revenue_types, mapped // to LOB (billed_fact has no line_of_business column). const priorActualRows = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT revenue_type, month, SUM(amount) AS total FROM billed_fact WHERE region_id = ? AND fiscal_year = ? GROUP BY revenue_type, month` ).bind(regionId, priorYear).all()).results || []; const priorActualByLob = {}; // lob -> { month: amount } for (const r of priorActualRows) { const lob = mapRevenueTypeToLob(r.revenue_type); if (!lob) continue; // unmapped revenue_type: excluded, not guessed at if (!priorActualByLob[lob]) priorActualByLob[lob] = {}; priorActualByLob[lob][r.month] = (priorActualByLob[lob][r.month] || 0) + (r.total || 0); } // Current (fiscalYear) monthly rows for this region, so already- // entered months can be excluded from the response entirely. const currentRows = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT line_of_business, month FROM revenue_budgets_monthly WHERE region_id = ? AND fiscal_year = ?` ).bind(regionId, fiscalYear).all()).results || []; const alreadyEntered = new Set(currentRows.map(r => `${r.line_of_business}|${r.month}`)); function shapeFrom(byMonth) { const total = Object.values(byMonth).reduce((s, v) => s + v, 0); if (total === 0) return null; const shape = {}; for (let m = 1; m <= 12; m++) shape[m] = (byMonth[m] || 0) / total; return shape; } const suggestions = {}; // lob -> { month: amount }, months already entered omitted for (const lob of VALID_LOBS) { const budgetShape = shapeFrom(priorBudgetByLob[lob] || {}); const actualShape = shapeFrom(priorActualByLob[lob] || {}); if (!budgetShape && !actualShape) continue; // nothing to base a suggestion on for this LOB const blendedShape = {}; for (let m = 1; m <= 12; m++) { if (budgetShape && actualShape) blendedShape[m] = (budgetShape[m] + actualShape[m]) / 2; else blendedShape[m] = (budgetShape || actualShape)[m]; } const priorActualTotal = Object.values(priorActualByLob[lob] || {}).reduce((s, v) => s + v, 0); if (priorActualTotal === 0) continue; // no real dollar basis to spread, even if a shape exists const lobSuggestions = {}; for (let m = 1; m <= 12; m++) { if (alreadyEntered.has(`${lob}|${m}`)) continue; // never touch a month with real data entry lobSuggestions[m] = Math.round(blendedShape[m] * priorActualTotal); } if (Object.keys(lobSuggestions).length > 0) suggestions[lob] = lobSuggestions; } return { ok: true, priorYear, suggestions }; } // ---- Weekly Uploads: bookings / backlog / proposal pipeline / proposal // volume (§24/§34/§44) -- // // Rows are parsed from the source .xls (really an HTML table, per §28/§34 // — Salesforce's export convention, not binary Excel) in the browser and // POSTed here as plain JSON arrays, in batches (BATCH_INSERT_SIZE rows per // D1 batch() call — these files run 12K-50K+ rows, well past what a single // D1 statement/round-trip should carry). Each upload REPLACES the whole // table (2026-09-08, see saveUploadRows) since each of these four is a // full current-state extract, not incremental new activity — was pure // append-only from §24 through 2026-09-08, which double-counted every // row still present in two overlapping uploads (caught live by Misty). // Rows still carry upload_batch_id for provenance (who/when/which file), // but a prior upload's rows no longer coexist with the current one, so // there's no more multi-snapshot history to query by batch within these // tables — only the single most recent upload's rows exist at any time. // // One shared region-resolution helper, but four distinct column mappings // — the four file types share an export mechanism, not a column // structure (confirmed directly by Misty, §34: "different file structures, // headings, etc"). const VALID_UPLOAD_TYPES = new Set(['bookings', 'backlog', 'proposal_pipeline', 'proposal_volume', 'billed_fact', 'billed_line_item', 'coverage_consultants', 'coverage_inspectors']); const BATCH_INSERT_SIZE = 100; // rows per D1 batch() call // Real raw-Salesforce-region -> canonical region_id lookups, loaded once // per upload rather than one query per row (these files run 10K-50K rows). async function loadRegionMap(env) { const rows = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT raw_value, region_id FROM region_raw_mappings').all(); const map = new Map(); for (const r of (rows.results || [])) map.set(r.raw_value, r.region_id); return map; } function resolveRegionId(regionMap, rawValue) { if (!rawValue) return null; return regionMap.get(String(rawValue).trim()) || null; } // Unmapped Regions review (2026-09-10, per Misty's direct request after // catching a real Revenue YTD discrepancy traced to unmapped billed_fact // rows: "where do I go for the unmapped regions" -> mockup approved -> // "lets move forward"). Replaces the old "contact your admin" upload // toast (unmappedRegions was computed fresh every upload and never // persisted) with a real, permanent queue: unmapped_region_log // accumulates row count/dollar impact per raw value across repeated // uploads until mapped, resolved_region_log keeps a permanent record of // what's already been resolved. Dollar field differs per source table // (billed_fact/billed_line_item_fact use amount, bookings/proposal_volume // use total_price, backlog uses contract_balance, proposal_pipeline uses // total_vda_fees) — DOLLAR_FIELD_BY_SOURCE keeps that mapping in one // place rather than repeating a switch at every call site. const DOLLAR_FIELD_BY_SOURCE = { bookings: 'total_price', backlog: 'contract_balance', proposal_pipeline: 'total_vda_fees', proposal_volume: 'total_price', billed_fact: 'amount', billed_line_item: 'amount', }; const UNMAPPED_SOURCE_LABELS = { bookings: 'Bookings', backlog: 'Backlog', proposal_pipeline: 'Proposal Pipeline', proposal_volume: 'Proposal Volume', billed_fact: 'Billed Fact', billed_line_item: 'Billed Line Item Fact', }; // Called once per upload (after rows are inserted) with the same `mapped` // array the insert loop already built and the sourceType key above. Logs // one row per distinct raw_value with region_raw set but region_id null, // accumulating row_count/dollar_impact into any existing log entry for // that same raw value from a prior upload (UPSERT on the raw_value // primary key) rather than overwriting it — the whole point is that this // doesn't reset every week. Cheap no-op when nothing is unmapped. async function logUnmappedRegions(env, sourceType, mapped, uploadBatchId) { const dollarField = DOLLAR_FIELD_BY_SOURCE[sourceType]; const bySRaw = new Map(); // raw_value -> { rowCount, dollarImpact } for (const r of mapped) { if (!r.region_raw || r.region_id) continue; const raw = String(r.region_raw).trim(); if (!raw) continue; const entry = bySRaw.get(raw) || { rowCount: 0, dollarImpact: 0 }; entry.rowCount += 1; entry.dollarImpact += (dollarField ? (r[dollarField] || 0) : 0); bySRaw.set(raw, entry); } if (bySRaw.size === 0) return [...bySRaw.keys()]; const stmts = []; for (const [raw, entry] of bySRaw) { stmts.push(env.DB.prepare( `INSERT INTO unmapped_region_log (raw_value, source_type, row_count, dollar_impact, last_upload_batch_id) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?) ON CONFLICT(raw_value) DO UPDATE SET row_count = row_count + excluded.row_count, dollar_impact = dollar_impact + excluded.dollar_impact, source_type = excluded.source_type, last_seen_at = strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%fZ','now'), last_upload_batch_id = excluded.last_upload_batch_id` ).bind(raw, sourceType, entry.rowCount, entry.dollarImpact, uploadBatchId)); } await env.DB.batch(stmts); return [...bySRaw.keys()]; } async function getUnmappedRegionsReview(env) { const rows = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT raw_value, source_type, row_count, dollar_impact, first_seen_at, last_seen_at FROM unmapped_region_log ORDER BY dollar_impact DESC` ).all()).results || []; const resolvedRows = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT rrl.raw_value, rrl.region_id, r.name AS region_name, rrl.resolved_at FROM resolved_region_log rrl LEFT JOIN regions r ON r.id = rrl.region_id ORDER BY rrl.resolved_at DESC LIMIT 25` ).all()).results || []; const totalUnmappedDollars = rows.reduce((s, r) => s + (r.dollar_impact || 0), 0); return { unmapped: rows.map(r => ({ rawValue: r.raw_value, sourceType: r.source_type, sourceLabel: UNMAPPED_SOURCE_LABELS[r.source_type] || r.source_type, rowCount: r.row_count, dollarImpact: r.dollar_impact, firstSeenAt: r.first_seen_at, lastSeenAt: r.last_seen_at, })), totalUnmappedDollars, resolved: resolvedRows.map(r => ({ rawValue: r.raw_value, regionId: r.region_id, regionName: r.region_name, resolvedAt: r.resolved_at })), }; } async function resolveUnmappedRegion(env, rawValue, regionId, actorUserId) { rawValue = (rawValue || '').trim(); if (!rawValue) return { error: 'A raw region value is required.', status: 400 }; if (!regionId) return { error: 'A region is required.', status: 400 }; const region = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM regions WHERE id = ?').bind(regionId).first(); if (!region) return { error: 'That region does not exist.', status: 400 }; // Real region_raw_mappings write (2026-09-10): the first one anywhere // in this app — every prior raw_value/region_id row was seeded by hand // via the D1 console. INSERT OR REPLACE so re-mapping an already-mapped // raw value (correcting a mistake) works the same way as mapping a new // one, rather than needing a separate "edit" path. await env.DB.prepare( `INSERT INTO region_raw_mappings (raw_value, region_id, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%fZ','now')) ON CONFLICT(raw_value) DO UPDATE SET region_id = excluded.region_id, updated_at = excluded.updated_at` ).bind(rawValue, regionId).run(); await env.DB.prepare( `INSERT INTO resolved_region_log (id, raw_value, region_id, resolved_by) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)` ).bind(randomToken(16), rawValue, regionId, actorUserId || null).run(); await env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM unmapped_region_log WHERE raw_value = ?').bind(rawValue).run(); return { ok: true }; } // Real Salesforce export column headers, per file type, read directly // from production exports (§24/§28) — never guessed. Each row from the // browser parser is a plain object keyed by these exact header strings // (the parser reads the HTML table's /first-row text verbatim). function mapBookingsRow(row, regionMap) { return { id: randomToken(16), opportunity_name: row['Opportunity Name'] || null, stage: row['Stage'] || null, service_name: row['Service Name'] || null, service_code: row['Service Code'] || null, service_family: row['Service Family'] || null, quantity: parseFloat(row['Quantity']) || null, sales_price: parseFloat(row['Sales Price']) || null, sales_price_converted: parseFloat(row['Sales Price (converted)']) || null, total_price: parseFloat(row['Total Price']) || null, total_vda_fees: parseFloat(row['Total VDA Fees']) || null, opportunity_primary_scope: row['Opportunity Primary Scope'] || null, opportunity_count: parseInt(row['Opportunity Count'], 10) || null, region_raw: row['Region'] || null, region_id: resolveRegionId(regionMap, row['Region']), parent_company: row['Parent Company'] || null, revenue_type: row['Revenue Type'] || null, proposal_issued_by: row['Proposal Issued By:'] || row['Proposal Issued By'] || null, awarded_date: row['Awarded Date'] || null, awarded_date_year: row['Awarded Date for Year'] || null, }; /* ============================================================ EXPLORER — pivot-table-style filtering/grouping over every real upload type, per Misty's request ("something to replicate a pivot table functionality"; extended 2026-09-10, "it should apply to every data upload"). Six datasets: backlog_fact, proposal_pipeline_fact, bookings_fact, proposal_volume_fact, billed_fact, billed_line_item_fact — real column names grounded against each table's own *_COLUMNS constant above before writing any of this, no invented fields. Every dimension/measure here is allowlisted by real column name; groupBy/measure/filters from the request are only ever used as lookup keys into this config, never interpolated into SQL directly, so there's no injection surface from a hand-built query string. ============================================================ */ const EXPLORER_DATASETS = { backlog: { table: 'backlog_fact', label: 'Backlog', dimensions: [ { field: 'parent_customer_name', label: 'Customer' }, { field: 'project_owner', label: 'Project Owner' }, { field: 'service_family', label: 'Service Family' }, { field: 'service', label: 'Service' }, { field: 'service_type', label: 'Service Type' }, { field: 'vertical_end_market', label: 'Vertical / End Market' }, { field: 'revenue_type', label: 'Revenue Type' }, { field: 'region_raw', label: 'Region' }, { field: 'responsibility_name', label: 'Responsibility' }, ], measures: [ { field: 'contract_balance', label: 'Contract Balance ($)', agg: 'sum' }, { field: 'amount_to_be_invoiced', label: 'Amount to be Invoiced ($)', agg: 'sum' }, { field: '*', label: 'Record Count', agg: 'count' }, ], regionField: 'region_id', }, proposal_pipeline: { table: 'proposal_pipeline_fact', label: 'Sales Opportunities', dimensions: [ { field: 'company_name', label: 'Company' }, { field: 'stage', label: 'Stage' }, { field: 'proposal_issued_by', label: 'Proposal Issued By' }, { field: 'proposal_issued_by_manager', label: 'Proposal Issued By Manager' }, { field: 'region_raw', label: 'Region' }, ], measures: [ { field: 'total_vda_fees', label: 'Total VDA Fees ($)', agg: 'sum' }, { field: 'probability_pct', label: 'Avg. Probability (%)', agg: 'avg' }, { field: '*', label: 'Record Count', agg: 'count' }, ], regionField: 'region_id', }, // Added 2026-09-10 per Misty's direct request ("build a pivot... it // should apply to every data upload") — the four datasets below bring // Explorer to full coverage of every real upload type in the app. // Every dimension/measure here is grounded in the real column // constants above (BOOKINGS_COLUMNS/PROPOSAL_VOLUME_COLUMNS/ // BILLED_FACT_COLUMNS/BILLED_LINE_ITEM_COLUMNS), same discipline as // the original two datasets, nothing invented. bookings: { table: 'bookings_fact', label: 'Bookings', dimensions: [ { field: 'parent_company', label: 'Parent Company' }, { field: 'stage', label: 'Stage' }, { field: 'service_family', label: 'Service Family' }, { field: 'service_name', label: 'Service' }, { field: 'revenue_type', label: 'Revenue Type' }, { field: 'region_raw', label: 'Region' }, { field: 'proposal_issued_by', label: 'Proposal Issued By' }, { field: 'opportunity_primary_scope', label: 'Opportunity Primary Scope' }, ], measures: [ { field: 'total_price', label: 'Total Price ($)', agg: 'sum' }, { field: 'total_vda_fees', label: 'Total VDA Fees ($)', agg: 'sum' }, { field: '*', label: 'Record Count', agg: 'count' }, ], regionField: 'region_id', }, proposal_volume: { table: 'proposal_volume_fact', label: 'Proposal Volume', dimensions: [ { field: 'company_name', label: 'Company' }, { field: 'stage', label: 'Stage' }, { field: 'service_name', label: 'Service' }, { field: 'revenue_type', label: 'Revenue Type' }, { field: 'region_raw', label: 'Region' }, { field: 'opportunity_primary_scope', label: 'Opportunity Primary Scope' }, ], measures: [ { field: 'total_price', label: 'Total Price ($)', agg: 'sum' }, { field: 'total_vda_fees', label: 'Total VDA Fees ($)', agg: 'sum' }, { field: '*', label: 'Record Count', agg: 'count' }, ], regionField: 'region_id', }, billed_fact: { table: 'billed_fact', label: 'Billed Fact', dimensions: [ { field: 'entity', label: 'Entity' }, { field: 'region_raw', label: 'Region' }, { field: 'revenue_type', label: 'Revenue Type' }, { field: 'revenue_subtype', label: 'Revenue Subtype' }, { field: 'fiscal_year', label: 'Fiscal Year' }, { field: 'month', label: 'Month' }, ], measures: [ { field: 'amount', label: 'Amount ($)', agg: 'sum' }, { field: '*', label: 'Record Count', agg: 'count' }, ], regionField: 'region_id', }, billed_line_item: { table: 'billed_line_item_fact', label: 'Billed Line Item Fact', dimensions: [ { field: 'parent_customer_name', label: 'Customer' }, { field: 'employee_name', label: 'Employee' }, { field: 'service_family', label: 'Service Family' }, { field: 'service_name', label: 'Service' }, { field: 'revenue_type', label: 'Revenue Type' }, { field: 'region_raw', label: 'Region' }, { field: 'fiscal_year', label: 'Fiscal Year' }, { field: 'month', label: 'Month' }, ], measures: [ { field: 'amount', label: 'Amount ($)', agg: 'sum' }, { field: '*', label: 'Record Count', agg: 'count' }, ], regionField: 'region_id', }, }; function getExplorerFieldConfig(dataset) { const cfg = EXPLORER_DATASETS[dataset]; if (!cfg) return { error: `Unknown dataset "${dataset}". Valid values: ${Object.keys(EXPLORER_DATASETS).join(', ')}.`, status: 400 }; return { dataset, label: cfg.label, table: cfg.table, dimensions: cfg.dimensions, measures: cfg.measures.map(m => ({ field: m.field, label: m.label })), }; } async function getExplorerPivot(env, { dataset, groupBy, measure, regionId, filters }) { const cfg = EXPLORER_DATASETS[dataset]; if (!cfg) return { error: `Unknown dataset "${dataset}". Valid values: ${Object.keys(EXPLORER_DATASETS).join(', ')}.`, status: 400 }; const dim = cfg.dimensions.find(d => d.field === groupBy); if (!dim) return { error: `Unknown groupBy field "${groupBy}" for dataset "${dataset}".`, status: 400 }; const meas = cfg.measures.find(m => m.field === measure); if (!meas) return { error: `Unknown measure "${measure}" for dataset "${dataset}".`, status: 400 }; const where = []; const binds = []; if (regionId) { where.push(`${cfg.regionField} = ?`); binds.push(regionId); } // Filters are validated against the same dimension allowlist as groupBy // (never an arbitrary column name), and each value is bound, not // interpolated. for (const [field, value] of Object.entries(filters || {})) { const filterDim = cfg.dimensions.find(d => d.field === field); if (!filterDim) return { error: `Unknown filter field "${field}" for dataset "${dataset}".`, status: 400 }; if (value === null || value === undefined || value === '') continue; where.push(`${field} = ?`); binds.push(value); } const whereSql = where.length ? `WHERE ${where.join(' AND ')}` : ''; const aggExpr = meas.agg === 'count' ? 'COUNT(*)' : `${meas.agg.toUpperCase()}(${meas.field})`; const sql = `SELECT ${groupBy} AS dim_value, ${aggExpr} AS agg_value, COUNT(*) AS row_count FROM ${cfg.table} ${whereSql} GROUP BY ${groupBy} ORDER BY agg_value DESC`; const res = await env.DB.prepare(sql).bind(...binds).all(); const rows = (res.results || []).map(r => ({ dimValue: r.dim_value === null || r.dim_value === '' ? '(blank)' : r.dim_value, value: r.agg_value === null ? 0 : r.agg_value, rowCount: r.row_count, })); const totalSql = `SELECT ${aggExpr} AS agg_value, COUNT(*) AS row_count FROM ${cfg.table} ${whereSql}`; const totalRes = await env.DB.prepare(totalSql).bind(...binds).first(); const grandTotal = { value: totalRes && totalRes.agg_value !== null ? totalRes.agg_value : 0, rowCount: totalRes ? totalRes.row_count : 0, }; return { dataset, label: cfg.label, groupBy: { field: groupBy, label: dim.label }, measure: { field: measure, label: meas.label, agg: meas.agg }, rows, grandTotal, }; } // billed_fact (2026-09-04): the real Billings/Revenue actuals source the // app had been waiting on (§48/§51's "no real billed_fact source yet"). // Deliberately NOT part of UPLOAD_TYPE_CONFIG/saveUploadRows above: this // source is uploaded one fiscal year at a time (she re-uploads the same // underlying "Details" export scoped to a single year), so each upload // REPLACES just that fiscal year's existing billed_fact rows, leaving // other fiscal years' rows untouched. See replaceBilledFactForYear(). // The four UPLOAD_TYPE_CONFIG types above (bookings/backlog/ // proposal_pipeline/proposal_volume) are each a full current-state // extract instead, so saveUploadRows replaces the WHOLE table on each // of those uploads (2026-09-08 fix — was pure append-only until Misty // caught two Bookings uploads doubling every total, see saveUploadRows's // own comment for the full story). // // Source columns confirmed directly from Details_1.xlsx (real export, // 3,947 real data rows read + 2 footer/total rows stripped): Period, // Entity, Ledger Name, Region, Profit Center, Revenue Type, Revenue // Subtype, Budget, Billings / Revenue, WIP, Queue, Adj. Billings, // Variance $, Variance %. Per Misty's explicit call, ONLY Adj. Billings // (the "amount" below) is stored — Budget/Billings-Revenue/WIP/Queue/ // Variance are not tracked, she confirmed Adj. Billings is the only // value column that matters. Ledger Name and Profit Center are also not // stored (finer-grained than anything the app models; Entity is kept // instead per her direct call to retain it even though nothing // downstream uses it yet). // // Period is real 'YYYY-MM' text (e.g. '2026-08'), parsed here into // fiscal_year/month integers for real querying rather than string // matching. Region resolves through the same region_raw_mappings table // every other upload uses (confirmed real values: Metro, Midwest, // Northeast, Northwest, South, West, Corporate, Key Accounts all match // existing raw mappings already seeded; D2E/Gunn appear as both an // Entity AND a Region value in this source, only the Region column // value is used for region_id resolution, matching Sales Budgets' own // d2e=uk/Gunn=canada mapping). function parsePeriod(periodStr) { const m = /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})$/.exec(String(periodStr || '').trim()); if (!m) return null; const year = parseInt(m[1], 10); const month = parseInt(m[2], 10); if (!isValidFiscalYear(year) || !isValidMonth(month)) return null; return { year, month }; } function mapBilledFactRow(row, regionMap) { const parsed = parsePeriod(row['Period']); if (!parsed) return null; // strips the 'No filters applied'/'Total'/blank footer rows const amount = parseFloat(row['Adj. Billings']); return { id: randomToken(16), period: row['Period'], fiscal_year: parsed.year, month: parsed.month, entity: row['Entity'] || null, region_raw: row['Region'] || null, region_id: resolveRegionId(regionMap, row['Region']), revenue_type: row['Revenue Type'] || null, revenue_subtype: row['Revenue Subtype'] || null, amount: Number.isFinite(amount) ? amount : 0, }; } const BILLED_FACT_COLUMNS = ['id','upload_batch_id','period','fiscal_year','month','entity', 'region_raw','region_id','revenue_type','revenue_subtype','amount']; // Replaces (not appends) all billed_fact rows for every fiscal year // actually present in the uploaded file with a freshly-uploaded set. // // 2026-09-10 FIX: this used to delete only WHERE fiscal_year = the ONE // year selected in the upload picker, then insert every row in the file // regardless of that row's own year — correct only if the file itself // were pre-filtered to that single year, which was the original // (2026-09-04) design assumption. The real Details_1.xlsx export Misty // re-uploads has always contained all 3 real years (2024-2026) in one // file, confirmed directly with her: she's never pre-filtered it before // uploading. So every re-upload selecting, say, "2026" correctly // replaced 2026's rows but ALSO re-inserted a full duplicate copy of // every 2024 and 2025 row on top of whatever was already there, // compounding further on each subsequent upload. Misty caught this // directly: 2024 showing $241.26M and 2025 showing $263.09M on the // Company-Wide Revenue Trend card, both far past real company scale // (company has never billed $240M in a year; FY2026 growth budget is // ~$137M), while 2026 YTD looked comparatively normal since it was the // one year actually being correctly replaced each time. // // Fix: delete every DISTINCT fiscal_year actually found in the parsed // rows (not just the fiscalYear parameter), so the replace always // matches what the file actually contains — correct whether the file // covers one year or several, and idempotent no matter how many times // the same multi-year file gets re-uploaded. The fiscalYear parameter // is still required and validated (kept for API-contract stability and // because upload_batches' own bookkeeping expects one), but no longer // used to scope the delete. async function replaceBilledFactForYear(env, fiscalYear, uploadBatchId, rows, actorUserId) { if (!isValidFiscalYear(fiscalYear)) return { error: 'Invalid fiscal year.', status: 400 }; if (!Array.isArray(rows) || rows.length === 0) return { error: 'No rows provided.', status: 400 }; const regionMap = await loadRegionMap(env); const mapped = rows.map(r => mapBilledFactRow(r, regionMap)).filter(Boolean); if (mapped.length === 0) { return { error: 'No usable rows found (all rows failed to parse a valid Period).', status: 400 }; } const placeholders = '(' + BILLED_FACT_COLUMNS.map(() => '?').join(',') + ')'; const insertSql = `INSERT INTO billed_fact (${BILLED_FACT_COLUMNS.join(',')}) VALUES ${placeholders}`; // Delete every distinct fiscal year actually present in this upload's // rows, not just the picker's selected fiscalYear — see the fix // comment above. All delete statements batch with the first chunk of // inserts so the whole replace happens in one D1 batch() call, real // replace semantics rather than a separate unprotected delete step. const yearsPresent = [...new Set(mapped.map(r => r.fiscal_year))]; const deleteStmts = yearsPresent.map(yr => env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM billed_fact WHERE fiscal_year = ?').bind(yr)); let inserted = 0; for (let i = 0; i < mapped.length; i += BATCH_INSERT_SIZE) { const slice = mapped.slice(i, i + BATCH_INSERT_SIZE).map(row => ({ upload_batch_id: uploadBatchId, ...row })); const stmts = slice.map(row => env.DB.prepare(insertSql).bind(...BILLED_FACT_COLUMNS.map(c => row[c] ?? null))); if (i === 0) { await env.DB.batch([...deleteStmts, ...stmts]); } else { await env.DB.batch(stmts); } inserted += slice.length; } await env.DB.prepare('UPDATE upload_batches SET row_count = ? WHERE id = ?').bind(inserted, uploadBatchId).run(); // Persisted to unmapped_region_log (2026-09-10), not just returned in // this response — see logUnmappedRegions' own comment. const unmapped = await logUnmappedRegions(env, 'billed_fact', mapped, uploadBatchId); // yearsReplaced (2026-09-10 fix): the real, complete list of years this // upload actually replaced, so the frontend can say so accurately // instead of only ever naming the picker's single selected year. return { ok: true, rowsInserted: inserted, unmappedRegions: unmapped, yearsReplaced: yearsPresent.sort((a, b) => a - b) }; } // billed_line_item_fact (2026-09-10): a second, complementary real // billing source, per Misty's direct correction — Details_1.xlsx (the // existing billed_fact table, Power BI, period/region/revenue-type // rollup grain, real history back to 2024) stays exactly as-is, // untouched. This is a genuinely different export: a real Salesforce // billing-line pull, 2026-forward only, at line-item grain with real // per-employee attribution (§99's "Sales & Billing Leaderboard" idea — // the billing side had no individual field anywhere in PULSE until this // export). Confirmed real column list, verbatim from the source header // row: Billing Line Name, Total Currency, Total, Project: Project Name, // Project Task: Project Task Name, Product: Service Family, Product: // Service Name, Profit Center: Region, Invoice: Parent Customer, // Employee: Full Name, Product: Revenue Type, Invoice: Period Year, // Invoice: Period Start Date. Same real "HTML table saved as .xls" file // shape as Bookings/Backlog/Proposal Pipeline (confirmed directly by // Misty), so this reuses parseSalesforceHtmlExport on the frontend, not // billed_fact's SheetJS/binary-.xlsx path. // // Per Misty's direct calls: Total is the one real dollar amount stored // (Total Currency dropped, same USD-only pattern every other source // follows); Profit Center: Region resolves through the existing // region_raw_mappings table like every other upload; Product: Revenue // Type maps through the existing REVENUE_TYPE_TO_LOB lookup at query // time (mapRevenueTypeToLob), not pre-computed into a stored lob column, // matching bookings_fact/backlog_fact/billed_fact's own convention; // Invoice: Period Start Date is a real parseable date, used to derive // fiscal_year/month for real querying, the same role billed_fact's // parsePeriod() plays against its own 'Period' column (kept as its own // parser below, parseLineItemPeriodStartDate, since the real date format // on this export is a full date, not billed_fact's 'YYYY-MM' string). // Invoice: Period Year is also kept as its own raw column for reference/ // audit, same reasoning as billed_fact keeping Entity even though // nothing reads it yet. // // Replace-per-fiscal-year on upload (not pure append), same real // reasoning and same shape as replaceBilledFactForYear: Misty re-pulls // this export scoped to one fiscal year at a time, so each upload // replaces just that year's existing rows rather than doubling them. function parseLineItemPeriodStartDate(dateStr) { const raw = String(dateStr || '').trim(); if (!raw) return null; // Real Salesforce date exports have shown up as M/D/YYYY elsewhere in // this project (estimated_award_date, awarded_date) — try that first, // matching the exact parsing already proven against real pipeline/ // bookings dates, then fall back to a plain Date parse (covers a real // ISO-style 'YYYY-MM-DD' export instead, without guessing which one // this source uses ahead of a real file to test against). const slashParts = raw.split('/'); if (slashParts.length === 3) { const m = parseInt(slashParts[0], 10), d = parseInt(slashParts[1], 10), y = parseInt(slashParts[2], 10); if (m && d && y && isValidFiscalYear(y) && isValidMonth(m)) return { year: y, month: m }; } const parsed = new Date(raw); if (!isNaN(parsed.getTime())) { const y = parsed.getUTCFullYear(), m = parsed.getUTCMonth() + 1; if (isValidFiscalYear(y) && isValidMonth(m)) return { year: y, month: m }; } return null; } function mapBilledLineItemRow(row, regionMap) { const parsed = parseLineItemPeriodStartDate(row['Invoice: Period Start Date']); if (!parsed) return null; // strips unparseable/footer/blank rows, same defensive pattern as mapBilledFactRow const amount = parseFloat(row['Total']); return { id: randomToken(16), billing_line_name: row['Billing Line Name'] || null, project_name: row['Project: Project Name'] || null, project_task_name: row['Project Task: Project Task Name'] || null, service_family: row['Product: Service Family'] || null, service_name: row['Product: Service Name'] || null, region_raw: row['Profit Center: Region'] || null, region_id: resolveRegionId(regionMap, row['Profit Center: Region']), parent_customer_name: row['Invoice: Parent Customer'] || null, employee_name: row['Employee: Full Name'] || null, revenue_type: row['Product: Revenue Type'] || null, period_year: row['Invoice: Period Year'] || null, period_start_date: row['Invoice: Period Start Date'] || null, fiscal_year: parsed.year, month: parsed.month, amount: Number.isFinite(amount) ? amount : 0, }; } const BILLED_LINE_ITEM_COLUMNS = ['id','upload_batch_id','billing_line_name','project_name','project_task_name', 'service_family','service_name','region_raw','region_id','parent_customer_name','employee_name','revenue_type', 'period_year','period_start_date','fiscal_year','month','amount']; // Replaces (not appends) all billed_line_item_fact rows for one fiscal // year with a freshly-uploaded set — identical shape to // replaceBilledFactForYear above (delete then insert in one batch, so a // failure partway through can't leave the year half-deleted). // Chunked, not single-request, unlike replaceBilledFactForYear above // (2026-09-10, confirmed with Misty: this real export runs ~144,000 rows // for one fiscal year, far past both MAX_ROWS_PER_REQUEST and what a // single Worker invocation should try to batch-insert in one shot — // closer to Bookings/Backlog's scale than billed_fact's ~3,950). So this // follows saveUploadRows' real chunking shape (the browser sends // sequential chunks, awaiting each one, against one upload_batch) // instead, with one real difference: the delete is scoped to fiscalYear, // not the whole table, since other fiscal years' rows must survive a // re-upload (matching replaceBilledFactForYear's year-scoping, not // saveUploadRows' whole-table scoping). "First chunk" is detected the // same reliable way saveUploadRows already does: upload_batches.row_count // is still 0 (set at creation, only ever incremented after a chunk's // insert here), and the frontend never sends chunks in parallel. async function saveBilledLineItemRows(env, uploadBatchId, fiscalYear, rows) { if (!isValidFiscalYear(fiscalYear)) return { error: 'A valid fiscalYear is required.', status: 400 }; if (!Array.isArray(rows) || rows.length === 0) return { error: 'No rows provided.', status: 400 }; if (rows.length > MAX_ROWS_PER_REQUEST) { return { error: `Too many rows in one request (max ${MAX_ROWS_PER_REQUEST}). Send in smaller chunks.`, status: 400 }; } const batch = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id, row_count FROM upload_batches WHERE id = ?').bind(uploadBatchId).first(); if (!batch) return { error: 'Upload batch not found.', status: 404 }; const isFirstChunk = (batch.row_count || 0) === 0; const regionMap = await loadRegionMap(env); const mapped = rows.map(r => mapBilledLineItemRow(r, regionMap)).filter(Boolean); if (mapped.length === 0 && rows.length > 0 && isFirstChunk) { // Only treated as a hard error on the first chunk — a later chunk // legitimately hitting zero parseable rows (e.g. a trailing all- // footer chunk) shouldn't fail an otherwise-successful upload. return { error: 'No usable rows found in this chunk (all rows failed to parse a valid Invoice: Period Start Date).', status: 400 }; } const placeholders = '(' + BILLED_LINE_ITEM_COLUMNS.map(() => '?').join(',') + ')'; const insertSql = `INSERT INTO billed_line_item_fact (${BILLED_LINE_ITEM_COLUMNS.join(',')}) VALUES ${placeholders}`; // 2026-09-10 FIX (same root cause as replaceBilledFactForYear, see its // own fix comment for the full story): deleting only WHERE fiscal_year // = the fiscalYear parameter is only correct if every row in the // upload actually belongs to that one year. This table is documented // as 2026-forward only today, but nothing enforces that at parse time, // and a future export spanning more than one year would silently // duplicate every other year's rows exactly like billed_fact did. // // Fix must also work correctly across CHUNKS (this upload arrives as // multiple separate HTTP requests, each a fresh Worker invocation with // no shared state), so a plain "delete each year once, on the first // chunk only" isn't safe — a later chunk can contain a year the first // chunk didn't. Every chunk instead deletes WHERE fiscal_year = (a // year present in THIS chunk) AND upload_batch_id != (this batch), // for every distinct year in this chunk's own rows. That's safe to run // on every chunk regardless of order: it always clears out any OTHER // batch's stale rows for a year this chunk touches, but can never // delete rows this same batch already inserted in an earlier chunk // (excluded by the upload_batch_id != check), so nothing this upload // itself just wrote is ever at risk of being deleted by its own later // chunk. const deleteStmtsForChunk = (chunkRows) => [...new Set(chunkRows.map(r => r.fiscal_year))] .map(yr => env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM billed_line_item_fact WHERE fiscal_year = ? AND upload_batch_id != ?').bind(yr, uploadBatchId)); let inserted = 0; for (let i = 0; i < mapped.length; i += BATCH_INSERT_SIZE) { const slice = mapped.slice(i, i + BATCH_INSERT_SIZE).map(row => ({ upload_batch_id: uploadBatchId, ...row })); const stmts = slice.map(row => env.DB.prepare(insertSql).bind(...BILLED_LINE_ITEM_COLUMNS.map(c => row[c] ?? null))); // Delete-then-insert in the same D1 batch() call on EVERY slice (not // just the first chunk), so a year first encountered in a later // chunk still gets its stale other-batch rows cleared exactly once, // safely, before this chunk's own rows land. await env.DB.batch([...deleteStmtsForChunk(slice), ...stmts]); inserted += slice.length; } await env.DB.prepare('UPDATE upload_batches SET row_count = row_count + ? WHERE id = ?').bind(inserted, uploadBatchId).run(); // Persisted to unmapped_region_log (2026-09-10), not just returned in // this response — see logUnmappedRegions' own comment. const unmapped = await logUnmappedRegions(env, 'billed_line_item', mapped, uploadBatchId); return { ok: true, inserted, unmappedRegions: unmapped }; } // Real Adj. Billings total for a fiscal year, grouped by month and line // of business — the query Forecast's Business Lines/Month Pace tabs read // once real billed_fact data exists, replacing the "revenueAvailable: // false" placeholder. async function loadBilledFactMonthlyByLob(env, fiscalYear) { const rows = await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT month, revenue_type, SUM(amount) AS total FROM billed_fact WHERE fiscal_year = ? GROUP BY month, revenue_type` ).bind(fiscalYear).all(); const results = rows.results || []; const byMonth = new Map(); // month -> { program_advisory, design_services, inspections, unmapped } let unmappedTotal = 0; for (const r of results) { const lob = mapRevenueTypeToLob(r.revenue_type); if (!byMonth.has(r.month)) byMonth.set(r.month, { program_advisory: 0, design_services: 0, inspections: 0 }); if (lob) { byMonth.get(r.month)[lob] += r.total; } else { unmappedTotal += r.total; } } return { byMonth, unmappedTotal }; } // ---- Forecast (§47-§51): Business Lines / Regional Drivers / Month Pace -- // // Real math, verified against Gavin's own workbook to the exact cent // (§48): Total Year Forecast = Jan..(currentMonth-1) actuals (sum) // + currentMonth's blended forecast (currentMonth actuals-to-date + // avg daily rate for currentMonth × currentMonth's remaining business // days) // + (Jan..(currentMonth-1) avg/month × remaining months after currentMonth) // "Current month" and the trailing window are derived from real data // (MAX(awarded_date) in bookings_fact), never hardcoded to a calendar // month — this is what makes the Jan-Jul/Jan-Aug/Jan-Sep window advance // automatically as new uploads land (§50, confirmed no manual "close the // month" step, no refresh button — every read is live against current // D1 rows). // // Dollar basis for Sales = bookings_fact.total_price, keyed by // awarded_date (§48/§50 confirmed real figures). Dollar basis for // Revenue = billed_fact.amount (Adj. Billings), keyed by fiscal_year/ // month, real as of 2026-09-04 once a fiscal year has an upload — both // Business Lines and Regional Drivers gate their Revenue output on real // data existing for the selected fiscal year (`revenueAvailable`/ // `revenueActualsAvailable`) rather than ever faking or estimating it. // // Real date format confirmed directly from the source data (§51 // verification): awarded_date is stored as plain TEXT 'M/D/YYYY', no // leading zeros (e.g. '9/2/2026', '5/7/2026') — NOT ISO, not lexically // sortable. Year is always the last 4 characters; month is always the // token before the first '/'. These two SQL fragments are the verified, // safe way to extract each from that exact format: // year: SUBSTR(awarded_date, -4) // month: CAST(SUBSTR(awarded_date, 1, INSTR(awarded_date, '/') - 1) AS INTEGER) const AWARDED_YEAR_SQL = `CAST(SUBSTR(awarded_date, -4) AS INTEGER)`; const AWARDED_MONTH_SQL = `CAST(SUBSTR(awarded_date, 1, INSTR(awarded_date, '/') - 1) AS INTEGER)`; // backlog_fact's completion date column is 'end_date', same real SFDC // M/D/YYYY export format (same mechanism, different column name). const END_YEAR_SQL = `CAST(SUBSTR(end_date, -4) AS INTEGER)`; const END_MONTH_SQL = `CAST(SUBSTR(end_date, 1, INSTR(end_date, '/') - 1) AS INTEGER)`; // proposal_volume_fact.proposal_issued_date: confirmed real format via a // live D1 query (2026-09-06), same M/D/YYYY text, no leading zeros, as // awarded_date/end_date (e.g. '8/3/2026', '8/17/2026', '7/17/2026') — not // a coincidence, same Salesforce export family, but verified directly // rather than assumed, per standing rule 4. Same extraction mechanism. const PROPOSAL_ISSUED_YEAR_SQL = `CAST(SUBSTR(proposal_issued_date, -4) AS INTEGER)`; const PROPOSAL_ISSUED_MONTH_SQL = `CAST(SUBSTR(proposal_issued_date, 1, INSTR(proposal_issued_date, '/') - 1) AS INTEGER)`; // year*12+month as a single sortable integer, for a trailing-N-months // window comparison without needing day-level precision (day-of-month // doesn't materially change a 12-month window and isn't needed elsewhere // in this format the way it is for business-day math in resolveForecastAsOf). const PROPOSAL_ISSUED_YM_SQL = `(${PROPOSAL_ISSUED_YEAR_SQL} * 12 + ${PROPOSAL_ISSUED_MONTH_SQL})`; // estimated_award_date, proposal_pipeline_fact — verified live (2026-09-06, // Misty ran the query directly) before writing this, same M/D/YYYY text // format as proposal_issued_date/awarded_date/end_date (e.g. '9/14/2026', // '2/27/2020', '1/11/2027' — real sample values included a stale 2020 row // and a next-fiscal-year 2027 row, exactly the kind of row Step 5 of the // Revenue Bridge needs to exclude). Same extraction mechanism as // PROPOSAL_ISSUED_YEAR_SQL/MONTH_SQL above. const ESTIMATED_AWARD_YEAR_SQL = `CAST(SUBSTR(estimated_award_date, -4) AS INTEGER)`; // Raw Salesforce revenue_type label -> canonical line-of-business key. // Confirmed rename (continuity brief §3 item 1): Existing Installations // -> Program Advisory, New Design -> Design Services. Inspections keeps // its name. Anything unrecognized maps to null and is excluded from // LOB-grouped totals (surfaced separately as "Unmapped" so a bad/blank // source value is visible rather than silently dropped into a bucket). const REVENUE_TYPE_TO_LOB = { 'Existing Installations': 'program_advisory', 'New Design': 'design_services', 'Inspections': 'inspections', // 'Design Services' added 2026-09-04 for the real billed_fact source // (Details_1.xlsx, confirmed with Misty): that export's own Revenue // Type column already uses the renamed label directly, rather than the // raw Salesforce 'New Design' value bookings_fact/backlog_fact use. // Both keys map to the same LOB, this is a second source system's own // spelling, not a conflicting one. 'Design Services': 'design_services', }; const LOB_LABELS = { program_advisory: 'Program Advisory', design_services: 'Design Services', inspections: 'Inspections', }; function mapRevenueTypeToLob(revenueType) { return REVENUE_TYPE_TO_LOB[revenueType] || null; } const REVENUE_LOB_KEYS = ['program_advisory', 'design_services', 'inspections']; // Shared by region-level and company-wide Revenue Trend (2026-09-10, per // Misty's direct request: "show on the regions and company wide trends // and averages for almost 3 years"). Takes a Map already summed for // exactly [priorYear-1, priorYear, fiscalYear], and years in that same // order, and returns each year's totals plus two real comparisons: YoY // (year-over-year, unchanged from before) and vsHistoricalAvg, this // year's total against the trailing 2-year average of the two years // immediately before it. Confirmed with Misty: trailing 2-year, not a // growing all-history average, so the baseline always reflects the two // most recent complete years rather than getting diluted as more years // of history pile up. Only fiscalYear (the last entry) gets a real // vsHistoricalAvg value, since it's the only year with two full prior // years available in this 3-year window; priorYear-1/priorYear both get // null (not enough trailing history yet, same "explicit gap, don't fake // it" convention as every other pace/forecast figure in this app). function computeRevenueTrendWithTrailingAvg(revenueByYear, revenueYears) { return revenueYears.map((yr, i) => { const bucket = revenueByYear.get(yr); const prevBucket = i > 0 ? revenueByYear.get(revenueYears[i - 1]) : null; const yoyPct = prevBucket && prevBucket.total > 0 ? ((bucket.total - prevBucket.total) / prevBucket.total) * 100 : null; const byLobYoy = {}; for (const lob of REVENUE_LOB_KEYS) { const prev = prevBucket ? prevBucket[lob] : null; byLobYoy[lob] = prev && prev > 0 ? ((bucket[lob] - prev) / prev) * 100 : null; } // Trailing 2-year historical average: needs both of the two years // immediately before this one present in the window, i.e. only the // last (most recent) year in a 3-year [priorYear-1, priorYear, // fiscalYear] window qualifies. let historicalAvg = null, vsHistoricalAvgPct = null, vsHistoricalAvgDollar = null; if (i >= 2) { const y1 = revenueByYear.get(revenueYears[i - 2]); const y2 = revenueByYear.get(revenueYears[i - 1]); if (y1 && y2) { historicalAvg = (y1.total + y2.total) / 2; vsHistoricalAvgDollar = bucket.total - historicalAvg; vsHistoricalAvgPct = historicalAvg > 0 ? (vsHistoricalAvgDollar / historicalAvg) * 100 : null; } } return { fiscalYear: yr, total: bucket.total, yoyPct, byLob: { program_advisory: bucket.program_advisory, design_services: bucket.design_services, inspections: bucket.inspections }, byLobYoy, historicalAvg, vsHistoricalAvgPct, vsHistoricalAvgDollar, }; }); } // ---- Regions "Biggest Accounts" service-family breakdown (2026-09-05) -- // // Local to the Regions screen's Biggest Accounts card only, confirmed // with Misty against real service_family/service_name data. VERIFY and // General Consulting are both recurring-revenue lines that live inside // the raw "Maintenance Management" family, carved out here so they get // their own dot instead of being buried (and so MM's own bucket doesn't // double-count them). CCM is already its own real service_family value, // kept standalone rather than merged into Inspections. "Maintenance // Management" (9th bucket) is genuinely everything else: whatever's left // of the raw MM family once VERIFY/General Consulting are pulled out, // plus Expediting Services (27 rows, real but too small for its own // bucket) folded in, this is why it carries a constituent-service list // for the frontend tooltip rather than being a single clean source value. const ACCOUNT_SERVICE_FAMILIES = [ { key: 'modernization', label: 'Modernization' }, { key: 'equipment_evaluations', label: 'Equipment Evaluations' }, { key: 'verify', label: 'VERIFY' }, { key: 'general_consulting', label: 'General Consulting' }, { key: 'inspections', label: 'Inspections' }, { key: 'ccm', label: 'CCM (Code Compliance)' }, { key: 'design_services', label: 'Design Services' }, { key: 'construction', label: 'Construction Site Visits' }, { key: 'maintenance_management', label: 'Maintenance Management' }, ]; const ACCOUNT_FAMILY_LABELS = Object.fromEntries(ACCOUNT_SERVICE_FAMILIES.map(f => [f.key, f.label])); // service_name values carved out of the raw "Maintenance Management" // family into their own buckets (matched case-insensitively, real data // has both "VERIFY" and "Verify"). Everything else under Maintenance // Management falls through to the maintenance_management bucket. const VERIFY_SERVICE_NAMES = new Set(['verify']); const GENERAL_CONSULTING_SERVICE_NAMES = new Set(['general retainor services', 'desktop services', 'monthly retainer']); function mapServiceToAccountFamily(serviceFamily, serviceName) { const family = (serviceFamily || '').trim(); const name = (serviceName || '').trim().toLowerCase(); if (family === 'Modernization Services') return 'modernization'; if (family === 'Equipment Evaluation') return 'equipment_evaluations'; if (family === 'Inspection Services') return 'inspections'; if (family === 'CCM') return 'ccm'; if (family === 'Design Services') return 'design_services'; if (family === 'Construction Services') return 'construction'; if (family === 'Maintenance Management') { if (VERIFY_SERVICE_NAMES.has(name)) return 'verify'; if (GENERAL_CONSULTING_SERVICE_NAMES.has(name)) return 'general_consulting'; return 'maintenance_management'; } if (family === 'Expediting Services') return 'maintenance_management'; return null; // unrecognized family, excluded rather than silently bucketed } // ---- Pipeline & Backlog: Service Mix + Regional Density (2026-09-07) -- // // proposal_volume_fact has no service_family column (unlike bookings_fact // and backlog_fact), only service_name, so it needs its own name-level // map into the same 9 ACCOUNT_SERVICE_FAMILIES buckets used elsewhere. // Built directly with Misty against real proposal_volume_fact.service_name // values (116 distinct) and cross-checked against bookings_fact's real // service_family/service_name pairs where the same name exists there too // -- several names appear under 2-3 different families in bookings_fact // depending on how a given deal was booked (e.g. "Bid Analysis" shows up // under Design Services, Maintenance Management, AND Modernization // Services), so this is NOT a mechanical lookup against bookings_fact, // it's Misty's confirmed call on what each line item really represents. // Her general rule for the remaining ambiguous names: unless the name // itself specifies construction, it's Maintenance Management. const PROPOSAL_SERVICE_NAME_TO_FAMILY = { // modernization 'CAPX Linear Budgeting for Upgrades & Modernization': 'modernization', 'Misc. MOD Services': 'modernization', 'Modernization Design Expenses': 'modernization', 'Modernization Evaluation': 'modernization', 'Modernization Specification': 'modernization', 'Modernization Technical Specification': 'modernization', 'Repair/Upgrade Technical Specification': 'modernization', 'Fixtures/Finishes Meeting': 'modernization', 'Progress Meetings': 'modernization', 'Project Management Support': 'modernization', // equipment_evaluations 'Basic Evaluation': 'equipment_evaluations', 'Comprehensive Evaluation': 'equipment_evaluations', 'Equipment Damage Assessment': 'equipment_evaluations', 'Equipment Evaluation Expenses': 'equipment_evaluations', 'Equipment Feasibility Study': 'equipment_evaluations', 'Misc. EE Services': 'equipment_evaluations', 'QCE': 'equipment_evaluations', 'Quality Control Evaluation': 'equipment_evaluations', 'Standard Evaluation': 'equipment_evaluations', 'Due Diligence': 'equipment_evaluations', 'On-Site Deficiency Tracking': 'equipment_evaluations', 'Remote Deficiency Tracking': 'equipment_evaluations', // verify 'VERIFY': 'verify', 'Verify': 'verify', // general_consulting 'Desktop Services': 'general_consulting', 'General Retainor Services': 'general_consulting', 'Monthly Retainer': 'general_consulting', // inspections 'AIC Inspection': 'inspections', 'AIC Re-Inspection': 'inspections', 'Acceptance Inspection': 'inspections', 'Alteration Inspection': 'inspections', 'Final Acceptance Test': 'inspections', 'Fire Test': 'inspections', 'Generator Test': 'inspections', 'Jurisdictional Inspection - Non-Witnessed': 'inspections', 'Jurisdictional Inspection - Witnessed': 'inspections', 'Mandated Periodic Testing': 'inspections', 'Miscellaneous Hourly Inspection': 'inspections', 'Miscellaneous Inspection Expenses': 'inspections', 'Municipality Inspection': 'inspections', 'Periodic Inspection': 'inspections', 'Periodic/Routine Inspection': 'inspections', 'Re-inspection': 'inspections', 'Semi-Annual Periodic Inspection': 'inspections', 'CAT1': 'inspections', 'CAT3': 'inspections', 'CAT5': 'inspections', 'Accident Investigation': 'inspections', 'QEI': 'inspections', 'AOC Tracking': 'inspections', // ccm 'AIC Certificate Fee': 'ccm', 'AIC Certificate Print Charge': 'ccm', 'CCM Processing Fee': 'ccm', 'Certificate Compliance Management (CCM)-?Inspections?': 'ccm', 'Certificate Fee': 'ccm', 'Certificate Renewal/Filing Fee': 'ccm', 'ECB OATH Violations': 'ccm', 'Local Certificate Compliance Management': 'ccm', 'National Certificate Compliance Management': 'ccm', 'PVT Violations': 'ccm', 'Property Profile with Violation Clearing': 'ccm', 'Violation Tracking': 'ccm', // design_services 'Concept Design': 'design_services', 'Construction Documents': 'design_services', 'Construction Documents/Specification': 'design_services', 'DD Level VDA Drawing Set': 'design_services', 'DS Expenses': 'design_services', 'Design Closeout Services': 'design_services', 'Design Development': 'design_services', 'Design Services Progress Meetings': 'design_services', 'Design and Finish Agenda': 'design_services', 'Misc. DS': 'design_services', 'Schematic Design': 'design_services', 'Engineering Survey': 'design_services', 'Traffic Analysis': 'design_services', 'Traffic Analysis Calculations': 'design_services', 'Traffic Analysis Report': 'design_services', 'Outline Specifications': 'design_services', 'Power Data/Work by Others Memo': 'design_services', 'Submittal Review': 'design_services', // construction 'Construction Services Expenses': 'construction', 'Construction Site Visits': 'construction', 'Misc Construction Services': 'construction', 'Project Kick Off-New Construction': 'construction', 'Construction Phase Coordination': 'construction', // maintenance_management 'Door Lock Monitoring': 'maintenance_management', 'Full Maintenance Agreement (FMA)': 'maintenance_management', 'Maintenance Management Expenses': 'maintenance_management', 'Misc. MM Services': 'maintenance_management', 'Water Damage Services': 'maintenance_management', 'Project Kick Off Meeting': 'maintenance_management', 'Project Kick Off-Modernization': 'maintenance_management', 'Modernization Closeout Services': 'maintenance_management', 'Modernization Progress Meetings': 'maintenance_management', 'Modernization Site Visits': 'maintenance_management', 'Comprehensive Warranty Equipment Evaluation': 'maintenance_management', 'Design Services Site Visit': 'maintenance_management', 'Technical Specification Compliance': 'maintenance_management', 'Contract Review': 'maintenance_management', 'Contract Negotiation': 'maintenance_management', 'Peer Review': 'maintenance_management', 'Bid Analysis': 'maintenance_management', 'Bidding': 'maintenance_management', 'Bid Meetings': 'maintenance_management', 'Bid Phase': 'maintenance_management', 'Bid Walk Through': 'maintenance_management', 'Bid-Walkthrough': 'maintenance_management', 'RFP Issuance': 'maintenance_management', 'Retainage': 'maintenance_management', '3rd Party Contract Review/Peer Review': 'maintenance_management', 'Forensics-Legal/Expert Witness': 'maintenance_management', 'Maintenance Verification': 'maintenance_management', 'Monthly Fee': 'maintenance_management', 'Permits and Amendments': 'maintenance_management', 'Deficiency Tracking & Follow Up Visit': 'maintenance_management', 'RFP Fulfillment': 'maintenance_management', }; function mapProposalServiceNameToFamily(serviceName) { return PROPOSAL_SERVICE_NAME_TO_FAMILY[(serviceName || '').trim()] || null; } // "Open" proposal volume, matching how "open pipeline" is already // treated elsewhere (proposal_pipeline_fact's Weighted Pipeline // implicitly excludes closed stages) -- real proposal_volume_fact.stage // distinct values confirmed with Misty: Closed Won (39,707), Negotiation/ // Review (15,770), Closed Lost (7,575), On Hold (295), Prospecting (36), // Qualification (14), Proposal/Price Quote (1). Excluding both closed // stages here, not just Closed Lost, since "what we're proposing" is // meant to read as current open activity, not historical volume. const PROPOSAL_VOLUME_CLOSED_STAGES = new Set(['Closed Won', 'Closed Lost']); // Backlog is a running balance, not an activity that happened "in" a // fiscal year -- per Misty's direct call (2026-09-07), this screen shows // the current total outstanding balance with no end_date filter, unlike // the Revenue Bridge's PA/DS Backlog steps (which do filter to a specific // fiscal year's conversion). No fiscal year parameter for this reason. async function getPipelineBacklogScreen(env, regionId, fiscalYear) { // ---- Proposal Mix (open only, company-wide or one region) ---- const proposalRows = (await env.DB.prepare( regionId ? `SELECT service_name, SUM(total_vda_fees) AS total FROM proposal_volume_fact WHERE region_id = ? AND total_vda_fees IS NOT NULL GROUP BY service_name` : `SELECT service_name, SUM(total_vda_fees) AS total FROM proposal_volume_fact WHERE total_vda_fees IS NOT NULL GROUP BY service_name` ).bind(...(regionId ?
