[regionId] : [])).all()).results || []; // stage isn't in the GROUP BY above (grouping by service_name is what // the mix needs), so closed-stage exclusion happens via a second pass // rather than a second query: pull stage alongside service_name/fees // and filter in JS, cheaper than a second round trip against the same // table. const proposalRowsWithStage = (await env.DB.prepare( regionId ? `SELECT service_name, stage, total_vda_fees FROM proposal_volume_fact WHERE region_id = ? AND total_vda_fees IS NOT NULL` : `SELECT service_name, stage, total_vda_fees FROM proposal_volume_fact WHERE total_vda_fees IS NOT NULL` ).bind(...(regionId ? [regionId] : [])).all()).results || []; const proposalFamilyTotals = {}; for (const f of ACCOUNT_SERVICE_FAMILIES) proposalFamilyTotals[f.key] = 0; let proposalUnmappedTotal = 0; const proposalUnmappedNames = new Set(); for (const r of proposalRowsWithStage) { if (PROPOSAL_VOLUME_CLOSED_STAGES.has(r.stage)) continue; const bucket = mapProposalServiceNameToFamily(r.service_name); if (bucket) proposalFamilyTotals[bucket] += r.total_vda_fees || 0; else { proposalUnmappedTotal += r.total_vda_fees || 0; if (r.service_name) proposalUnmappedNames.add(r.service_name); } } // ---- Backlog Mix (current total, company-wide or one region) ---- const backlogRows = (await env.DB.prepare( regionId ? `SELECT service_family, service, SUM(contract_balance) AS total FROM backlog_fact WHERE region_id = ? AND contract_balance IS NOT NULL GROUP BY service_family, service` : `SELECT service_family, service, SUM(contract_balance) AS total FROM backlog_fact WHERE contract_balance IS NOT NULL GROUP BY service_family, service` ).bind(...(regionId ? [regionId] : [])).all()).results || []; const backlogFamilyTotals = {}; for (const f of ACCOUNT_SERVICE_FAMILIES) backlogFamilyTotals[f.key] = 0; let backlogUnmappedTotal = 0; for (const r of backlogRows) { const bucket = mapServiceToAccountFamily(r.service_family, r.service); if (bucket) backlogFamilyTotals[bucket] += r.total || 0; else backlogUnmappedTotal += r.total || 0; } const serviceMix = ACCOUNT_SERVICE_FAMILIES.map(f => ({ key: f.key, label: f.label, proposalTotal: proposalFamilyTotals[f.key], backlogTotal: backlogFamilyTotals[f.key], })); // ---- Sales (Bookings) by Service Family (2026-09-08) ------------------ // Moved here from Executive Summary per Misty's reorganization call: it // reads as sales mix, the same lens as the two service-mix cards above, // not an executive KPI. Same exact computation Executive Summary used // (mapServiceToAccountFamily/ACCOUNT_SERVICE_FAMILIES, bookings_fact by // awarded year), just fiscal-year- and region-scoped like the rest of // this screen (this screen previously had no year concept at all, so a // fiscalYear param and Year picker were added alongside the existing // Region picker rather than dropping the year scoping this card always // had on Executive Summary). const bookingsFamilyRows = (await env.DB.prepare( regionId ? `SELECT service_family, service_name, SUM(total_price) AS total FROM bookings_fact WHERE ${AWARDED_YEAR_SQL} = ? AND region_id = ? GROUP BY service_family, service_name` : `SELECT service_family, service_name, SUM(total_price) AS total FROM bookings_fact WHERE ${AWARDED_YEAR_SQL} = ? GROUP BY service_family, service_name` ).bind(...(regionId ? [fiscalYear, regionId] : [fiscalYear])).all()).results || []; const bookingsFamilyTotals = {}; for (const f of ACCOUNT_SERVICE_FAMILIES) bookingsFamilyTotals[f.key] = 0; let bookingsFamilyUnmapped = 0; for (const r of bookingsFamilyRows) { const bucket = mapServiceToAccountFamily(r.service_family, r.service_name); if (bucket) bookingsFamilyTotals[bucket] += r.total || 0; else bookingsFamilyUnmapped += r.total || 0; } const serviceFamilyChart = ACCOUNT_SERVICE_FAMILIES .map(f => ({ key: f.key, label: f.label, total: bookingsFamilyTotals[f.key] })) .sort((a, b) => b.total - a.total); // ---- Regional Backlog Density (always all regions) ---- // Split Inspections from everything else (2026-09-07, per Misty: field // examiners only do inspections, so a blended per-staff figure hid real // staffing pressure on both sides). Pulled row-level so the JS family // mapper (mapServiceToAccountFamily) can bucket the same way the Service // Mix panel above does, instead of trusting the raw service_family text. const regionBacklogRowsDetailed = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT region_id, service_family, service, SUM(contract_balance) AS total FROM backlog_fact WHERE contract_balance IS NOT NULL AND region_id IS NOT NULL GROUP BY region_id, service_family, service` ).all()).results || []; const regionInspectionsBacklog = new Map(); const regionNonInspectionBacklog = new Map(); for (const r of regionBacklogRowsDetailed) { const bucket = mapServiceToAccountFamily(r.service_family, r.service); const target = bucket === 'inspections' ? regionInspectionsBacklog : regionNonInspectionBacklog; target.set(r.region_id, (target.get(r.region_id) || 0) + (r.total || 0)); } // Field examiners (source_file='inspectors') only ever work Inspections; // consultants and area managers (source_file='consultants') cover // everything else. Same coverage_roster the Regions screen's Market // Coverage card uses, split by role instead of lumped together. const regionStaffRowsSplit = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT region_id, role, COUNT(*) AS n FROM coverage_roster WHERE counts_toward_coverage = 1 AND region_id IS NOT NULL GROUP BY region_id, role` ).all()).results || []; const regionFieldExaminerCounts = new Map(); const regionConsultantCounts = new Map(); for (const r of regionStaffRowsSplit) { const target = r.role === 'Field Examiner' ? regionFieldExaminerCounts : regionConsultantCounts; target.set(r.region_id, (target.get(r.region_id) || 0) + (r.n || 0)); } const allRegions = (await env.DB.prepare(`SELECT id, name FROM regions ORDER BY name`).all()).results || []; const regionalDensity = allRegions .map(r => { const inspectionsBacklogTotal = regionInspectionsBacklog.get(r.id) || 0; const nonInspectionBacklogTotal = regionNonInspectionBacklog.get(r.id) || 0; const fieldExaminerCount = regionFieldExaminerCounts.get(r.id) || 0; const consultantCount = regionConsultantCounts.get(r.id) || 0; return { regionId: r.id, regionName: r.name, inspectionsBacklogTotal, fieldExaminerCount, inspectionsDensityPerStaff: fieldExaminerCount > 0 ? inspectionsBacklogTotal / fieldExaminerCount : null, nonInspectionBacklogTotal, consultantCount, nonInspectionDensityPerStaff: consultantCount > 0 ? nonInspectionBacklogTotal / consultantCount : null, }; }) .filter(r => r.inspectionsBacklogTotal > 0 || r.nonInspectionBacklogTotal > 0 || r.fieldExaminerCount > 0 || r.consultantCount > 0) .sort((a, b) => (b.nonInspectionDensityPerStaff || 0) - (a.nonInspectionDensityPerStaff || 0)); const coverageRowCount = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM coverage_roster WHERE counts_toward_coverage = 1` ).first())?.n || 0; const backlogMovement = await getBacklogMovement(env, regionId); return { regionId: regionId || null, fiscalYear, serviceMix, proposalUnmapped: { total: proposalUnmappedTotal, names: [...proposalUnmappedNames] }, backlogUnmapped: { total: backlogUnmappedTotal }, serviceFamilyChart, serviceFamilyUnmapped: bookingsFamilyUnmapped, regionalDensity, coverageAvailable: coverageRowCount > 0, backlogMovement, }; } // Backlog Movement (2026-09-12, Backlog History / Pull-Through, see // scoping_backlog_history.md and captureBacklogSnapshot above). Compares // the most recent two real backlog_snapshots dates for a real, measured // view of how backlog actually moved, rather than a point-in-time ratio // alone. Company-wide or region-scoped (region_id is stored per key on // the snapshot, same as backlog_fact). // // Real classification, deliberately conservative: "billed / pulled // through" vs. "fell out" can't be told apart from backlog_snapshots // alone (both look like "disappeared from the later snapshot") without // cross-referencing billed_fact for that exact project — an open // question flagged in the scoping doc, not yet resolved with Misty — so // this returns a single "no longer in backlog" bucket rather than // guessing which is which, plus separate "still open" and "new" buckets // that ARE unambiguous from the snapshots alone. async function getBacklogMovement(env, regionId) { const dateRows = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT DISTINCT snapshot_date FROM backlog_snapshots ORDER BY snapshot_date DESC LIMIT 2` ).all()).results || []; if (dateRows.length < 2) { // Only one (or zero) real snapshot exists yet -- nothing to compare, // and no fabricated "0% change" should be shown instead. return { available: false, snapshotCount: dateRows.length, latestSnapshotDate: dateRows[0]?.snapshot_date || null }; } const [latestDate, priorDate] = [dateRows[0].snapshot_date, dateRows[1].snapshot_date]; const regionClause = regionId ? 'AND region_id = ?' : ''; const bindArgs = (date) => regionId ? [date, regionId] : [date]; const priorRows = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT project_number, project_task_name, contract_balance FROM backlog_snapshots WHERE snapshot_date = ? ${regionClause}` ).bind(...bindArgs(priorDate)).all()).results || []; const latestRows = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT project_number, project_task_name, contract_balance FROM backlog_snapshots WHERE snapshot_date = ? ${regionClause}` ).bind(...bindArgs(latestDate)).all()).results || []; const priorByKey = new Map(priorRows.map(r => [`${r.project_number}${r.project_task_name}`, r.contract_balance || 0])); const latestByKey = new Map(latestRows.map(r => [`${r.project_number}${r.project_task_name}`, r.contract_balance || 0])); let noLongerInBacklog = 0; // in prior, absent (or fully reduced to 0) in latest let stillOpen = 0; // in both, unchanged or reduced but still present let newBacklog = 0; // in latest, absent from prior const priorTotal = [...priorByKey.values()].reduce((s, v) => s + v, 0); const latestTotal = [...latestByKey.values()].reduce((s, v) => s + v, 0); for (const [key, priorAmt] of priorByKey) { const latestAmt = latestByKey.has(key) ? latestByKey.get(key) : 0; if (!latestByKey.has(key) || latestAmt <= 0) { noLongerInBacklog += priorAmt; } else if (latestAmt < priorAmt) { noLongerInBacklog += (priorAmt - latestAmt); stillOpen += latestAmt; } else { stillOpen += latestAmt; } } for (const [key, latestAmt] of latestByKey) { if (!priorByKey.has(key)) newBacklog += latestAmt; } return { available: true, priorSnapshotDate: priorDate, latestSnapshotDate: latestDate, priorTotal, latestTotal, noLongerInBacklog, stillOpen, newBacklog, // A rate is only meaningful against real eligible backlog, and only // an approximation of "pull-through" until billed_fact // cross-referencing (see comment above) can separate billed from // fallen-out -- labeled accordingly in the UI, not presented as a // precise realization rate. noLongerInBacklogRate: priorTotal > 0 ? noLongerInBacklog / priorTotal : null, }; } // ---- Line of Business resolution (v1q, 2026-09-06) --------------------- // // Every bookings_fact row needs a real line_of_business (program_advisory // / design_services / inspections) for LOB-grain reporting (Slide 3's // attach/retention metrics, the first real consumer). service_alignment // is an OVERRIDE table only — confirmed empty in the live app before this // batch (Misty ran the query directly) — so a default mapping has to // exist for every real service_family/service_name combination, with // service_alignment able to override any of it for a specific fiscal // year (the QCE 2027 reclassification is the first real override, seeded // below). // // DEFAULT_LOB_MAP is deliberately visible data, not buried logic: the // Service Alignment screen renders it as a read-only "Defaults" table // (GET /api/baseline/service-alignment-defaults) so Misty can see and // correct it without touching code — "editing" a default there creates a // real service_alignment override row for the year in question rather // than silently rewriting this table, so every departure from the // default stays auditable and effective-dated, exactly like every other // correction in this system (region renames, service_alignment itself). // // Confirmed directly with Misty, family by family, against the real // distinct service_family/service_name values in bookings_fact: // - Modernization Services, Equipment Evaluation, Expediting Services, // and Maintenance Management (including its VERIFY/General Consulting // carve-outs) all default to Program Advisory. // - Inspection Services and CCM default to Inspections. // - Design Services defaults to Design Services. // - Construction Services is genuinely split, confirmed against the // real service_name breakdown (SELECT service_name, SUM(total_price) // FROM bookings_fact WHERE service_family = 'Construction Services' // GROUP BY service_name): only 'Design Services Site Visit' (the // 3rd-largest bucket by $, ~$5M) goes to Design Services; every other // name under this family (Modernization Site Visits, Misc Construction // Services, Construction Site Visits, the Project Kick Off variants, // Modernization Closeout/Progress/Phase/Commencement, Construction // Services Expenses, Technical Specification Compliance, Comprehensive // Warranty Equipment Evaluation) goes to Program Advisory. const CONSTRUCTION_DESIGN_SERVICE_NAMES = new Set(['design services site visit']); const DEFAULT_LOB_MAP = [ { serviceFamily: 'Modernization Services', serviceName: null, lineOfBusiness: 'program_advisory' }, { serviceFamily: 'Equipment Evaluation', serviceName: null, lineOfBusiness: 'program_advisory' }, { serviceFamily: 'Expediting Services', serviceName: null, lineOfBusiness: 'program_advisory' }, { serviceFamily: 'Maintenance Management', serviceName: null, lineOfBusiness: 'program_advisory' }, { serviceFamily: 'Inspection Services', serviceName: null, lineOfBusiness: 'inspections' }, { serviceFamily: 'CCM', serviceName: null, lineOfBusiness: 'inspections' }, { serviceFamily: 'Design Services', serviceName: null, lineOfBusiness: 'design_services' }, { serviceFamily: 'Construction Services', serviceName: 'Design Services Site Visit', lineOfBusiness: 'design_services' }, { serviceFamily: 'Construction Services', serviceName: null, lineOfBusiness: 'program_advisory' }, ]; function defaultLineOfBusiness(serviceFamily, serviceName) { const family = (serviceFamily || '').trim(); const name = (serviceName || '').trim().toLowerCase(); if (family === 'Construction Services') { return CONSTRUCTION_DESIGN_SERVICE_NAMES.has(name) ? 'design_services' : 'program_advisory'; } const match = DEFAULT_LOB_MAP.find(m => m.serviceFamily === family && m.serviceName === null); return match ? match.lineOfBusiness : null; // unrecognized family: excluded, not silently bucketed } // Resolves the real line_of_business for a bookings_fact row as of a // given fiscal year: an exact service_alignment override (service_name- // level first, then family-level) wins if one exists for that year, // otherwise falls back to defaultLineOfBusiness above. `alignmentRows` // is the full service_alignment table (or the subset already loaded by // the caller) — passed in rather than queried per-row so a report // scanning thousands of bookings rows does one query, not one per row. function resolveLineOfBusiness(serviceFamily, serviceName, fiscalYear, alignmentRows) { const family = (serviceFamily || '').trim(); const name = (serviceName || '').trim(); const nameOverride = alignmentRows.find(a => a.service_family === family && a.service_name === name && a.effective_fiscal_year === fiscalYear); if (nameOverride) return nameOverride.line_of_business; const familyOverride = alignmentRows.find(a => a.service_family === family && a.service_name === null && a.effective_fiscal_year === fiscalYear); if (familyOverride) return familyOverride.line_of_business; return defaultLineOfBusiness(family, name); } const MONTH_NAMES = ['', 'Jan','Feb','Mar','Apr','May','Jun','Jul','Aug','Sep','Oct','Nov','Dec']; // Business days (Mon-Fri) between day 1 and day `throughDay` (inclusive) // of the given month/year, and the month's total business days — used // for the "avg daily rate x remaining business days" leg of the blended // forecast, exactly matching Gavin's own formula (§48, verified to the // exact cent). function businessDaysInRange(year, month, fromDay, throughDay) { let count = 0; for (let d = fromDay; d <= throughDay; d++) { const dow = new Date(Date.UTC(year, month - 1, d)).getUTCDay(); if (dow !== 0 && dow !== 6) count++; } return count; } function daysInMonth(year, month) { return new Date(Date.UTC(year, month, 0)).getUTCDate(); } // Determines "today" from the data itself (MAX(awarded_date) in // bookings_fact) rather than wall-clock time, so the forecast reflects // what's actually been uploaded, not the calendar date the server // happens to run on. Falls back to wall-clock only when the table is // empty (fresh install, nothing uploaded yet). async function resolveForecastAsOf(env) { const row = await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT awarded_date, ${AWARDED_YEAR_SQL} AS yr, ${AWARDED_MONTH_SQL} AS mo FROM bookings_fact WHERE awarded_date IS NOT NULL AND awarded_date != '' ORDER BY yr DESC, mo DESC, CAST(SUBSTR(awarded_date, INSTR(awarded_date,'/')+1, INSTR(SUBSTR(awarded_date, INSTR(awarded_date,'/')+1), '/') - 1) AS INTEGER) DESC LIMIT 1` ).first(); if (!row || !row.yr || !row.mo) { const now = new Date(); return { year: now.getUTCFullYear(), month: now.getUTCMonth() + 1, day: now.getUTCDate() }; } // Day-of-month for business-day math: parse it out of the winning row's // own awarded_date string (M/D/YYYY) rather than re-deriving from SQL. const parts = row.awarded_date.split('/'); const day = parseInt(parts[1], 10) || 1; return { year: row.yr, month: row.mo, day }; } // Core blended-forecast calculator for a set of bookings_fact rows // already filtered to the relevant scope (a region, a LOB, company-wide // — caller's WHERE clause decides). `monthlyTotals` is a Map for fiscalYear, already summed by the caller's query. function computeBlendedForecast(monthlyTotals, asOf) { const { year, month: currentMonth, day: currentDay } = asOf; const priorMonths = []; for (let m = 1; m < currentMonth; m++) priorMonths.push(m); const remainingMonths = []; for (let m = currentMonth + 1; m <= 12; m++) remainingMonths.push(m); const priorActualsSum = priorMonths.reduce((sum, m) => sum + (monthlyTotals.get(m) || 0), 0); const priorAvgPerMonth = priorMonths.length ? priorActualsSum / priorMonths.length : 0; const currentMonthActuals = monthlyTotals.get(currentMonth) || 0; const totalBizDaysInMonth = businessDaysInRange(year, currentMonth, 1, daysInMonth(year, currentMonth)); const bizDaysElapsed = businessDaysInRange(year, currentMonth, 1, currentDay); const bizDaysRemaining = Math.max(totalBizDaysInMonth - bizDaysElapsed, 0); const avgDailyRate = bizDaysElapsed > 0 ? currentMonthActuals / bizDaysElapsed : 0; const currentMonthBlended = currentMonthActuals + (avgDailyRate * bizDaysRemaining); const remainingMonthsForecast = priorAvgPerMonth * remainingMonths.length; const totalYearForecast = priorActualsSum + currentMonthBlended + remainingMonthsForecast; return { priorActualsSum, priorAvgPerMonth, priorMonths, currentMonth, currentMonthActuals, currentMonthBlended, bizDaysElapsed, bizDaysRemaining, totalBizDaysInMonth, remainingMonths, remainingMonthsForecast, totalYearForecast, }; } // Revenue Backlog, scheduled by month (2026-09-10, per Misty's direct // request: "for the revenue blended forecast we should take the run rate // but also what's in the backlog mainly"). backlog_fact is real, already- // won business with an outstanding Contract Balance not yet invoiced (the // "Revenue Backlog" concept, per its own schema comment) — end_date is // when that balance is expected to complete/bill, so bucketing // contract_balance by END_MONTH_SQL gives a real month-by-month schedule // of backlog completing within fiscalYear, not a guess. Returns // Map, optionally scoped to one region and/or one // line of business (lobKey matched via mapRevenueTypeToLob on // backlog_fact.revenue_type, same mapping every other backlog read in // this file already uses). async function loadRevenueBacklogScheduledByMonth(env, fiscalYear, { regionId = null, lobKey = null } = {}) { const conditions = [`end_date IS NOT NULL`, `end_date != ''`, `${END_YEAR_SQL} = ?`, `contract_balance IS NOT NULL`]; const params = [fiscalYear]; if (regionId) { conditions.push(`region_id = ?`); params.push(regionId); } const rows = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT ${END_MONTH_SQL} AS month, revenue_type, SUM(contract_balance) AS total FROM backlog_fact WHERE ${conditions.join(' AND ')} GROUP BY month, revenue_type` ).bind(...params).all()).results || []; const byMonth = new Map(); for (const r of rows) { if (lobKey && mapRevenueTypeToLob(r.revenue_type) !== lobKey) continue; byMonth.set(r.month, (byMonth.get(r.month) || 0) + (r.total || 0)); } return byMonth; } // Generic scheduled-blend forecast (2026-09-10, generalized from the // Revenue-only version to also serve Sales): same computeBlendedForecast // math for prior months and the current month (those are real actuals/ // actual-to-date either way, a schedule doesn't change them), but the // REMAINING months' projection prefers a real month-by-month schedule // (Revenue Backlog for Revenue, Weighted Pipeline for Sales) over the // pure trailing-average run rate. // // Floored at the trailing average (2026-09-14, per Misty's direct // request, reversing the original 2026-09-10 design). The comment this // replaces said "scheduled-per-month first... not capped to whichever // is greater." Real case that prompted the change: Sales' // Total Year Forecast tile came in at $74.12M, well BELOW the $96.26M // trailing-average-only figure the Sales-by-LOB table showed for the // same fiscal year, because Oct/Nov/Dec's real logged Weighted // Pipeline was thinner than the Jan-Aug run rate, and the old logic // substituted that smaller pipeline number in place of the average // rather than using whichever was higher. Misty's call: "It should use // the blended run rate unless the pipeline value happens to be higher // and there should be a signal there." Thin/incomplete real-time // pipeline data should never drag the forecast below a proven // historical baseline; it should only ever raise it. Applied to both // Sales (pipeline) and Revenue (backlog) per Misty's explicit // confirmation that the two should stay consistent with each other. // // `sourceTag` labels remainingMonthsDetail's rows: a month keeps its // real source tag ('backlog' or 'pipeline') only when that schedule // actually beat the floor and is the number actually used; a month // where the schedule existed but lost to the floor, or where nothing // was scheduled at all, is tagged 'run_rate' either way, since the // trailing average is what's actually driving that month's number in // both cases, so the frontend's source signal should reflect what's // really feeding the total, not merely whether a schedule existed. function computeScheduledBlendedForecast(monthlyTotals, asOf, scheduledByMonth, sourceTag) { const base = computeBlendedForecast(monthlyTotals, asOf); let remainingMonthsForecast = 0; const remainingMonthsDetail = base.remainingMonths.map(m => { const scheduledAmount = scheduledByMonth.get(m); const hasSchedule = scheduledAmount !== undefined && scheduledAmount !== null; // Floor: never let a real schedule pull a remaining month below the // trailing average. A month with nothing scheduled just uses the // average outright, same as before. const amount = hasSchedule ? Math.max(scheduledAmount, base.priorAvgPerMonth) : base.priorAvgPerMonth; const scheduleWon = hasSchedule && scheduledAmount >= base.priorAvgPerMonth; remainingMonthsForecast += amount; return { month: m, amount, source: scheduleWon ? sourceTag : 'run_rate', scheduledAmount: hasSchedule ? scheduledAmount : null }; }); const totalYearForecast = base.priorActualsSum + base.currentMonthBlended + remainingMonthsForecast; return { ...base, remainingMonthsForecast, remainingMonthsDetail, totalYearForecast, }; } // Revenue's blended forecast: remaining months prefer real scheduled // Revenue Backlog (backlog_fact, per-month via loadRevenueBacklogScheduledByMonth // above). Sales keeps using computeBlendedForecast directly/unchanged in // most places; computeSalesBlendedForecast below is the one Sales call // site (Month Pace, company-wide) that also picked up scheduled blending, // via Weighted Pipeline instead of backlog. function computeRevenueBlendedForecast(monthlyTotals, asOf, backlogByMonth) { return computeScheduledBlendedForecast(monthlyTotals, asOf, backlogByMonth, 'backlog'); } // Sales' blended forecast (2026-09-10, per Misty's direct request: "should // sales look at what's in the proposed opportunities?"). Mirrors the // Revenue treatment above, using Weighted Pipeline (proposal_pipeline_fact, // loadWeightedPipelineScheduledByMonth below) as the real remaining-months // schedule instead of backlog — the same real, already-tuned probability- // weighted pipeline concept the Revenue Bridge's own Step 5 already uses, // just bucketed by month instead of summed to one fiscal-year total. function computeSalesBlendedForecast(monthlyTotals, asOf, pipelineByMonth) { return computeScheduledBlendedForecast(monthlyTotals, asOf, pipelineByMonth, 'pipeline'); } // Weighted Pipeline, scheduled by month (2026-09-10). Reuses the Revenue // Bridge's own Step 5 methodology exactly (see that comment block, ~line // 4993, for the full history/reasoning) rather than re-deriving it: // proposal_pipeline_fact rows in an open stage (not Closed Won/Lost) with // a real fee and probability, estimated_award_date within fiscalYear, // excluded once more than 60 days past due (asOf-relative, the same // stale-pipeline guard), and the same admin-set stage-default-probability // override (pipeline_default_probability_settings) applied to // Negotiation/Review and Proposal/Price Quote rows still carrying // Salesforce's flat 75% stage default. The one real difference from Step // 5: that function sums everything into one fiscal-year total; this one // buckets each row's weighted contribution into the month its // estimated_award_date falls in, so it can feed a remaining-months // schedule instead of a single number. proposal_pipeline_fact has no LOB // column at all (same real limitation Step 5 itself notes — "table has no // LOB column"), so this is company-wide/region-wide only, never per-LOB; // there is no Sales equivalent of Business Lines' per-LOB backlog blend // for that reason. Returns Map. async function loadWeightedPipelineScheduledByMonth(env, fiscalYear, asOf, { regionId = null } = {}) { const conditions = [ `total_vda_fees IS NOT NULL`, `probability_pct IS NOT NULL`, `estimated_award_date IS NOT NULL`, `estimated_award_date != ''`, `${ESTIMATED_AWARD_YEAR_SQL} = ?`, `stage NOT IN ('Closed Won', 'Closed Lost')`, ]; const params = [fiscalYear]; if (regionId) { conditions.push(`region_id = ?`); params.push(regionId); } const pipelineRows = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT total_vda_fees, probability_pct, estimated_award_date, stage FROM proposal_pipeline_fact WHERE ${conditions.join(' AND ')}` ).bind(...params).all()).results || []; const defaultProbabilitySetting = await getRegionPipelineDefaultProbability(env, fiscalYear); const STAGE_DEFAULT_PROBABILITY = 75; const STAGE_DEFAULT_OVERRIDE_STAGES = new Set(['Negotiation/Review', 'Proposal/Price Quote']); const PIPELINE_PAST_DUE_GRACE_DAYS = 60; const asOfDateForPipeline = new Date(Date.UTC(asOf.year, asOf.month - 1, asOf.day)); const byMonth = new Map(); for (const r of pipelineRows) { const parts = r.estimated_award_date.split('/'); if (parts.length !== 3) continue; const m = parseInt(parts[0], 10), d = parseInt(parts[1], 10), y = parseInt(parts[2], 10); if (!m || !d || !y) continue; const awardDate = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d)); const daysPastDue = (asOfDateForPipeline - awardDate) / (24 * 60 * 60 * 1000); if (daysPastDue > PIPELINE_PAST_DUE_GRACE_DAYS) continue; let probability = r.probability_pct; if (probability === STAGE_DEFAULT_PROBABILITY && STAGE_DEFAULT_OVERRIDE_STAGES.has(r.stage)) { probability = defaultProbabilitySetting.probabilityPct; } const contribution = r.total_vda_fees * probability / 100.0; byMonth.set(m, (byMonth.get(m) || 0) + contribution); } return byMonth; } // ---- Forecast: Business Lines (Sales, real; Revenue, illustrative) ---- async function getForecastBusinessLines(env, fiscalYear) { const asOf = await resolveForecastAsOf(env); const rows = await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT revenue_type, ${AWARDED_MONTH_SQL} AS month, SUM(total_price) AS total FROM bookings_fact WHERE ${AWARDED_YEAR_SQL} = ? AND awarded_date IS NOT NULL AND awarded_date != '' GROUP BY revenue_type, month` ).bind(fiscalYear).all(); // lob -> Map const byLob = { program_advisory: new Map(), design_services: new Map(), inspections: new Map() }; let unmappedTotal = 0; for (const r of (rows.results || [])) { const lob = mapRevenueTypeToLob(r.revenue_type); if (!lob) { unmappedTotal += r.total || 0; continue; } byLob[lob].set(r.month, (byLob[lob].get(r.month) || 0) + (r.total || 0)); } // Prior-year same-month actuals, by LOB (2026-09-11, Open Items 2(c)): // Misty wants a directional arrow on each forecast month showing // whether it's tracking above or below the same month last year. Real // history already exists in bookings_fact — one extra query for // fiscalYear-1, bucketed the same way as the current-year query above, // no new table or fabricated data. Only feeds the arrow on forecast- // basis months (m > asOf.month); actual and current-month cells already // show real numbers and don't need a directional hint. const priorYearRows = await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT revenue_type, ${AWARDED_MONTH_SQL} AS month, SUM(total_price) AS total FROM bookings_fact WHERE ${AWARDED_YEAR_SQL} = ? AND awarded_date IS NOT NULL AND awarded_date != '' GROUP BY revenue_type, month` ).bind(fiscalYear - 1).all(); const priorYearByLob = { program_advisory: new Map(), design_services: new Map(), inspections: new Map() }; for (const r of (priorYearRows.results || [])) { const lob = mapRevenueTypeToLob(r.revenue_type); if (!lob) continue; priorYearByLob[lob].set(r.month, (priorYearByLob[lob].get(r.month) || 0) + (r.total || 0)); } const lines = Object.keys(byLob).map((lobKey) => { const forecast = computeBlendedForecast(byLob[lobKey], asOf); const months = []; for (let m = 1; m <= 12; m++) { const isForecastMonth = m > asOf.month; const priorYearActual = isForecastMonth ? (priorYearByLob[lobKey].has(m) ? priorYearByLob[lobKey].get(m) : null) : null; const forecastAmount = isForecastMonth ? forecast.priorAvgPerMonth : null; months.push({ month: m, label: MONTH_NAMES[m], actual: m < asOf.month ? (byLob[lobKey].get(m) || 0) : null, current: m === asOf.month ? forecast.currentMonthBlended : null, forecast: forecastAmount, // priorYearActual/yoyVsPriorYear: forecast-months only, null // (never a fake 0/flat arrow) when last year has no real data for // that month at all. priorYearActual, yoyVsPriorYear: (isForecastMonth && priorYearActual !== null && forecastAmount !== null) ? (forecastAmount >= priorYearActual ? 'up' : 'down') : null, }); } const ytdActual = forecast.priorActualsSum + (asOf.month <= 12 ? (byLob[lobKey].get(asOf.month) || 0) : 0); return { lob: lobKey, label: LOB_LABELS[lobKey], months, ytdActual, totalYearForecast: forecast.totalYearForecast, }; }); // Company Total row = sum of the 3 LOB rows, computed here (not typed // separately) so it can never drift from its own components — this is // exactly the class of bug caught and fixed in the round-1 mockup (§48: // "Company Total row was independently typed... off by $8-40K"). const companyTotal = { lob: 'company_total', label: 'Company Total', months: Array.from({ length: 12 }, (_, i) => { const m = i + 1; const actual = lines.reduce((s, l) => s + (l.months[i].actual || 0), 0); const current = lines.reduce((s, l) => s + (l.months[i].current || 0), 0); const forecastM = lines.reduce((s, l) => s + (l.months[i].forecast || 0), 0); // Company Total's own priorYearActual/yoyVsPriorYear (2(c)): summed // the same way as every other Company Total figure on this row — // null only when EVERY LOB has null prior-year data for that month, // otherwise sums whatever real LOB-level data exists. const priorYearParts = lines.map(l => l.months[i].priorYearActual); const anyPriorYear = priorYearParts.some(v => v !== null); const priorYearTotal = anyPriorYear ? priorYearParts.reduce((s, v) => s + (v || 0), 0) : null; return { month: m, label: MONTH_NAMES[m], actual: m < asOf.month ? actual : null, current: m === asOf.month ? current : null, forecast: m > asOf.month ? forecastM : null, priorYearActual: m > asOf.month ? priorYearTotal : null, yoyVsPriorYear: (m > asOf.month && priorYearTotal !== null) ? (forecastM >= priorYearTotal ? 'up' : 'down') : null, }; }), ytdActual: lines.reduce((s, l) => s + l.ytdActual, 0), totalYearForecast: lines.reduce((s, l) => s + l.totalYearForecast, 0), }; // Company Total pipeline override (2026-09-14), per Misty's direct // request. proposal_pipeline_fact has no LOB column, so the 3 Sales // LOB rows above can never use real Weighted Pipeline for their own // remaining months. Only Forecast's whole-company Month Pace tile // could, which is exactly why that tile's Total Year Forecast used to // silently diverge from this table's Company Total (the tile pipeline- // blended, this table didn't). Rather than leave two different // "company total" numbers on two screens, or force a made-up per-LOB // pipeline split, Company Total's remaining-month cells and // totalYearForecast are overridden here with the same real, floored, // pipeline-aware company-wide figure the tile shows, computed the // exact same way (computeSalesBlendedForecast against the whole- // company monthly total, not a sum of the 3 LOB forecasts). Company // Total is therefore INTENTIONALLY no longer a pure sum of the 3 LOB // rows above it for any remaining month with real pipeline beating the // floor. companyTotalIsPipelineAware flags this so the frontend can // label it rather than let it look like a math error (the §48 mockup // bug this row's own comment above warns about was a genuine typo; // this is a deliberate, labeled exception to "always sum the LOBs"). const companyMonthlyTotals = new Map(); for (const lobKey of Object.keys(byLob)) { for (const [m, dollars] of byLob[lobKey].entries()) { companyMonthlyTotals.set(m, (companyMonthlyTotals.get(m) || 0) + dollars); } } const companyPipelineByMonth = await loadWeightedPipelineScheduledByMonth(env, fiscalYear, asOf, {}); const companyForecast = computeSalesBlendedForecast(companyMonthlyTotals, asOf, companyPipelineByMonth); let companyTotalIsPipelineAware = false; for (let i = 0; i < 12; i++) { const m = i + 1; if (m <= asOf.month) continue; // only remaining months are affected const detail = companyForecast.remainingMonthsDetail.find(d => d.month === m); if (!detail) continue; companyTotal.months[i].forecast = detail.amount; companyTotal.months[i].forecastSource = detail.source; // 'pipeline' or 'run_rate' companyTotal.months[i].forecastScheduledAmount = detail.scheduledAmount; if (detail.source === 'pipeline') companyTotalIsPipelineAware = true; // Re-derive the YoY arrow against the corrected forecast amount, same // rule as every other cell on this row. const priorYearTotal = companyTotal.months[i].priorYearActual; companyTotal.months[i].yoyVsPriorYear = (priorYearTotal !== null) ? (detail.amount >= priorYearTotal ? 'up' : 'down') : null; } companyTotal.totalYearForecast = companyForecast.totalYearForecast; companyTotal.companyTotalIsPipelineAware = companyTotalIsPipelineAware; // Revenue (2026-09-04): now real, from billed_fact, once that fiscal // year has real uploaded data. Uses the exact same blended-forecast // math and the same asOf window as Sales above, just fed by // billed_fact.amount (Adj. Billings) grouped by month/LOB instead of // bookings_fact.total_price. revenueAvailable stays false when no // billed_fact rows exist yet for this fiscal year at all, so the // frontend can still show its explicit "pending billed data" state for // any year that hasn't been uploaded, rather than a misleading all-zero // table. const { byMonth: revenueByMonthLob, unmappedTotal: revenueUnmappedTotal } = await loadBilledFactMonthlyByLob(env, fiscalYear); const revenueAvailable = revenueByMonthLob.size > 0; // Prior-year same-month actuals for Revenue's forecast-month arrows // (2(c)), same treatment as Sales above — real billed_fact history for // fiscalYear-1, no new table. const { byMonth: priorYearRevenueByMonthLob } = await loadBilledFactMonthlyByLob(env, fiscalYear - 1); let revenue = null; if (revenueAvailable) { const revenueByLob = { program_advisory: new Map(), design_services: new Map(), inspections: new Map() }; for (const [month, lobs] of revenueByMonthLob.entries()) { for (const lobKey of Object.keys(revenueByLob)) { revenueByLob[lobKey].set(month, lobs[lobKey] || 0); } } // Backlog-blended (2026-09-10, per Misty's direct request): each LOB's // remaining months use that LOB's real scheduled Revenue Backlog where // it exists, trailing average only as fallback — see // computeRevenueBlendedForecast. Per-LOB backlog query (lobKey), no // region filter, matching this function's own company-wide scope. // Promise.all since each LOB now needs its own backlog fetch (this // was a synchronous .map before). const revenueLines = await Promise.all(Object.keys(revenueByLob).map(async (lobKey) => { const backlogByMonth = await loadRevenueBacklogScheduledByMonth(env, fiscalYear, { lobKey }); const forecast = computeRevenueBlendedForecast(revenueByLob[lobKey], asOf, backlogByMonth); const months = []; for (let m = 1; m <= 12; m++) { const remainingDetail = forecast.remainingMonthsDetail.find(d => d.month === m); const isForecastMonth = m > asOf.month; const forecastAmount = remainingDetail ? remainingDetail.amount : null; const priorYearMonthLobs = priorYearRevenueByMonthLob.get(m); const priorYearActual = (isForecastMonth && priorYearMonthLobs) ? (priorYearMonthLobs[lobKey] || 0) : null; months.push({ month: m, label: MONTH_NAMES[m], actual: m < asOf.month ? (revenueByLob[lobKey].get(m) || 0) : null, current: m === asOf.month ? forecast.currentMonthBlended : null, forecast: forecastAmount, // forecastSource (2026-09-14): 'backlog' only when this LOB's // real scheduled Revenue Backlog for this month actually beat // the trailing-average floor (see computeScheduledBlendedForecast's // 2026-09-14 comment) and is what's really driving the number; // 'run_rate' otherwise, including a month with backlog // scheduled that lost to the floor. Drives the small source // marker on this cell in the LOB table. forecastSource: remainingDetail ? remainingDetail.source : null, // forecastScheduledAmount (2026-09-14): the raw real backlog // figure for this month, whether or not it won the floor. // lets the frontend's source-dot tooltip show the actual // number rather than a generic label. forecastScheduledAmount: remainingDetail ? remainingDetail.scheduledAmount : null, priorYearActual, yoyVsPriorYear: (isForecastMonth && priorYearActual !== null && forecastAmount !== null) ? (forecastAmount >= priorYearActual ? 'up' : 'down') : null, }); } const ytdActual = forecast.priorActualsSum + (asOf.month <= 12 ? (revenueByLob[lobKey].get(asOf.month) || 0) : 0); return { lob: lobKey, label: LOB_LABELS[lobKey], months, ytdActual, totalYearForecast: forecast.totalYearForecast }; })); const revenueCompanyTotal = { lob: 'company_total', label: 'Company Total', months: Array.from({ length: 12 }, (_, i) => { const m = i + 1; const actual = revenueLines.reduce((s, l) => s + (l.months[i].actual || 0), 0); const current = revenueLines.reduce((s, l) => s + (l.months[i].current || 0), 0); const forecastM = revenueLines.reduce((s, l) => s + (l.months[i].forecast || 0), 0); const priorYearParts = revenueLines.map(l => l.months[i].priorYearActual); const anyPriorYear = priorYearParts.some(v => v !== null); const priorYearTotal = anyPriorYear ? priorYearParts.reduce((s, v) => s + (v || 0), 0) : null; // Company Total's forecastSource (2026-09-14): 'backlog' if ANY // contributing LOB's real scheduled backlog beat the floor for // this month (Revenue's Company Total is a real sum of the 3 LOB // forecasts, unlike Sales', so no override is needed here, just a // rollup of what's already true per LOB). const anyBacklog = m > asOf.month && revenueLines.some(l => l.months[i].forecastSource === 'backlog'); return { month: m, label: MONTH_NAMES[m], actual: m < asOf.month ? actual : null, current: m === asOf.month ? current : null, forecast: m > asOf.month ? forecastM : null, forecastSource: m > asOf.month ? (anyBacklog ? 'backlog' : 'run_rate') : null, priorYearActual: m > asOf.month ? priorYearTotal : null, yoyVsPriorYear: (m > asOf.month && priorYearTotal !== null) ? (forecastM >= priorYearTotal ? 'up' : 'down') : null, }; }), ytdActual: revenueLines.reduce((s, l) => s + l.ytdActual, 0), totalYearForecast: revenueLines.reduce((s, l) => s + l.totalYearForecast, 0), }; revenue = { lines: revenueLines, companyTotal: revenueCompanyTotal, unmappedTotal: revenueUnmappedTotal }; } return { fiscalYear, asOf, unmappedTotal, sales: { lines, companyTotal }, revenue, revenueAvailable, }; } // Loads this fiscal year's real Revenue Budget monthly figures, keyed by // month (1-12), summed across every region/LOB — used by Month Pace's // budget-comparison tiles. Returns null (not a Map) when no monthly rows // exist for this year at all, so the caller can distinguish "real // monthly data exists, this particular month just has $0 entered" from // "no monthly entry has happened yet, fall back to annual÷12". async function loadRevenueMonthlyBudgetTotals(env, fiscalYear) { const rows = await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT month, SUM(amount) AS total FROM revenue_budgets_monthly WHERE fiscal_year = ? GROUP BY month` ).bind(fiscalYear).all(); const results = rows.results || []; if (results.length === 0) return null; const map = new Map(); for (const r of results) map.set(r.month, r.total || 0); return map; } // ---- Forecast: Month Pace (company-wide roll-up of the same math) ---- async function getForecastMonthPace(env, fiscalYear) { const asOf = await resolveForecastAsOf(env); const rows = await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT ${AWARDED_MONTH_SQL} AS month, SUM(total_price) AS total FROM bookings_fact WHERE ${AWARDED_YEAR_SQL} = ? AND awarded_date IS NOT NULL AND awarded_date != '' GROUP BY month` ).bind(fiscalYear).all(); const monthlyTotals = new Map(); for (const r of (rows.results || [])) monthlyTotals.set(r.month, r.total || 0); // Pipeline-blended (2026-09-10, per Misty's direct request: "should // sales look at what's in the proposed opportunities?"): remaining // months use real Weighted Pipeline (proposal_pipeline_fact, same // methodology as the Revenue Bridge's own Step 5) where a month has // scheduled pipeline, trailing average only as fallback — see // computeSalesBlendedForecast. Company-wide here (no regionId), // matching this function's own scope; proposal_pipeline_fact has no // LOB column, so this can't extend to Business Lines' per-LOB Sales // table (see loadWeightedPipelineScheduledByMonth's comment). const salesPipelineByMonth = await loadWeightedPipelineScheduledByMonth(env, fiscalYear, asOf, {}); const forecast = computeSalesBlendedForecast(monthlyTotals, asOf, salesPipelineByMonth); // Sales Budget for the current month, company-wide, so pace can be // measured "on track to make budget" not just "on track to match its // own trailing average" (§49: Misty wants both comparisons available). // Sales Budgets is always annual÷12 here — there is no // sales_budgets_monthly table by design (§55, 2026-09-04: "sales // tables did not have monthly budgets... divide by 12 methodology for // sales to monitor momentum"). const budgetRows = await listBudgets(env, 'sales_budgets', fiscalYear); const companyMonthBudget = budgetRows.reduce((s, r) => s + (r.amount || 0), 0) / 12; const currentMonthTotal = (monthlyTotals.get(asOf.month) || 0); const pacePctOfBudget = companyMonthBudget > 0 ? (forecast.currentMonthBlended / companyMonthBudget) * 100 : null; const pacePctOfTrailingAvg = forecast.priorAvgPerMonth > 0 ? (forecast.currentMonthBlended / forecast.priorAvgPerMonth) * 100 : null; // Revenue Budget pace, company-wide, alongside the Sales figures above. // Prefers real monthly Revenue Budget entry for the current month when // it exists; falls back to annual÷12 only when no monthly rows have // been entered for this fiscal year at all. const revenueMonthlyTotals = await loadRevenueMonthlyBudgetTotals(env, fiscalYear); const revenueAnnualRows = await listRevenueBudgetsAnnualDerived(env, fiscalYear); const revenueAnnualTotal = revenueAnnualRows.reduce((s, r) => s + (r.amount || 0), 0); let companyMonthRevenueBudget = null; let revenueBudgetSource = 'none'; if (revenueMonthlyTotals && revenueMonthlyTotals.has(asOf.month)) { companyMonthRevenueBudget = revenueMonthlyTotals.get(asOf.month); revenueBudgetSource = 'monthly'; } else if (revenueAnnualTotal > 0) { companyMonthRevenueBudget = revenueAnnualTotal / 12; revenueBudgetSource = 'annual_divided'; } // Revenue ACTUALS/blended forecast, company-wide (2026-09-08, per // Misty's direct request: Month Pace should show "Sales Company // Blended Forecast" and "Revenue Company Blended Forecast" as two // real tile rows, not Sales-only with Revenue budget as a footnote). // Mirrors the Sales computation above exactly, against billed_fact // instead of bookings_fact — same computeBlendedForecast helper, same // asOf window (billed_fact.month is already a plain month column, no // AWARDED_MONTH_SQL translation needed the way bookings_fact needs). // revenueAvailable false only when there are zero billed_fact rows for // this fiscal year at all (matches the "explicit pending state, never // a fake number" convention used everywhere else Revenue actuals are // shown), same billedRows.length>0 check getRevenueBridge already uses. const revenueRows = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT month, SUM(amount) AS total FROM billed_fact WHERE fiscal_year = ? GROUP BY month` ).bind(fiscalYear).all()).results || []; const revenueAvailable = revenueRows.length > 0; let revenuePace = null; if (revenueAvailable) { const revenueMonthlyTotalsMap = new Map(); for (const r of revenueRows) revenueMonthlyTotalsMap.set(r.month, r.total || 0); // Backlog-blended (2026-09-10, per Misty's direct request): remaining // months use real scheduled Revenue Backlog where it exists, trailing // average only as fallback — see computeRevenueBlendedForecast. // Company-wide here (no regionId/lobKey), matching this function's // own scope. const revenueBacklogByMonth = await loadRevenueBacklogScheduledByMonth(env, fiscalYear, {}); const revenueForecast = computeRevenueBlendedForecast(revenueMonthlyTotalsMap, asOf, revenueBacklogByMonth); const revenueCurrentMonthTotal = (revenueMonthlyTotalsMap.get(asOf.month) || 0); const revenuePacePctOfBudget = companyMonthRevenueBudget > 0 ? (revenueForecast.currentMonthBlended / companyMonthRevenueBudget) * 100 : null; const revenuePacePctOfTrailingAvg = revenueForecast.priorAvgPerMonth > 0 ? (revenueForecast.currentMonthBlended / revenueForecast.priorAvgPerMonth) * 100 : null; revenuePace = { priorActualsSum: revenueForecast.priorActualsSum, priorAvgPerMonth: revenueForecast.priorAvgPerMonth, currentMonth: { label: MONTH_NAMES[asOf.month], actualsToDate: revenueCurrentMonthTotal, blended: revenueForecast.currentMonthBlended, bizDaysElapsed: revenueForecast.bizDaysElapsed, bizDaysRemaining: revenueForecast.bizDaysRemaining, totalBizDaysInMonth: revenueForecast.totalBizDaysInMonth }, // currentMonthActualsToDate/currentMonthBlended (2026-09-10 fix): // flat aliases of currentMonth.actualsToDate/.blended above, added // so getExecutiveSummary can use THIS object (real, unfiltered, // company-wide billed_fact — the same one Forecast's own "Revenue // Company Blended Forecast" tile already reads) directly as its own // revenuePace, instead of reconstructing a second, DIFFERENT company // total by summing getForecastRegionalDrivers' per-region Revenue // figures. That region-sum path filters billed_fact to // region_id IS NOT NULL (a region-scoped function's own correct // requirement — it can't attribute an unmapped row to a region), so // any billed_fact row whose raw region text never resolved through // region_raw_mappings was silently excluded from Executive Summary's // Revenue YTD while still being counted here on Forecast — caught // when Misty reported two different Revenue YTD numbers on two // screens (Forecast's $95.20M vs. Executive Summary's $89.05M) on // the same real data. Confirmed with Misty: company-wide totals // should include unmapped dollars rather than silently drop them; // per-region screens still can't attribute those dollars to a // specific region, a separate, real mapping gap (same class as the // still-unresolved "APEX" region on billed_line_item_fact). currentMonthActualsToDate: revenueCurrentMonthTotal, currentMonthBlended: revenueForecast.currentMonthBlended, pacePctOfBudget: revenuePacePctOfBudget, pacePctOfTrailingAvg: revenuePacePctOfTrailingAvg, totalYearForecast: revenueForecast.totalYearForecast, // remainingMonthsDetail (2026-09-10): per-month breakdown of what // fed the remaining-months projection, backlog vs. run-rate fallback // — surfaced so the frontend can show which months are backlog- // scheduled vs. trailing-average estimated, not folded silently // into one number. remainingMonthsDetail: revenueForecast.remainingMonthsDetail, }; } // Critical Actions (2026-09-08, per Misty's direct request): a short // list of concrete, mechanical flags computed from the real numbers // already on this screen, matching PULSE's existing "system flags, // human decides" pattern (e.g. Regions Below Budget Pace) rather than // AI-generated text. Each flag names a real threshold crossed, not a // vague warning. severity follows the same good/warn/crit vocabulary // paceClass already uses on the frontend, so rendering can reuse it // directly. const criticalActions = []; if (companyMonthBudget <= 0) { criticalActions.push({ id: 'no-sales-budget', severity: 'crit', text: `No Sales Budget set for FY${fiscalYear}. Sales pace can't be measured against budget until one is entered on Baseline.` }); } else if (pacePctOfBudget !== null && pacePctOfBudget < 95) { criticalActions.push({ id: 'sales-below-budget', severity: pacePctOfBudget < 80 ? 'crit' : 'warn', text: `Sales is pacing at ${pacePctOfBudget.toFixed(1)}% of this month's budget with ${forecast.bizDaysRemaining} business day${forecast.bizDaysRemaining === 1 ? '' : 's'} left in ${MONTH_NAMES[asOf.month]}.` }); } if (!revenueAvailable) { criticalActions.push({ id: 'no-revenue-data', severity: 'warn', text: `No Revenue actuals uploaded yet for FY${fiscalYear}. Upload a Billed Fact export on Data Updates to see Revenue pace.` }); } else if (companyMonthRevenueBudget === null || companyMonthRevenueBudget <= 0) { criticalActions.push({ id: 'no-revenue-budget', severity: 'crit', text: `No Revenue Budget set for FY${fiscalYear}. Revenue pace can't be measured against budget until one is entered on Baseline.` }); } else if (revenuePace && revenuePace.pacePctOfBudget !== null && revenuePace.pacePctOfBudget < 95) { criticalActions.push({ id: 'revenue-below-budget', severity: revenuePace.pacePctOfBudget < 80 ? 'crit' : 'warn', text: `Revenue is pacing at ${revenuePace.pacePctOfBudget.toFixed(1)}% of this month's budget with ${revenuePace.currentMonth.bizDaysRemaining} business day${revenuePace.currentMonth.bizDaysRemaining === 1 ? '' : 's'} left in ${MONTH_NAMES[asOf.month]}.` }); } return { fiscalYear, asOf, trailingWindowLabel: `Jan-${MONTH_NAMES[Math.max(asOf.month - 1, 1)]} avg`, priorActualsSum: forecast.priorActualsSum, priorAvgPerMonth: forecast.priorAvgPerMonth, currentMonth: { label: MONTH_NAMES[asOf.month], actualsToDate: currentMonthTotal, blended: forecast.currentMonthBlended, bizDaysElapsed: forecast.bizDaysElapsed, bizDaysRemaining: forecast.bizDaysRemaining, totalBizDaysInMonth: forecast.totalBizDaysInMonth }, companyMonthBudget, pacePctOfBudget, pacePctOfTrailingAvg, companyMonthRevenueBudget, revenueBudgetSource, totalYearForecast: forecast.totalYearForecast, // remainingMonthsDetail (2026-09-10): per-month breakdown of what fed // Sales' remaining-months projection, Weighted Pipeline vs. run-rate // fallback — mirrors Revenue's own remainingMonthsDetail below, same // shape, so the frontend's shared detail-table renderer works for both. remainingMonthsDetail: forecast.remainingMonthsDetail, revenueAvailable, revenuePace, criticalActions, }; } // ---- Forecast: Regional Drivers ---- // Per region: blended-forecast pace vs. its own trailing average AND vs. // its Sales Budget (§49: "I want both comparisons... I want to know if // we're on pace to make budget"). For any region below pace, a two-level // drill-down finds the real driver: which LOB (revenue_type) is furthest // below ITS OWN trailing average within that region, then which Service // Family within that LOB is furthest below ITS OWN trailing average. // Deliberately NOT a proportional split of company-wide LOB share — that // approach was tried and rejected (§49) because it mechanically always // names the biggest company-wide LOB as "the driver" everywhere, // regardless of what's actually happening in that specific region. // // Sales Budget's per-region monthBudget is always annual÷12 (Sales has // no monthly table, §55). The Sales-side pace comparison below (vs. this // region's own trailing average, vs. its Sales Budget month-equivalent) // is unchanged from before Worker batch A. // // Revenue Regional Drivers (§55, Worker batch A, 2026-09-04): each // region now also gets its Revenue Budget context — annual figure and a // month-equivalent that prefers real monthly `revenue_budgets_monthly` // entry (summed across that region's LOBs) and falls back to that // region's annual Revenue Budget÷12 only when no monthly rows exist for // this fiscal year at all, same preference order as Month Pace's // companyMonthRevenueBudget. This is budget context only, not a pace // comparison — there is still no Revenue-actuals fact table (no // `billed_fact` source; confirmed both in the continuity brief §48/§51 // and in the frontend's own "real Jan-through-today Revenue needs its // own billed-data upload, which hasn't been provided yet" copy already // shipped on this screen). So every region's Revenue figures come back // with `revenueActualsAvailable: false` and no pace percentage — the // frontend should show Revenue Budget as reference numbers, never as a // pace bar, until a real Revenue actuals source exists. // // §55 Worker batch B: uses listRegions(env) with its new active-only // default, so a merged-away region correctly drops out of this screen — // its historical rows are still queryable (they were reassigned to the // merge target, not deleted), so nothing here needed to change beyond // picking up the new default. // Shared per-region pace/shortfall computation, used identically for the // Sales basis (bookings_fact, grouped by service_family) and the Revenue // basis (billed_fact, grouped by revenue_subtype in place of // service_family — billed_fact has no service_family column; §Regional // Drivers Revenue build, confirmed revenue_subtype is a meaningful, // comparable second-level grouping in the real data). `rows` is this // region's already-filtered fact rows; each row must carry // { revenue_type, groupKey, month, total }, where groupKey is // service_family for Sales or revenue_subtype for Revenue. function computeRegionDriverForScope(rows, asOf, annualBudget) { const monthlyTotals = new Map(); for (const r of rows) monthlyTotals.set(r.month, (monthlyTotals.get(r.month) || 0) + (r.total || 0)); const forecast = computeBlendedForecast(monthlyTotals, asOf); const monthBudget = annualBudget / 12; const pacePctOfBudget = monthBudget > 0 ? (forecast.currentMonthBlended / monthBudget) * 100 : null; const pacePctOfTrailingAvg = forecast.priorAvgPerMonth > 0 ? (forecast.currentMonthBlended / forecast.priorAvgPerMonth) * 100 : null; const dollarVsAvgMonth = forecast.currentMonthBlended - forecast.priorAvgPerMonth; // dollarVsBudget (2026-09-11, Open Items 1(h)/1(i)/2(d)): Misty wants // dollars, not percentages, for the vs.-Budget column on Regions Below // Pace (both Sales and Revenue cards) and Forecast's Regional Drivers — // all three read off this same shared function (buildRegionDriverRow on // the frontend), so one new field here fixes all three at once. null // (never a fake $0) when there's no budget entered at all, matching // pacePctOfBudget's own null convention just above. const dollarVsBudget = monthBudget > 0 ? (forecast.currentMonthBlended - monthBudget) : null; // Shortfall drill-down only computed when the scope is actually behind // its own trailing average (vs.-budget basis not used here per §49: // real per-LOB budgets don't exist yet, so "Use vs. trailing average // for now" was the explicit, confirmed choice; carried over unchanged // for Revenue). let shortfallLob = null, shortfallGroup = null; if (pacePctOfTrailingAvg !== null && pacePctOfTrailingAvg < 100) { const byLob = {}; for (const r of rows) { const lob = mapRevenueTypeToLob(r.revenue_type); if (!lob) continue; if (!byLob[lob]) byLob[lob] = new Map(); byLob[lob].set(r.month, (byLob[lob].get(r.month) || 0) + (r.total || 0)); } let worstLob = null, worstLobGap = 0; for (const lobKey of Object.keys(byLob)) { const lobForecast = computeBlendedForecast(byLob[lobKey], asOf); const gap = lobForecast.currentMonthBlended - lobForecast.priorAvgPerMonth; if (gap < worstLobGap) { worstLobGap = gap; worstLob = lobKey; } } if (worstLob) { shortfallLob = { lob: worstLob, label: LOB_LABELS[worstLob], dollarGap: worstLobGap }; // Second level: within worstLob, by groupKey (Service Family for // Sales, revenue_subtype for Revenue — §49 correction for Sales; // same grain choice extended to Revenue). const byGroup = {}; for (const r of rows) { if (mapRevenueTypeToLob(r.revenue_type) !== worstLob) continue; const key = r.groupKey || 'Unspecified'; if (!byGroup[key]) byGroup[key] = new Map(); byGroup[key].set(r.month, (byGroup[key].get(r.month) || 0) + (r.total || 0)); } let worstGroup = null, worstGroupGap = 0; for (const groupKey of Object.keys(byGroup)) { const groupForecast = computeBlendedForecast(byGroup[groupKey], asOf); const gap = groupForecast.currentMonthBlended - groupForecast.priorAvgPerMonth; if (gap < worstGroupGap) { worstGroupGap = gap; worstGroup = groupKey; } } if (worstGroup) { shortfallGroup = { groupLabel: worstGroup, dollarGap: worstGroupGap }; } } } return { currentMonthBlended: forecast.currentMonthBlended, priorAvgPerMonth: forecast.priorAvgPerMonth, // priorActualsSum/currentMonthActualsToDate (2026-09-09 fix): these // were missing from this return object even though computeBlendedForecast // (via `forecast` above) always computes them — getExecutiveSummary's // company-wide Revenue YTD tile reads r.revenue.priorActualsSum and // r.revenue.currentMonth.actualsToDate expecting this shape, silently // got undefined -> 0 on both, so Revenue YTD rendered $0 regardless of // real revenue. Adding them here fixes every caller of this function // (getForecastRegionalDrivers's sales/revenue scopes both), not just // Executive Summary's read of it. priorActualsSum: forecast.priorActualsSum, currentMonthActualsToDate: forecast.currentMonthActuals, dollarVsAvgMonth, pacePctOfTrailingAvg, annualBudget, monthBudget, pacePctOfBudget, dollarVsBudget, totalYearForecast: forecast.totalYearForecast, shortfallLob, shortfallGroup, // YTD vs. YTD-budget-to-date (2026-09-09, Top Performing Region YTD // ranking, Misty's confirmed basis). Budget-to-date = monthBudget for // each fully-elapsed prior month, plus monthBudget prorated by the // fraction of this month's business days that have elapsed so far — // same flat annual/12 monthBudget already used everywhere else in this // app, just accumulated over the year-to-date window instead of one // month. ytdActual is real actuals only (no blended/projected portion), // matching the existing Sales YTD / Revenue YTD tiles' own definition. ytdActual: forecast.priorActualsSum + forecast.currentMonthActuals, ytdBudgetToDate: monthBudget > 0 ? monthBudget * forecast.priorMonths.length + monthBudget * (forecast.totalBizDaysInMonth > 0 ? forecast.bizDaysElapsed / forecast.totalBizDaysInMonth : 0) : 0, pacePctOfYtdBudget: (monthBudget > 0 && (forecast.priorMonths.length > 0 || forecast.bizDaysElapsed > 0)) ? ((forecast.priorActualsSum + forecast.currentMonthActuals) / (monthBudget * forecast.priorMonths.length + monthBudget * (forecast.totalBizDaysInMonth > 0 ? forecast.bizDaysElapsed / forecast.totalBizDaysInMonth : 0))) * 100 : null, }; } async function getForecastRegionalDrivers(env, fiscalYear) { const asOf = await resolveForecastAsOf(env); const regions = await listRegions(env); const bookingRows = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT region_id, revenue_type, service_family, ${AWARDED_MONTH_SQL} AS month, SUM(total_price) AS total FROM bookings_fact WHERE ${AWARDED_YEAR_SQL} = ? AND awarded_date IS NOT NULL AND awarded_date != '' AND region_id IS NOT NULL GROUP BY region_id, revenue_type, service_family, month` ).bind(fiscalYear).all()).results || []; // Revenue basis (2026-09-05): real, from billed_fact, mirroring Sales // exactly via computeRegionDriverForScope above — same asOf window as // Sales (bookings_fact's own MAX(awarded_date), per the established // precedent in getForecastBusinessLines, so the two bases stay // comparable at the same point in the year) rather than a separate // billed_fact-derived as-of. groupKey here is revenue_subtype, standing // in for Sales' service_family (billed_fact has no service_family // column). const billedRows = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT region_id, revenue_type, revenue_subtype, month, SUM(amount) AS total FROM billed_fact WHERE fiscal_year = ? AND region_id IS NOT NULL GROUP BY region_id, revenue_type, revenue_subtype, month` ).bind(fiscalYear).all()).results || []; const regionsWithRevenue = new Set(billedRows.map(r => r.region_id)); const budgetRows = await listBudgets(env, 'sales_budgets', fiscalYear); const budgetByRegion = new Map(); // region_id -> annual total across LOBs for (const b of budgetRows) { budgetByRegion.set(b.region_id, (budgetByRegion.get(b.region_id) || 0) + (b.amount || 0)); } // Revenue Budget: annual totals always available; monthly totals only // where entered (revenueMonthlyByRegion holds a region's // month->dollars map only if at least one monthly row exists for that // region this fiscal year — absence, not a $0 entry, is what triggers // the annual÷12 fallback below). Used both as the Revenue panel's own // budget comparison and, unchanged, as Sales' Revenue-Budget reference // note. const revenueAnnualRows = await listRevenueBudgetsAnnualDerived(env, fiscalYear); const revenueAnnualByRegion = new Map(); // region_id -> annual total across LOBs for (const b of revenueAnnualRows) { revenueAnnualByRegion.set(b.region_id, (revenueAnnualByRegion.get(b.region_id) || 0) + (b.amount || 0)); } const revenueMonthlyRows = await listRevenueBudgetsMonthly(env, fiscalYear); const revenueMonthlyByRegion = new Map(); // region_id -> Map (summed across LOBs) for (const r of revenueMonthlyRows) { if (!revenueMonthlyByRegion.has(r.region_id)) revenueMonthlyByRegion.set(r.region_id, new Map()); const monthMap = revenueMonthlyByRegion.get(r.region_id); monthMap.set(r.month, (monthMap.get(r.month) || 0) + (r.amount || 0)); } const results = regions.map((region) => { const regionBookingRows = bookingRows.filter(r => r.region_id === region.id) .map(r => ({ revenue_type: r.revenue_type, groupKey: r.service_family, month: r.month, total: r.total })); const annualBudget = budgetByRegion.get(region.id) || 0; const sales = computeRegionDriverForScope(regionBookingRows, asOf, annualBudget); const revenueAnnualBudget = revenueAnnualByRegion.get(region.id) || 0; const regionRevenueMonthly = revenueMonthlyByRegion.get(region.id); let revenueMonthBudget = null; let revenueBudgetSource = 'none'; if (regionRevenueMonthly && regionRevenueMonthly.has(asOf.month)) { revenueMonthBudget = regionRevenueMonthly.get(asOf.month); revenueBudgetSource = 'monthly'; } else if (revenueAnnualBudget > 0) { revenueMonthBudget = revenueAnnualBudget / 12; revenueBudgetSource = 'annual_divided'; } const revenueActualsAvailable = regionsWithRevenue.has(region.id); let revenue = null; if (revenueActualsAvailable) { const regionBilledRows = billedRows.filter(r => r.region_id === region.id) .map(r => ({ revenue_type: r.revenue_type, groupKey: r.revenue_subtype, month: r.month, total: r.total })); revenue = computeRegionDriverForScope(regionBilledRows, asOf, revenueAnnualBudget); } return { regionId: region.id, regionName: region.name, sales, revenue, revenueActualsAvailable, revenueAnnualBudget, revenueMonthBudget, revenueBudgetSource, // Flattened aliases kept for the Sales panel's existing field names // (regionName + these) so the frontend's Sales rendering needs no // changes; the Revenue panel reads from `revenue.*` instead. currentMonthBlended: sales.currentMonthBlended, priorAvgPerMonth: sales.priorAvgPerMonth, dollarVsAvgMonth: sales.dollarVsAvgMonth, pacePctOfTrailingAvg: sales.pacePctOfTrailingAvg, annualBudget: sales.annualBudget, monthBudget: sales.monthBudget, pacePctOfBudget: sales.pacePctOfBudget, pacePctOfYtdBudget: sales.pacePctOfYtdBudget, totalYearForecast: sales.totalYearForecast, shortfallLob: sales.shortfallLob, shortfallServiceFamily: sales.shortfallGroup ? { serviceFamily: sales.shortfallGroup.groupLabel, dollarGap: sales.shortfallGroup.dollarGap } : null, }; }); // Sorted furthest-below-pace first on the Sales basis (by trailing-avg // pct, matching the confirmed mockup layout, §49) — both panels share // this one region order so a region stays in the same row position // whether you're looking at its Sales or Revenue figures. results.sort((a, b) => { const pa = a.pacePctOfTrailingAvg === null ? Infinity : a.pacePctOfTrailingAvg; const pb = b.pacePctOfTrailingAvg === null ? Infinity : b.pacePctOfTrailingAvg; return pa - pb; }); return { fiscalYear, asOf, regions: results, revenueAvailable: regionsWithRevenue.size > 0 }; } // Company-wide Revenue Trend (2026-09-10, per Misty's direct request: // "show on the regions and company wide trends and averages for almost // 3 years"). Regions already had this (see the revenueTrend block in // getRegionsScreen); this is the same real query and the same shared // computeRevenueTrendWithTrailingAvg methodology, just summed across all // regions instead of filtered to one — billed_fact is confirmed the // region/LOB source of truth (Misty directly: "Billed Fact will be the // region and LOB source of truth"), no region_id filter needed here. async function getCompanyRevenueTrend(env, fiscalYear) { const priorYear = fiscalYear - 1; const revenueYears = [priorYear - 1, priorYear, fiscalYear]; const revenueRows = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT fiscal_year, revenue_type, SUM(amount) AS total FROM billed_fact WHERE fiscal_year IN (?, ?, ?) GROUP BY fiscal_year, revenue_type` ).bind(...revenueYears).all()).results || []; const revenueByYear = new Map(); for (const yr of revenueYears) revenueByYear.set(yr, { program_advisory: 0, design_services: 0, inspections: 0, total: 0 }); let revenueUnmapped = 0; for (const r of revenueRows) { const lob = mapRevenueTypeToLob(r.revenue_type); const bucket = revenueByYear.get(r.fiscal_year); if (!bucket) continue; if (lob) { bucket[lob] += r.total || 0; bucket.total += r.total || 0; } else revenueUnmapped += r.total || 0; } const revenueAvailable = revenueRows.length > 0; const trend = computeRevenueTrendWithTrailingAvg(revenueByYear, revenueYears); return { fiscalYear, revenueYears, available: revenueAvailable, unmapped: revenueUnmapped, trend }; } /* ============================================================ COMPANY-WIDE GROWTH TARGET PLAN (2026-09-10), per Misty's direct request on Growth Strategy > Company-Wide: "let's make it flat growth in revenue, but the tool should tell me how much topline growth I'll need [in Sales] based on historical data to achieve that growth and how each LOB and Region needs to do." Three real, grounded pieces, each confirmed with Misty before writing this: 1. The flat company-wide REVENUE growth % is a stored planning input (company_revenue_growth_targets), same shape/independence-from-lock as region_growth_targets. 2. Required SALES (bookings) growth is backsolved from that Revenue target using a real 3-year (2024-2026) average Sales-to-Revenue ratio: total bookings_fact.total_price divided by total billed_fact.amount across the same 3-year window getCompanyRevenueTrend already uses, both real, both already- uploaded data, no invented conversion factor. Confirmed with Misty: 3-year average, not most-recent-year-only, to smooth one unusually big or slow year. 3. The by-LOB and by-Region breakdown is APPORTIONED by each one's own historical trend (confirmed with Misty: not a flat % applied identically to every LOB/Region) — each entity's own trailing YoY growth rate (bookings, prior year -> current year) is compared to the company-wide average trailing YoY rate, and that relative strength scales its share of the flat target so the dollar-weighted average across all entities still nets out to exactly the flat target. A LOB/Region with no usable prior-year base (yoyPct null) falls back to the flat target itself, never a divide-by-zero or a fabricated trend. ============================================================ */ // Company Backlog + YTD Revenue vs. Revenue Budget, current fiscal year. // Pulled into a shared helper (2026-09-10) so Growth Target's "vs. YTD // Actual + Backlog" / "vs. Revenue Budget" comparisons read the exact same // real figures as Executive Summary's own Company Backlog tile, instead of // a second parallel derivation -- the same bug class as the earlier // Revenue YTD discrepancy between Executive Summary and Forecast, which // Misty caught directly by comparing two real screens. Caller supplies // revenueAvailable/revenuePace (from getForecastMonthPace) rather than this // helper re-fetching them, since both current callers already have that // object in hand and a second getForecastMonthPace call per request is // wasted work. async function getCompanyBacklogCurrentYear(env, fiscalYear, revenueAvailable, revenuePace) { const companyBacklogRow = await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT SUM(contract_balance) AS total FROM backlog_fact WHERE contract_balance IS NOT NULL` ).first(); const companyBacklog = (companyBacklogRow && companyBacklogRow.total) || 0; const companyBacklogCurrentYearRow = await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT SUM(contract_balance) AS total FROM backlog_fact WHERE contract_balance IS NOT NULL AND end_date IS NOT NULL AND end_date != '' AND ${END_YEAR_SQL} = ?` ).bind(fiscalYear).first(); const companyBacklogThisYear = (companyBacklogCurrentYearRow && companyBacklogCurrentYearRow.total) || 0; const revenueAnnualRowsForBacklog = await listRevenueBudgetsAnnualDerived(env, fiscalYear); const companyRevenueBudget = revenueAnnualRowsForBacklog.reduce((s, r) => s + (r.amount || 0), 0); const revenueYtdActual = revenueAvailable && revenuePace ? (revenuePace.priorActualsSum || 0) + (revenuePace.currentMonthActualsToDate || 0) : null; const backlogPlusYtd = revenueYtdActual !== null ? revenueYtdActual + companyBacklogThisYear : null; return { backlogThisYear: companyBacklogThisYear, backlogTotal: companyBacklog, revenueYtdActual, backlogPlusYtd, revenueBudget: companyRevenueBudget > 0 ? companyRevenueBudget : null, gapToRevenueBudget: (companyRevenueBudget > 0 && backlogPlusYtd !== null) ? (backlogPlusYtd - companyRevenueBudget) : null, }; } // mode ('this_year' | 'next_year', default 'next_year') added 2026-09-10 // per Misty's direct request for a year picker: "confirm is this current // year growth or is this next year growth. Originally I was thinking next // year growth." The card originally always used the selected fiscal year's // OWN (partial-year) actual as the growth base while labeling the result // as if it were also that same year's target -- structurally already // "next year" math (grow off the selected year's actual) under a // misleading "This Year" label. Default is 'next_year' so already-saved // growth_pct rows and everything Misty has already seen on this card keep // their real meaning; 'this_year' is the new, explicit alternative. // // this_year: targetYear = selectedFiscalYear, baseYear = targetYear - 1 // next_year: targetYear = selectedFiscalYear + 1, baseYear = targetYear - 1 (= selectedFiscalYear) // // baseYear is always "the most recent year with real actuals" in both // modes, so the 3-year Sales-to-Revenue ratio window and the by-LOB/by- // Region apportionment (both trailing-trend calculations) anchor off // baseYear rather than off selectedFiscalYear directly -- this is what // makes This Year and Next Year share one consistent methodology instead // of two different ones. // // The stored growth_pct row is keyed by TARGET year (company_revenue_ // growth_targets.fiscal_year = targetYear) -- "the year this % target is // for" needs no schema change under either mode, and switching modes on // the same selected year naturally shows/edits two independent planning // inputs (e.g. a real FY2026 target and a separate real FY2027 target), // which is the correct behavior, not a bug. // // Target vs. Reality comparisons (2026-09-10, per Misty's direct request // -- "growth on top of where I think we can land," explicitly not vs. // plain YTD Actual alone). First shipped checking TARGET year's Budget/ // Backlog, corrected same day per Misty's direct catch: "If the toggle is // next year, everything should be based on 2026" -- real YTD actuals, // Backlog and Budget only exist for the year that's actually happening // right now (selectedFiscalYear), never the future year a next_year // target is being solved for, which typically has no budget entered yet. // All three comparisons below -- vsYtdPlusBacklog, vsRevenueBudget, // vsSalesBudget -- always check selectedFiscalYear, in both modes. In // this_year mode selectedFiscalYear === targetYear already, so nothing // changes there; next_year mode is the real fix. Only the two headline // figures (revenueTargetAmount, requiredSalesAmount) are actually FOR // targetYear -- everything they're compared against is real, current, // selectedFiscalYear data. // basis: 'budget' (default, unchanged historical behavior — baseYear's // full real actual, from billed_fact/bookings_fact) or 'actual_run_rate' // (Open Items 3(a), 2026-09-11: "let me pick, Growth on Budget or // Actual + Run Rate... accommodate a scenario where I've hit budget by // Q4, so maybe I want to set a new growth target for the current year"). // "Budget" here means "the year's own real total" (not a budget-table // figure — no such distinct concept existed before this item; kept the // label Misty used), and Actual + Run Rate substitutes baseYear's // YTD-actual-plus-trailing-run-rate blended total wherever baseYear's // full actual would otherwise be used as the starting point, so a // mid-year reset can target off where the year is actually trending // rather than waiting for the year to close. Only meaningful for // baseYear === the real "as of" year (the year that's actually in // progress right now) — computeRevenueBlendedForecast/ // computeSalesBlendedForecast blend real remaining-month data against // TODAY's date, so this basis falls back to the full-actual figure for // any other baseYear (a closed-out prior year has no "remaining months" // left to blend), with runRateApplied:false telling the frontend why. async function getGrowthTargetPlan(env, selectedFiscalYear, mode, basis) { const resolvedMode = (mode === 'this_year') ? 'this_year' : 'next_year'; const resolvedBasis = (basis === 'actual_run_rate') ? 'actual_run_rate' : 'budget'; const targetYear = resolvedMode === 'this_year' ? selectedFiscalYear : selectedFiscalYear + 1; const baseYear = targetYear - 1; const threeYears = [baseYear - 2, baseYear - 1, baseYear]; const storedTargetPct = await getCompanyRevenueGrowthTarget(env, targetYear); const targetPct = storedTargetPct !== null ? storedTargetPct : 0; // ---- Real 3-year Revenue (billed_fact) and Sales (bookings_fact) // totals, company-wide, ending at baseYear. Revenue reuses // getCompanyRevenueTrend (already built, already correct) rather than // re-querying billed_fact a second time. const revenueTrend = await getCompanyRevenueTrend(env, baseYear); const revenueAvailable = revenueTrend.available; const revenue3yrTotal = revenueTrend.trend.reduce((s, t) => s + (t.total || 0), 0); const revenueCurrentActual = revenueTrend.trend[revenueTrend.trend.length - 1]?.total || 0; const revenuePriorActual = revenueTrend.trend[revenueTrend.trend.length - 2]?.total || 0; // Historical YoY recommendation for the flat-growth slider (Open Items // 3(b), 2026-09-11: "keep the slider... make a recommendation based on // historical performance"). Real company-wide Revenue YoY, baseYear // actual vs. baseYear-1 actual — the same trailing-year comparison // already shown elsewhere in the app (Revenue Trend's own YoY column), // not a separate invented formula. Null when there's no real prior-year // total to compare against yet. const historicalYoyPct = revenuePriorActual > 0 ? Math.round(((revenueCurrentActual - revenuePriorActual) / revenuePriorActual) * 1000) / 10 : null; const bookingsRows = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT ${AWARDED_YEAR_SQL} AS yr, SUM(total_price) AS total FROM bookings_fact WHERE awarded_date IS NOT NULL AND awarded_date != '' AND ${AWARDED_YEAR_SQL} IN (?, ?, ?) GROUP BY yr` ).bind(...threeYears).all()).results || []; const salesByYear = new Map(threeYears.map(y => [y, 0])); for (const r of bookingsRows) if (salesByYear.has(r.yr)) salesByYear.set(r.yr, r.total || 0); const sales3yrTotal = [...salesByYear.values()].reduce((s, v) => s + v, 0); const salesCurrentActual = salesByYear.get(baseYear) || 0; const salesAvailable = bookingsRows.length > 0; // Actual + Run Rate substitution: reuses the exact same blended-forecast // machinery Forecast's own Month Pace tiles use (getForecastMonthPace), // rather than a second, different run-rate formula. let runRateApplied = false; let revenueCurrent = revenueCurrentActual; let salesCurrent = salesCurrentActual; if (resolvedBasis === 'actual_run_rate') { const asOf = await resolveForecastAsOf(env); if (asOf.year === baseYear) { // Same real blended-forecast figures Forecast's own Month Pace tiles // show (getForecastMonthPace): totalYearForecast is Sales' own // YTD-actual + Weighted-Pipeline/trailing-run-rate blended full-year // total; revenuePace.totalYearForecast is Revenue's equivalent. const monthPace = await getForecastMonthPace(env, baseYear); salesCurrent = monthPace.totalYearForecast; if (revenueAvailable && monthPace.revenuePace) { revenueCurrent = monthPace.revenuePace.totalYearForecast; } runRateApplied = true; } } // ---- Backsolve: 3-year avg Sales-to-Revenue ratio applied to the // Revenue target, to get the required Sales dollar figure and its // implied growth % over this year's actual Sales.
