Null (never a fake // 0 or Infinity) when either side has no real data yet. const salesToRevenueRatio = (revenueAvailable && salesAvailable && revenue3yrTotal > 0) ? sales3yrTotal / revenue3yrTotal : null; const revenueTargetAmount = revenueCurrent * (1 + targetPct / 100); const requiredSalesAmount = salesToRevenueRatio !== null ? revenueTargetAmount * salesToRevenueRatio : null; const requiredSalesGrowthPct = (requiredSalesAmount !== null && salesCurrent > 0) ? ((requiredSalesAmount - salesCurrent) / salesCurrent) * 100 : null; // ---- By-LOB breakdown, apportioned by each LOB's own historical // trailing YoY (baseYear-1 -> baseYear), scaled so the dollar-weighted // average across LOBs still equals targetPct exactly. function apportionByTrend(entities) { // entities: [{ key, label, priorAmount, currentAmount }] const withYoy = entities.map(e => ({ ...e, yoyPct: e.priorAmount > 0 ? ((e.currentAmount - e.priorAmount) / e.priorAmount) * 100 : null, })); const companyPrior = withYoy.reduce((s, e) => s + e.priorAmount, 0); const companyCurrent = withYoy.reduce((s, e) => s + e.currentAmount, 0); const companyYoyPct = companyPrior > 0 ? ((companyCurrent - companyPrior) / companyPrior) * 100 : null; // Relative strength = this entity's own YoY vs. the company average // YoY, shifted so relative strength is always positive (avoids // negative-weight nonsense when an entity or the company shrank), // entities with no usable prior-year base get weight 1 (parity), so // they land exactly on the flat target rather than skewing the scale. const SHIFT = 100; // shifts any realistic YoY% (clamped range) into positive territory const weights = withYoy.map(e => { if (e.yoyPct === null || companyYoyPct === null) return 1; return Math.max(0.1, (e.yoyPct + SHIFT) / (companyYoyPct + SHIFT)); }); // Scale factor so the CURRENT-dollar-weighted average of (weight * // targetPct) across entities equals targetPct exactly, i.e. the // apportioned targets still net out to the flat company-wide target. const weightedCurrentSum = withYoy.reduce((s, e, i) => s + e.currentAmount * weights[i], 0); const scale = weightedCurrentSum > 0 ? companyCurrent / weightedCurrentSum : 1; return withYoy.map((e, i) => { const apportionedPct = targetPct * weights[i] * scale; const targetAmount = e.currentAmount * (1 + apportionedPct / 100); return { key: e.key, label: e.label, currentAmount: e.currentAmount, yoyPct: e.yoyPct, apportionedGrowthPct: Math.round(apportionedPct * 10) / 10, targetAmount, }; }); } // By-LOB: current = baseYear's Revenue (billed_fact) by LOB, prior = // baseYear-1's, same lobKeys/labels as everywhere else in the app // (VALID_LOBS/LOB_LABELS), Revenue-basis since the target itself is a // Revenue target. Apportioned targetAmount here lands in targetYear. const revByLobCurrent = revenueTrend.trend[revenueTrend.trend.length - 1]?.byLob || {}; const revByLobPrior = revenueTrend.trend[revenueTrend.trend.length - 2]?.byLob || {}; // Backlog attached to both breakdowns below (Open Items 3(d)/3(e), // 2026-09-11: "let's show what's in the backlog" for both By Line of // Business and By Region). Same real query shape getRegionsScreen // already uses for its own "Backlog by Line of Business" card // (backlog_fact.contract_balance, grouped by revenue_type -> LOB, or by // region_id, filtered to end_date's year), just company-wide/all-region // here instead of one region, and keyed to targetYear (this card's own // "next year" framing) rather than baseYear + 1 as a separate constant. const backlogByLobRows = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT revenue_type, SUM(contract_balance) AS total FROM backlog_fact WHERE end_date IS NOT NULL AND end_date != '' AND ${END_YEAR_SQL} = ? AND contract_balance IS NOT NULL GROUP BY revenue_type` ).bind(targetYear).all()).results || []; const backlogByLob = {}; for (const lobKey of VALID_LOBS) backlogByLob[lobKey] = 0; for (const r of backlogByLobRows) { const lob = mapRevenueTypeToLob(r.revenue_type); if (lob) backlogByLob[lob] += r.total || 0; } const backlogByRegionRows = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT region_id, SUM(contract_balance) AS total FROM backlog_fact WHERE end_date IS NOT NULL AND end_date != '' AND ${END_YEAR_SQL} = ? AND contract_balance IS NOT NULL AND region_id IS NOT NULL GROUP BY region_id` ).bind(targetYear).all()).results || []; const backlogByRegion = new Map(backlogByRegionRows.map(r => [r.region_id, r.total || 0])); const byLob = revenueAvailable ? apportionByTrend(VALID_LOBS.map(lobKey => ({ key: lobKey, label: LOB_LABELS[lobKey], priorAmount: revByLobPrior[lobKey] || 0, currentAmount: revByLobCurrent[lobKey] || 0, }))).map(row => ({ ...row, backlogNext: backlogByLob[row.key] || 0 })) : []; // By-Region: Revenue (billed_fact) by region, baseYear-1 vs. baseYear, // same real region_id join every other region-scoped billed_fact read // in this file uses. let byRegion = []; if (revenueAvailable) { const regions = await listRegions(env); const regionRevRows = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT region_id, fiscal_year, SUM(amount) AS total FROM billed_fact WHERE fiscal_year IN (?, ?) AND region_id IS NOT NULL GROUP BY region_id, fiscal_year` ).bind(baseYear - 1, baseYear).all()).results || []; const byRegionYear = new Map(); // region_id -> {prior, current} for (const r of regionRevRows) { if (!byRegionYear.has(r.region_id)) byRegionYear.set(r.region_id, { prior: 0, current: 0 }); const bucket = byRegionYear.get(r.region_id); if (r.fiscal_year === baseYear - 1) bucket.prior = r.total || 0; if (r.fiscal_year === baseYear) bucket.current = r.total || 0; } byRegion = apportionByTrend(regions.map(region => { const d = byRegionYear.get(region.id) || { prior: 0, current: 0 }; return { key: region.id, label: region.name, priorAmount: d.prior, currentAmount: d.current }; })).map(row => ({ ...row, backlogNext: backlogByRegion.get(row.key) || 0 })); } // ---- Target vs. Reality: Revenue (YTD Actual + Backlog, Revenue // Budget) and Sales (Sales Budget). Fixed 2026-09-10, same day as first // shipped -- these all originally checked TARGET year's budget/backlog // (so Next Year mode checked FY2027's, before any FY2027 budget has // been entered), which Misty caught immediately: "If the toggle is // next year, everything should be based on 2026." Real budgets/backlog/ // YTD actuals only exist for the year that's actually happening right // now -- selectedFiscalYear (the section's own Year picker, e.g. 2026), // regardless of which year the growth % target is being solved FOR. // In this_year mode selectedFiscalYear === targetYear already, so this // is a no-op there; in next_year mode it's the real fix -- both modes // now share one reality-check basis, always selectedFiscalYear, so // there's no more separate "backlog only, target year hasn't started" // branch to fall back to. const monthPaceForReality = await getForecastMonthPace(env, selectedFiscalYear); const backlogYtd = await getCompanyBacklogCurrentYear(env, selectedFiscalYear, monthPaceForReality.revenueAvailable, monthPaceForReality.revenuePace); const vsYtdPlusBacklog = backlogYtd.backlogPlusYtd !== null ? { amount: backlogYtd.backlogPlusYtd, revenueYtdActual: backlogYtd.revenueYtdActual, backlogThisYear: backlogYtd.backlogThisYear, gapToTarget: revenueTargetAmount - backlogYtd.backlogPlusYtd, gapPct: backlogYtd.backlogPlusYtd > 0 ? ((revenueTargetAmount - backlogYtd.backlogPlusYtd) / backlogYtd.backlogPlusYtd) * 100 : null, } : null; const revenueBudgetRowsForTarget = await listRevenueBudgetsAnnualDerived(env, selectedFiscalYear); const revenueBudgetForTarget = revenueBudgetRowsForTarget.reduce((s, r) => s + (r.amount || 0), 0); const vsRevenueBudget = revenueBudgetForTarget > 0 ? { amount: revenueBudgetForTarget, gapToTarget: revenueTargetAmount - revenueBudgetForTarget, gapPct: ((revenueTargetAmount - revenueBudgetForTarget) / revenueBudgetForTarget) * 100, } : null; const salesBudgetRowsForTarget = await listBudgets(env, 'sales_budgets', selectedFiscalYear); const salesBudgetForTarget = salesBudgetRowsForTarget.reduce((s, r) => s + (r.amount || 0), 0); const vsSalesBudget = (salesBudgetForTarget > 0 && requiredSalesAmount !== null) ? { amount: salesBudgetForTarget, gapToTarget: requiredSalesAmount - salesBudgetForTarget, gapPct: ((requiredSalesAmount - salesBudgetForTarget) / salesBudgetForTarget) * 100, } : null; return { selectedFiscalYear, mode: resolvedMode, basis: resolvedBasis, runRateApplied, historicalYoyPct, targetYear, baseYear, targetPct, revenueAvailable, revenueCurrent, revenueCurrentActual, revenueTargetAmount, salesAvailable, salesCurrent, salesCurrentActual, salesToRevenueRatio, requiredSalesAmount, requiredSalesGrowthPct, vsYtdPlusBacklog, vsRevenueBudget, vsSalesBudget, byLob, byRegion, }; } // A single request body is capped well under Cloudflare Workers' own // request-size ceiling — the browser sends rows in multiple sequential // requests (one per upload_batch, chunked client-side) rather than one // giant POST, so this is a per-request sanity cap, not the real limit. const MAX_ROWS_PER_REQUEST = 5000; async function createUploadBatch(env, { uploadType, sourceFilename }, actorUserId) { if (!VALID_UPLOAD_TYPES.has(uploadType)) return { error: 'Invalid upload type.', status: 400 }; const id = randomToken(16); await env.DB.prepare( `INSERT INTO upload_batches (id, upload_type, uploaded_by, source_filename, row_count) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, 0)` ).bind(id, uploadType, actorUserId, sourceFilename || null).run(); return { ok: true, uploadBatchId: id }; } // Saves one chunk of rows to an existing upload_batch (the browser may // call this multiple times for one large file, per MAX_ROWS_PER_REQUEST). // Each call inserts BATCH_INSERT_SIZE rows per D1 batch() round-trip and // bumps upload_batches.row_count by however many rows this call added. // // Full-table REPLACE, not append (2026-09-08, Misty, confirmed live: two // Bookings uploads with only ~320 rows apart were both fully counted, // doubling every company-wide total to ~1.97x real — bookings_fact had // been pure append-only since 2026-09-04 on the stated assumption that // each Salesforce export was "genuinely new activity, nothing ever re- // reported"; the real exports are full current-state extracts re- // uploaded periodically, so append duplicated every row still present // in both files). Misty's direct call: "This should be a replace not an // add. Same for all the other files as well" — so this now applies to // all four of bookings/backlog/proposal_pipeline/proposal_volume, a full // DELETE of that table (not scoped to a fiscal year the way // replaceBilledFactForYear is — these exports aren't uploaded one year // at a time) followed by inserting the new file's rows. // // The delete only needs to run once per upload, not once per chunk — it // runs on the first chunk only, detected via upload_batches.row_count // still being 0 (set at creation in createUploadBatch, only ever // incremented after a chunk's insert below). The frontend sends chunks // sequentially and awaits each one before sending the next (never in // parallel), so this is a reliable signal, not a race condition. // Backlog History / Pull-Through (2026-09-12, per Misty's direct // request: "there was a ton of historical data that I think we need to // archive... to help with averages and trends... specifically thinking // the backlog pull through"). backlog_fact has never had any historical // snapshot mechanism — saveUploadRows' DELETE FROM below is a full, // unscoped replace on every Backlog upload, with nothing preserved of // the prior state. This captures one BEFORE that delete runs, so every // future Backlog upload automatically becomes a real historical data // point with no separate manual step. // // Snapshot grain is (snapshot_date, project_number, project_task_name), // NOT a copy of every backlog_fact row: real data check against the Nov // 2025 seed file (see scoping_backlog_history.md) found this 2-column // key is not perfectly unique — 122 of 10,241 rows shared a key with // another row (same project/task, presumably split across service // lines) — so contract_balance is SUMMED across any rows sharing a key, // never assumed 1:1. A NULL project_number or project_task_name row is // skipped rather than grouped under a NULL key, which would silently // merge unrelated backlog together. async function captureBacklogSnapshot(env, snapshotDate) { const rows = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT project_number, project_task_name, region_id, service_family, revenue_type, contract_balance, end_date FROM backlog_fact WHERE project_number IS NOT NULL AND project_task_name IS NOT NULL` ).all()).results || []; if (rows.length === 0) return { captured: 0 }; // nothing to snapshot yet (e.g. very first upload) const byKey = new Map(); for (const r of rows) { const key = `${r.project_number}${r.project_task_name}`; if (!byKey.has(key)) { byKey.set(key, { project_number: r.project_number, project_task_name: r.project_task_name, region_id: r.region_id, service_family: r.service_family, revenue_type: r.revenue_type, contract_balance: 0, end_date: r.end_date, }); } const acc = byKey.get(key); acc.contract_balance += (r.contract_balance || 0); // Keep the latest non-null end_date among grouped rows as the // representative schedule date for this key, rather than the first // one seen arbitrarily. if (r.end_date && (!acc.end_date || r.end_date > acc.end_date)) acc.end_date = r.end_date; } const cols = ['snapshot_date','project_number','project_task_name','region_id','service_family','revenue_type','contract_balance','end_date']; const placeholders = '(' + cols.map(() => '?').join(',') + ')'; const insertSql = `INSERT OR REPLACE INTO backlog_snapshots (${cols.join(',')}) VALUES ${placeholders}`; const entries = [...byKey.values()]; let captured = 0; for (let i = 0; i < entries.length; i += BATCH_INSERT_SIZE) { const slice = entries.slice(i, i + BATCH_INSERT_SIZE); const stmts = slice.map(e => env.DB.prepare(insertSql).bind( snapshotDate, e.project_number, e.project_task_name, e.region_id, e.service_family, e.revenue_type, e.contract_balance, e.end_date )); await env.DB.batch(stmts); captured += slice.length; } return { captured }; } // One-time historical seed import (2026-09-12), per Misty's direct // request: "can we just do a one time import of the file in the UI?" // rather than hundreds of hand-pasted SQL batches for a 10,119-row real // dataset (this project's standing D1-console rule caps individual // pastes at ~20 rows; this file is 500x that). Reuses the same parsed- // row shape the live Backlog upload already produces // (parseSalesforceHtmlExport on the frontend), but writes to // backlog_snapshots under an explicit snapshot date instead of // replacing backlog_fact — this is historical data older than anything // currently live, not a new current-state upload. // // Two real, Misty-confirmed decisions baked in here, not invented: // - Southeast and Southwest (real regions in this Nov 2025 file, not // present in today's 8-region schema) both fold into South. // - (project_number, project_task_name) is aggregated (SUM contract_ // balance), not assumed 1:1 — 122 of 10,241 real rows in this file // shared a key with another row (scoping_backlog_history.md). const HISTORICAL_SEED_REGION_FOLD = { 'Southeast': 'South', 'Southwest': 'South' }; async function importHistoricalBacklogSnapshot(env, snapshotDate, rows, isFirstChunk) { if (!snapshotDate || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(snapshotDate)) { return { error: 'snapshotDate must be a real YYYY-MM-DD date.', status: 400 }; } if (!Array.isArray(rows) || rows.length === 0) return { error: 'No rows provided.', status: 400 }; // Clear any prior rows for this exact snapshot_date on the FIRST chunk // only, so re-running the whole import (e.g. after a failed chunk, or // a deliberate re-import to correct the file) starts clean instead of // double-adding on top of a partial or previous run — the additive // ON CONFLICT below is safe across chunks of the SAME run, but not // safe across separate runs without this. if (isFirstChunk) { await env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM backlog_snapshots WHERE snapshot_date = ?').bind(snapshotDate).run(); } const regionMap = await loadRegionMap(env); const byKey = new Map(); let skippedNoKey = 0; for (const row of rows) { const pn = row['Project Task : Project : Project Number'] || null; const ptn = row['Project Task Name'] || null; if (!pn || !ptn) { skippedNoKey++; continue; } const rawRegion = row['Responsibility: Region'] || null; const foldedRawRegion = rawRegion ? (HISTORICAL_SEED_REGION_FOLD[String(rawRegion).trim()] || rawRegion) : null; const regionId = resolveRegionId(regionMap, foldedRawRegion); const serviceFamily = row['Service Family'] || null; const revenueType = row['Service: Revenue Type'] || null; const balance = parseFloat(row['Contract Balance']) || 0; const endDate = row['End Date'] || null; const key = `${pn}${ptn}`; if (!byKey.has(key)) { byKey.set(key, { project_number: pn, project_task_name: ptn, region_id: regionId, service_family: serviceFamily, revenue_type: revenueType, contract_balance: 0, end_date: endDate }); } const acc = byKey.get(key); acc.contract_balance += balance; if (endDate && (!acc.end_date || endDate > acc.end_date)) acc.end_date = endDate; } // ON CONFLICT ... DO UPDATE (not INSERT OR REPLACE) is what makes this // safe to call once per chunk: the same real (project_number, // project_task_name) key can legitimately land in two different // chunks of a 10,000+ row file (chunking is by row position, not by // key), and a plain REPLACE would silently overwrite chunk 1's // aggregated balance with chunk 2's instead of adding them — // understating real backlog dollars for any key split across a chunk // boundary. Accumulating via SQL addition, keyed on the table's own // UNIQUE(snapshot_date, project_number, project_task_name), makes // repeated calls for the SAME snapshot_date correctly additive no // matter how the file was chunked. const upsertSql = ` INSERT INTO backlog_snapshots (snapshot_date, project_number, project_task_name, region_id, service_family, revenue_type, contract_balance, end_date) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?) ON CONFLICT(snapshot_date, project_number, project_task_name) DO UPDATE SET contract_balance = contract_balance + excluded.contract_balance, end_date = CASE WHEN excluded.end_date IS NOT NULL AND (end_date IS NULL OR excluded.end_date > end_date) THEN excluded.end_date ELSE end_date END, region_id = COALESCE(region_id, excluded.region_id), service_family = COALESCE(service_family, excluded.service_family), revenue_type = COALESCE(revenue_type, excluded.revenue_type) `; const entries = [...byKey.values()]; let imported = 0; for (let i = 0; i < entries.length; i += BATCH_INSERT_SIZE) { const slice = entries.slice(i, i + BATCH_INSERT_SIZE); const stmts = slice.map(e => env.DB.prepare(upsertSql).bind( snapshotDate, e.project_number, e.project_task_name, e.region_id, e.service_family, e.revenue_type, e.contract_balance, e.end_date )); await env.DB.batch(stmts); imported += slice.length; } return { ok: true, imported, skippedNoKey, uniqueKeys: entries.length, totalRowsReceived: rows.length }; } async function saveUploadRows(env, uploadBatchId, uploadType, rows) { const config = UPLOAD_TYPE_CONFIG[uploadType]; if (!config) return { error: 'Invalid upload type.', status: 400 }; if (!Array.isArray(rows) || rows.length === 0) return { error: 'No rows provided.', status: 400 }; if (rows.length > MAX_ROWS_PER_REQUEST) { return { error: `Too many rows in one request (max ${MAX_ROWS_PER_REQUEST}). Send in smaller chunks.`, status: 400 }; } const batch = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id, row_count FROM upload_batches WHERE id = ?').bind(uploadBatchId).first(); if (!batch) return { error: 'Upload batch not found.', status: 404 }; const isFirstChunk = (batch.row_count || 0) === 0; // Snapshot the CURRENT (about-to-be-replaced) Backlog state before its // delete runs, first chunk of a backlog upload only — see // captureBacklogSnapshot above. Deliberately a separate statement // executed before the delete+insert batch below, not folded into that // same env.DB.batch() call: a snapshot failure should not block the // upload itself (it's an enhancement, not a requirement for the // upload to succeed), so it's awaited and any error is swallowed here // rather than surfaced as an upload failure. let backlogSnapshotResult = null; if (uploadType === 'backlog' && isFirstChunk) { try { backlogSnapshotResult = await captureBacklogSnapshot(env, new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)); } catch (e) { backlogSnapshotResult = { error: String(e && e.message || e) }; } } const regionMap = await loadRegionMap(env); const mapped = rows.map(r => ({ upload_batch_id: uploadBatchId, ...config.mapRow(r, regionMap) })); const placeholders = '(' + config.columns.map(() => '?').join(',') + ')'; const insertSql = `INSERT INTO ${config.table} (${config.columns.join(',')}) VALUES ${placeholders}`; const deleteStmt = env.DB.prepare(`DELETE FROM ${config.table}`); let inserted = 0; for (let i = 0; i < mapped.length; i += BATCH_INSERT_SIZE) { const slice = mapped.slice(i, i + BATCH_INSERT_SIZE); const stmts = slice.map(row => env.DB.prepare(insertSql).bind(...config.columns.map(c => row[c] ?? null))); if (i === 0 && isFirstChunk) { // Same-batch(), one-logical-operation pattern as // replaceBilledFactForYear: delete and the first insert slice // happen in one D1 batch() call, so a failure partway through // can't leave the table half-deleted with no new rows in it. await env.DB.batch([deleteStmt, ...stmts]); } else { await env.DB.batch(stmts); } inserted += slice.length; } await env.DB.prepare('UPDATE upload_batches SET row_count = row_count + ? WHERE id = ?') .bind(inserted, uploadBatchId).run(); // Surface rows whose raw region didn't resolve, so the admin can see // gaps rather than have them silently land as NULL region_id — real // production data has occasionally had a few null/blank region rows // (§24/§28), which is expected, but an unmapped NON-blank raw value is // worth flagging since it means region_raw_mappings needs a new row. // Persisted to unmapped_region_log (2026-09-10), not just returned in // this response — uploadType here is already one of DOLLAR_FIELD_BY_ // SOURCE's real keys (bookings/backlog/proposal_pipeline/proposal_ // volume), same strings VALID_UPLOAD_TYPES uses. const unmapped = await logUnmappedRegions(env, uploadType, mapped, uploadBatchId); return { ok: true, inserted, unmappedRegions: unmapped, backlogSnapshot: backlogSnapshotResult }; } async function listUploadBatches(env) { const rows = await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT ub.*, u.name AS uploaded_by_name FROM upload_batches ub LEFT JOIN users u ON u.id = ub.uploaded_by ORDER BY ub.uploaded_at DESC LIMIT 100` ).all(); return rows.results || []; } // ---- Regions: Growth Snapshot (2026-09-05) ----------------------------- // // Phase 1 of the Regions nav item (previously a disabled stub, per the // confirmed scoping in claude/PULSE-regions-growth-snapshot-scoping.md). // Assembles everything the Region Growth Strategy deck template needs // that is genuinely derivable from real PULSE data today: // - Backlog by Line of Business (bookings_fact + backlog_fact), with a // user-set growth target per region/LOB (region_growth_targets), // never a hardcoded percentage. // - Largest Accounts, Sales-only for now (bookings_fact.parent_company). // A combined view including backlog_fact.parent_customer_name is // explicitly deferred, real account names were checked directly // against live D1 data and the two fields are often different grains // of the same relationship (e.g. bookings' "Cushman & Wakefield" vs. // backlog's "Cushman & Wakefield (500 New Jersey)" for one specific // property), so a real reviewable mapping table is needed before this // can be shown safely, exact-match alone would silently under-report // real account concentration. // - Revenue Trend by service family (billed_fact), a genuinely new // multi-year query, every other billed_fact read in this file is // scoped to one fiscal year at a time. // - Market Tier count (city_market_tiers joined via state_region_map). // Real gap detection ("which top-priority markets have no team // nearby") is deferred pending the not-yet-built Coverage upload. async function listRegionGrowthTargets(env, fiscalYear) { const rows = await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT region_id, line_of_business, growth_pct FROM region_growth_targets WHERE fiscal_year = ?` ).bind(fiscalYear).all(); const byRegion = new Map(); // region_id -> { [lob]: pct } for (const r of (rows.results || [])) { if (!byRegion.has(r.region_id)) byRegion.set(r.region_id, {}); byRegion.get(r.region_id)[r.line_of_business] = r.growth_pct; } return byRegion; } // Independent of the fiscal-year lock used by Sales/Revenue Budgets, // confirmed directly with Misty: a backlog growth target is a Regions // planning input, not a Setup Baseline budget cell, so it stays editable // even when that year's budgets are locked. async function upsertRegionGrowthTarget(env, { regionId, lineOfBusiness, fiscalYear, growthPct }, actorUserId) { if (!VALID_LOBS.includes(lineOfBusiness)) return { error: 'Invalid line of business.', status: 400 }; if (!isValidFiscalYear(fiscalYear)) return { error: 'Invalid fiscal year.', status: 400 }; const region = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM regions WHERE id = ?').bind(regionId).first(); if (!region) return { error: 'Region not found.', status: 404 }; const pct = Number(growthPct); if (!Number.isFinite(pct)) return { error: 'Invalid growth percentage.', status: 400 }; const id = randomToken(16); await env.DB.prepare( `INSERT INTO region_growth_targets (id, region_id, line_of_business, fiscal_year, growth_pct, updated_by, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?) ON CONFLICT(region_id, line_of_business, fiscal_year) DO UPDATE SET growth_pct = excluded.growth_pct, updated_by = excluded.updated_by, updated_at = excluded.updated_at` ).bind(id, regionId, lineOfBusiness, fiscalYear, pct, actorUserId, new Date().toISOString()).run(); return { ok: true }; } // Company-Wide Revenue Growth Target (2026-09-10), per Misty's direct // request: a single flat company-wide Revenue growth % for the fiscal // year, separate from both region_growth_targets (per-region/per-LOB // Sales/Backlog planning input) and budget_company_targets (Build Growth // Budget's lock-gated wizard target). Same independence-from-the-lock // convention as region_growth_targets, confirmed by precedent: this is a // live planning input, always editable, never gated by // assertFiscalYearUnlocked. async function getCompanyRevenueGrowthTarget(env, fiscalYear) { const row = await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT growth_pct FROM company_revenue_growth_targets WHERE fiscal_year = ?` ).bind(fiscalYear).first(); return row ? row.growth_pct : null; } async function upsertCompanyRevenueGrowthTarget(env, { fiscalYear, growthPct }, actorUserId) { if (!isValidFiscalYear(fiscalYear)) return { error: 'Invalid fiscal year.', status: 400 }; const pct = Number(growthPct); if (!Number.isFinite(pct)) return { error: 'Invalid growth percentage.', status: 400 }; await env.DB.prepare( `INSERT INTO company_revenue_growth_targets (fiscal_year, growth_pct, updated_by, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?) ON CONFLICT(fiscal_year) DO UPDATE SET growth_pct = excluded.growth_pct, updated_by = excluded.updated_by, updated_at = excluded.updated_at` ).bind(fiscalYear, pct, actorUserId, new Date().toISOString()).run(); return { ok: true }; } // A suggested growth percentage per region/LOB, never auto-applied, only // ever returned for the frontend to show as a clickable starting point // (confirmed with Misty: the user sets the real target, the system never // decides it for them). Based on this LOB's own recent YoY growth // (2025-to-2026 actual, from bookings_fact), rounded to the nearest whole // percent. Falls back to 0 when there's no prior-year figure to compare // against. Clamped to [-50, 100]: a LOB with a small or volatile prior-year // base can otherwise produce a YoY swing like +766% or -900%, which is // real math but a useless, distracting suggestion for a growth target. // The clamp keeps the suggestion data-informed without being absurd; the // user can still type any value they want regardless of this suggestion. const SUGGESTED_GROWTH_PCT_MIN = -50; const SUGGESTED_GROWTH_PCT_MAX = 100; function suggestGrowthPct(yoyPct) { if (yoyPct === null || !Number.isFinite(yoyPct)) return 0; const rounded = Math.round(yoyPct); return Math.min(SUGGESTED_GROWTH_PCT_MAX, Math.max(SUGGESTED_GROWTH_PCT_MIN, rounded)); } // Company-wide Backlog by Line of Business (2026-09-08, added for the new // Executive Summary "By Line of Business" section, the same 3 tiles as // Slide 1 of the Growth Strategy Report but summed across every region // instead of one). Reuses getRegionsScreen's exact per-region formula // (bookings_fact prior/current by awarded year, backlog_fact by end-date // year, region_growth_targets override else suggestGrowthPct) rather than // inventing a single company-wide target percentage (standing rule: // never hardcode a value copied from the source PPTX, e.g. its "+12%", // as a real default) — each region's own real, already-set target (or its // own suggested fallback) is computed first, and targetAmount/gap are // SUMMED across regions, so this is a true rollup of what's already on // the live Regions screen, not a second, parallel target concept. async function getCompanyLobBacklog(env, fiscalYear) { const priorYear = fiscalYear - 1; const bookingsRows = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT region_id, revenue_type, ${AWARDED_YEAR_SQL} AS yr, SUM(total_price) AS total FROM bookings_fact WHERE awarded_date IS NOT NULL AND awarded_date != '' AND ${AWARDED_YEAR_SQL} IN (?, ?) GROUP BY region_id, revenue_type, yr` ).bind(priorYear, fiscalYear).all()).results || []; // Current-year Sales actuals by LOB, bucketed by month (2026-09-11, // fixing the Sales-tile gap-to-close bug below): company-wide, same // grouping shape as revenueRowsForLob just below, but from bookings_fact // so a real per-LOB blended Sales figure (computeBlendedForecast) can be // compared against Sales Budget instead of Revenue Budget. No pipeline- // blending here (proposal_pipeline_fact has no LOB column -- same // documented limitation as Business Lines' own per-LOB Sales table, // getForecastBusinessLines above), so this uses the same plain trailing- // average computeBlendedForecast that table already uses per LOB. const salesRowsForLob = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT revenue_type, ${AWARDED_MONTH_SQL} AS month, SUM(total_price) AS total FROM bookings_fact WHERE ${AWARDED_YEAR_SQL} = ? AND awarded_date IS NOT NULL AND awarded_date != '' GROUP BY revenue_type, month` ).bind(fiscalYear).all()).results || []; const salesByLobMonthly = {}; for (const lobKey of VALID_LOBS) salesByLobMonthly[lobKey] = new Map(); for (const r of salesRowsForLob) { const lob = mapRevenueTypeToLob(r.revenue_type); if (!lob) continue; const m = salesByLobMonthly[lob]; m.set(r.month, (m.get(r.month) || 0) + (r.total || 0)); } const asOfForSalesLob = await resolveForecastAsOf(env); const backlogRows = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT region_id, revenue_type, SUM(contract_balance) AS total FROM backlog_fact WHERE end_date IS NOT NULL AND end_date != '' AND ${END_YEAR_SQL} = ? GROUP BY region_id, revenue_type` ).bind(fiscalYear + 1).all()).results || []; // Current-year Revenue actuals by LOB (2026-09-09, for the reworked // "gap to close" basis, per Misty's direct call: current-year actual/ // blended Revenue vs. current-year Revenue Budget for that LOB, replacing // the next-year-backlog-vs-growth-target comparison below, which is kept // only as the "Next-year backlog" footnote now). billed_fact has no // service_family, so groupKey isn't needed here, only the month-by-month // totals computeBlendedForecast needs to produce a real blended figure // consistent with how Forecast treats "this year" everywhere else. const revenueRowsForLob = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT revenue_type, month, SUM(amount) AS total FROM billed_fact WHERE fiscal_year = ? GROUP BY revenue_type, month` ).bind(fiscalYear).all()).results || []; const revenueAvailableForLob = revenueRowsForLob.length > 0; const revenueByLobMonthly = {}; for (const lobKey of VALID_LOBS) revenueByLobMonthly[lobKey] = new Map(); for (const r of revenueRowsForLob) { const lob = mapRevenueTypeToLob(r.revenue_type); if (!lob) continue; const m = revenueByLobMonthly[lob]; m.set(r.month, (m.get(r.month) || 0) + (r.total || 0)); } const asOfForLob = revenueAvailableForLob ? await resolveForecastAsOf(env) : null; // Current-year Revenue Budget by LOB, summed across all regions — // revenue_budgets_monthly is keyed by (region_id, line_of_business, // fiscal_year, month), so this is a real per-LOB figure, not an invented // split of a company-wide total. const revenueBudgetMonthlyRows = await listRevenueBudgetsMonthly(env, fiscalYear); const revenueBudgetByLob = {}; for (const lobKey of VALID_LOBS) revenueBudgetByLob[lobKey] = 0; for (const r of revenueBudgetMonthlyRows) { if (revenueBudgetByLob[r.line_of_business] === undefined) continue; revenueBudgetByLob[r.line_of_business] += r.amount || 0; } // Current-year Sales Budget by LOB (2026-09-11, fixing a real bug: the // "By Line of Business (Sales)" tile's gap-to-close line had been // comparing Sales actual/blended against Revenue Budget for that LOB -- // two different metrics with two different budget tables in this // schema, misleading regardless of which basis Revenue's own tile uses. // sales_budgets is keyed by (line_of_business, fiscal_year) directly, no // region split, so this is a straight per-LOB sum, same shape as // vsSalesBudget's company-wide version above (getGrowthTargetPlan). const salesBudgetRows = await listBudgets(env, 'sales_budgets', fiscalYear); const salesBudgetByLob = {}; for (const lobKey of VALID_LOBS) salesBudgetByLob[lobKey] = 0; for (const r of salesBudgetRows) { if (salesBudgetByLob[r.line_of_business] === undefined) continue; salesBudgetByLob[r.line_of_business] += r.amount || 0; } const growthTargetsByRegion = await listRegionGrowthTargets(env, fiscalYear); const byRegionLob = new Map(); // regionId -> { [lob]: {prior, current, backlogNext} } function regionBucket(regionId) { if (!byRegionLob.has(regionId)) { const b = {}; for (const lobKey of VALID_LOBS) b[lobKey] = { prior: 0, current: 0, backlogNext: 0 }; byRegionLob.set(regionId, b); } return byRegionLob.get(regionId); } for (const r of bookingsRows) { const lob = mapRevenueTypeToLob(r.revenue_type); if (!lob) continue; const bucket = regionBucket(r.region_id)[lob]; if (r.yr === priorYear) bucket.prior += r.total || 0; if (r.yr === fiscalYear) bucket.current += r.total || 0; } for (const r of backlogRows) { const lob = mapRevenueTypeToLob(r.revenue_type); if (!lob) continue; regionBucket(r.region_id)[lob].backlogNext += r.total || 0; } const companyTotals = {}; for (const lobKey of VALID_LOBS) companyTotals[lobKey] = { prior: 0, current: 0, backlogNext: 0, targetAmount: 0 }; for (const [regionId, byLob] of byRegionLob.entries()) { const regionTargets = growthTargetsByRegion.get(regionId) || {}; for (const lobKey of VALID_LOBS) { const d = byLob[lobKey]; const yoyPct = d.prior > 0 ? ((d.current - d.prior) / d.prior) * 100 : null; const growthPct = regionTargets[lobKey] !== undefined ? regionTargets[lobKey] : null; const effectivePct = growthPct !== null ? growthPct : suggestGrowthPct(yoyPct); const targetAmount = d.current * (1 + effectivePct / 100); const t = companyTotals[lobKey]; t.prior += d.prior; t.current += d.current; t.backlogNext += d.backlogNext; t.targetAmount += targetAmount; } } return Promise.all(VALID_LOBS.map(async lobKey => { const t = companyTotals[lobKey]; const yoyPct = t.prior > 0 ? ((t.current - t.prior) / t.prior) * 100 : null; // Legacy next-year-backlog-vs-growth-target gap, kept as-is: still the // real underlying data, just demoted to a footnote in the UI now // rather than the card's headline "gap to close" (2026-09-09, Misty: // "next year backlog is a good footnote in this box but not the main // story"). const gap = t.backlogNext - t.targetAmount; // New headline gap-to-close (2026-09-09, Misty's confirmed basis): // current-year actual/blended Revenue for this LOB vs. current-year // Revenue Budget for this LOB. revenueBlended stays null (never a fake // $0) when billed_fact has zero rows this fiscal year at all, same // "explicit pending state" convention used everywhere else Revenue // appears in this app. Backlog-blended (2026-09-10, per Misty's direct // request): remaining months use this LOB's real scheduled Revenue // Backlog where it exists, trailing average only as fallback. let revenueBlended = null, revenueGapToBudget = null; const lobRevenueBudget = revenueBudgetByLob[lobKey] || 0; if (revenueAvailableForLob && asOfForLob) { const backlogByMonth = await loadRevenueBacklogScheduledByMonth(env, fiscalYear, { lobKey }); const forecast = computeRevenueBlendedForecast(revenueByLobMonthly[lobKey], asOfForLob, backlogByMonth); revenueBlended = forecast.currentMonthBlended + forecast.priorActualsSum + forecast.remainingMonthsForecast; if (lobRevenueBudget > 0) revenueGapToBudget = revenueBlended - lobRevenueBudget; } // Sales gap-to-close (2026-09-11, fix): current-year actual/blended // Sales for this LOB vs. current-year Sales Budget for this LOB -- // real Sales-basis fields, replacing the Revenue-basis ones the Sales // tile had been reading by mistake. bookings_fact always has rows once // the fiscal year has any real activity (unlike billed_fact, which // depends on a Revenue upload), so this doesn't need the "available at // all" gate revenueBlended above uses -- computeBlendedForecast on an // all-zero month map just returns 0s, which is a real, not fabricated, // answer here. const lobSalesBudget = salesBudgetByLob[lobKey] || 0; const salesForecast = computeBlendedForecast(salesByLobMonthly[lobKey], asOfForSalesLob); const salesBlended = salesForecast.totalYearForecast; const salesGapToBudget = lobSalesBudget > 0 ? salesBlended - lobSalesBudget : null; return { lob: lobKey, label: LOB_LABELS[lobKey], prior: t.prior, current: t.current, yoyPct, // Headline gap-to-close for the Revenue-by-LOB tile, current-year // Revenue Budget basis. revenueBudget: lobRevenueBudget > 0 ? lobRevenueBudget : null, revenueBlended, revenueGapToBudget, revenueOnPace: revenueGapToBudget === null ? null : revenueGapToBudget >= 0, // Headline gap-to-close for the Sales-by-LOB tile, current-year // Sales Budget basis (2026-09-11 fix -- see comment above). salesBudget: lobSalesBudget > 0 ? lobSalesBudget : null, salesBlended, salesGapToBudget, salesOnPace: salesGapToBudget === null ? null : salesGapToBudget >= 0, // Footnote-only now: next-year backlog vs. computed growth target. backlogNext: t.backlogNext, targetAmount: t.targetAmount, gap, onPace: gap >= 0, }; })); } async function getRegionsScreen(env, fiscalYear, regionId) { const region = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id, name FROM regions WHERE id = ?').bind(regionId).first(); if (!region) return { error: 'Region not found.', status: 404 }; const priorYear = fiscalYear - 1; // ---- Backlog by Line of Business ---- // "2025"/"2026" actuals = bookings_fact.total_price by awarded year, // this region only. "2027 Backlog" = backlog_fact.contract_balance by // end_date year, this region only (the deck's own "already booked for // next year" framing, per the confirmed scoping doc). const bookingsRows = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT revenue_type, ${AWARDED_YEAR_SQL} AS yr, SUM(total_price) AS total FROM bookings_fact WHERE region_id = ? AND awarded_date IS NOT NULL AND awarded_date != '' AND ${AWARDED_YEAR_SQL} IN (?, ?) GROUP BY revenue_type, yr` ).bind(regionId, priorYear, fiscalYear).all()).results || []; const backlogRows = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT revenue_type, SUM(contract_balance) AS total FROM backlog_fact WHERE region_id = ? AND end_date IS NOT NULL AND end_date != '' AND ${END_YEAR_SQL} = ? GROUP BY revenue_type` ).bind(regionId, fiscalYear + 1).all()).results || []; const growthTargetsByRegion = await listRegionGrowthTargets(env, fiscalYear); const regionTargets = growthTargetsByRegion.get(regionId) || {}; const byLob = {}; for (const lobKey of VALID_LOBS) byLob[lobKey] = { prior: 0, current: 0, backlogNext: 0 }; let unmappedBookings = 0, unmappedBacklog = 0; for (const r of bookingsRows) { const lob = mapRevenueTypeToLob(r.revenue_type); if (!lob) { unmappedBookings += r.total || 0; continue; } if (r.yr === priorYear) byLob[lob].prior += r.total || 0; if (r.yr === fiscalYear) byLob[lob].current += r.total || 0; } for (const r of backlogRows) { const lob = mapRevenueTypeToLob(r.revenue_type); if (!lob) { unmappedBacklog += r.total || 0; continue; } byLob[lob].backlogNext += r.total || 0; } const backlogLines = VALID_LOBS.map(lobKey => { const d = byLob[lobKey]; const yoyPct = d.prior > 0 ? ((d.current - d.prior) / d.prior) * 100 : null; const growthPct = regionTargets[lobKey] !== undefined ? regionTargets[lobKey] : null; const suggestedPct = suggestGrowthPct(yoyPct); const effectivePct = growthPct !== null ? growthPct : suggestedPct; const targetAmount = d.current * (1 + effectivePct / 100); const gap = d.backlogNext - targetAmount; return { lob: lobKey, label: LOB_LABELS[lobKey], prior: d.prior, current: d.current, yoyPct, backlogNext: d.backlogNext, growthPct, suggestedPct, targetAmount, gap, onPace: gap >= 0, }; }); const backlogTotal = { prior: backlogLines.reduce((s, l) => s + l.prior, 0), current: backlogLines.reduce((s, l) => s + l.current, 0), backlogNext: backlogLines.reduce((s, l) => s + l.backlogNext, 0), targetAmount: backlogLines.reduce((s, l) => s + l.targetAmount, 0), }; backlogTotal.yoyPct = backlogTotal.prior > 0 ? ((backlogTotal.current - backlogTotal.prior) / backlogTotal.prior) * 100 : null; backlogTotal.gap = backlogTotal.backlogNext - backlogTotal.targetAmount; backlogTotal.onPace = backlogTotal.gap >= 0; // ---- Largest Accounts (Sales only, per the confirmed deferral above) ---- // Service-family breakdown, confirmed with Misty 2026-09-05: the prior // 3-bucket LOB dots (Inspections/Design Services/Program Advisory) were // too coarse for this card specifically, she wants the real service // families/names, with VERIFY and General Consulting carved OUT of // Maintenance Management (both are recurring-revenue lines that would // otherwise be double-counted inside MM's family total), and CCM // standing on its own rather than merged into Inspections. This grouping // is local to this card, it does NOT touch VALID_LOBS or any other // screen's LOB logic (Regions backlog targets, Revenue Trend, Baseline // Service Alignment all keep using the existing 3-bucket LOB system). const accountRows = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT parent_company, service_family, service_name, SUM(total_price) AS total FROM bookings_fact WHERE region_id = ? AND ${AWARDED_YEAR_SQL} = ? AND parent_company IS NOT NULL AND parent_company != '' GROUP BY parent_company, service_family, service_name` ).bind(regionId, fiscalYear).all()).results || []; const byAccount = new Map(); // parent_company -> { total, families: Set } // Region-scoped service-family totals, ALL accounts in this region (not // just the eventual top 5), reused below to rank each account's missing // service lines by how big that line actually is here. Derived from the // same accountRows query above rather than a second round trip. const regionFamilyTotals = {}; for (const f of ACCOUNT_SERVICE_FAMILIES) regionFamilyTotals[f.key] = 0; for (const r of accountRows) { if (!byAccount.has(r.parent_company)) byAccount.set(r.parent_company, { total: 0, families: new Set() }); const acct = byAccount.get(r.parent_company); acct.total += r.total || 0; const bucket = mapServiceToAccountFamily(r.service_family, r.service_name); if (bucket) { acct.families.add(bucket); regionFamilyTotals[bucket] += r.total || 0; } } // Company-wide (every region) service-family totals for the same fiscal // year, confirmed with Misty 2026-09-06: the whitespace columns rank // each account's MISSING service lines two ways, by how big that line // is in THIS region, and separately by how big it is across all of VDA, // so a rep can see both "what's big here" and "what's big everywhere." const companyRows = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT service_family, service_name, SUM(total_price) AS total FROM bookings_fact WHERE ${AWARDED_YEAR_SQL} = ? GROUP BY service_family, service_name` ).bind(fiscalYear).all()).results || []; const companyFamilyTotals = {}; for (const f of ACCOUNT_SERVICE_FAMILIES) companyFamilyTotals[f.key] = 0; for (const r of companyRows) { const bucket = mapServiceToAccountFamily(r.service_family, r.service_name); if (bucket) companyFamilyTotals[bucket] += r.total || 0; } // Ranks an account's MISSING service families highest-$-first against a // given totals basis, top 3 only (confirmed with Misty), purely // informational, never auto-applied to anything. function rankMissingFamilies(presentFamilies, totals) { return ACCOUNT_SERVICE_FAMILIES .filter(f => !presentFamilies.has(f.key)) .sort((a, b) => (totals[b.key] || 0) - (totals[a.key] || 0)) .slice(0, 3) .map(f => f.label); } const largestAccounts = [...byAccount.entries()] .map(([name, d]) => ({ name, total: d.total, coverage: ACCOUNT_SERVICE_FAMILIES.map(f => d.families.has(f.key)), coverageCount: d.families.size, whitespaceRegion: rankMissingFamilies(d.families, regionFamilyTotals), whitespaceCompany: rankMissingFamilies(d.families, companyFamilyTotals), })) .sort((a, b) => b.total - a.total) .slice(0, 5); // ---- Revenue Trend by service family (real, multi-year) ---- // Genuinely new: every other billed_fact read in this file scopes to // one fiscal_year at a time. This one spans priorYear-1..fiscalYear // (a 3-year window when data exists that far back) since Details_1.xlsx // carries real 2024-2026 history, more than bookings_fact currently has. const revenueYears = [priorYear - 1, priorYear, fiscalYear]; const revenueRows = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT fiscal_year, revenue_type, SUM(amount) AS total FROM billed_fact WHERE region_id = ? AND fiscal_year IN (?, ?, ?) GROUP BY fiscal_year, revenue_type` ).bind(regionId, ...revenueYears).all()).results || []; const revenueByYear = new Map(); // year -> { [lob]: total, total } for (const yr of revenueYears) revenueByYear.set(yr, { program_advisory: 0, design_services: 0, inspections: 0, total: 0 }); let revenueUnmapped = 0; for (const r of revenueRows) { const lob = mapRevenueTypeToLob(r.revenue_type); const bucket = revenueByYear.get(r.fiscal_year); if (!bucket) continue; if (lob) { bucket[lob] += r.total || 0; bucket.total += r.total || 0; } else revenueUnmapped += r.total || 0; } const revenueAvailable = revenueRows.length > 0; // byLobYoy: each line of business's OWN YoY, computed the same way as // the total-level yoyPct but per LOB (added alongside byLob, not // replacing anything existing) — fixes a real bug caught by Misty on // the live Growth Strategy Report: Inspections/Design Services/Program // Advisory were all showing the identical total-level yoyPct on their // own rows, even when their real dollar figures moved in opposite // directions (e.g. Design Services up while Inspections dropped // sharply). byLob's dollar figures were always correct; only the // frontend's reuse of the single total-level percentage was wrong. // historicalAvg/vsHistoricalAvgPct/vsHistoricalAvgDollar (2026-09-10): // trailing 2-year average baseline, shared with the new company-wide // trend via computeRevenueTrendWithTrailingAvg — see that function's // comment for the real methodology and why trailing 2-year was chosen // over a growing all-history average. const revenueTrend = computeRevenueTrendWithTrailingAvg(revenueByYear, revenueYears); // ---- Market Tier count ---- // Joins city_market_tiers (state-keyed) through state_region_map (also // state-keyed) to this region. A state maps to exactly one region // today, matching state_region_map's existing shape, so this is a // direct count, not an approximation. const tierRows = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT cmt.tier, COUNT(*) AS n FROM city_market_tiers cmt JOIN state_region_map srm ON srm.state = cmt.state WHERE srm.region_id = ? GROUP BY cmt.tier` ).bind(regionId).all()).results || []; const tierCounts = { tier_1: 0, tier_2: 0, tier_3: 0 }; for (const r of tierRows) if (tierCounts[r.tier] !== undefined) tierCounts[r.tier] = r.n; // ---- Growth Opportunity Markets (Coverage upload, redesigned 2026-09-06) -- // Real once both roster files have been uploaded at least once; // coverageAvailable reflects whether coverage_roster has any counting // rows yet at all (company-wide), not just this region, so the // frontend can tell "no team data yet" apart from "everyone's close". const coverageRowCount = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM coverage_roster WHERE counts_toward_coverage = 1` ).first())?.n || 0; const coverageAvailable = coverageRowCount > 0; const coverage = coverageAvailable ? await getTopGrowthOpportunityMarkets(env, regionId, fiscalYear) : { markets: [], geocodeMissing: [], dismissedCount: 0, totalEligible: 0 }; // Pace vs. Budget + driver drill-down (2026-09-10, Regions redesign per // the approved mockup: "what a regional VP needs to make adjustments // and ensure budget attainment"). Reuses getForecastRegionalDrivers' // already-real per-region computation (Sales and Revenue blended // forecast vs. trailing average vs. budget, plus the LOB/service-family // shortfall drill-down computeRegionDriverForScope already returns) // rather than re-querying — this is the exact same real number already // shown on Forecast > Regional Drivers, just picked out for one region // instead of listing all of them, so Regions and Forecast can never // silently disagree. regionPace is null only if this region_id somehow // isn't in the regions table getForecastRegionalDrivers reads from // (shouldn't happen given `region` was already confirmed to exist // above, but kept defensive rather than assuming array position). const forecastRegionalDrivers = await getForecastRegionalDrivers(env, fiscalYear); const regionPace = forecastRegionalDrivers.regions.find(r => r.regionId === regionId) || null; // Top Sales Performers / Top Billers, this region (2026-09-10, per the // approved mockup — same real leaderboard logic built for Executive // Summary's company-wide cards, regionId scoped here instead of null). // Top 3, matching the mockup (Executive Summary's company-wide cards // are Top 5, confirmed as the one real difference between the two). const [topSalesPerformers, topBillers] = await Promise.all([ getTopSalesPerformers(env, fiscalYear, { regionId, limit: 3 }), getTopBillers(env, fiscalYear, { regionId, limit: 3 }), ]); return { regionId: region.id, regionName: region.name, fiscalYear, backlog: { lines: backlogLines, total: backlogTotal, unmappedBookings, unmappedBacklog }, asOf: forecastRegionalDrivers.asOf, regionPace, topSalesPerformers, topBillers, largestAccounts, accountServiceFamilies: ACCOUNT_SERVICE_FAMILIES.map(f => f.label), // Real constituent service_name values folded into the Maintenance // Management bucket, for the frontend's hover tooltip, confirmed with // Misty (2026-09-05) rather than a plain unlabeled "Other" bucket. maintenanceManagementIncludes: [ 'Full Maintenance Agreement (FMA)', 'Bid Analysis', 'Misc. MM Services', 'Deficiency Tracking & Follow Up Visit', 'RFP Fulfillment', 'Bid Walk Through', 'Contract Review', 'Peer Review', 'Bidding', 'Repair/Upgrade Technical Specification', 'Contract Negotiation', 'Maintenance Management Expenses', 'Expediting Services', ], revenue: { trend: revenueTrend, available: revenueAvailable, unmapped: revenueUnmapped }, marketTiers: tierCounts, coverageAvailable, growthOpportunityMarkets: coverage.markets, growthOpportunityDismissedCount: coverage.dismissedCount, growthOpportunityTotalEligible: coverage.totalEligible, coverageGeocodeMissing: coverage.geocodeMissing, regionBookingsCurrentYear: backlogTotal.current, }; } // ---- Growth Strategy Report (2026-09-06) ------------------------------- // // Real build of the mockup approved this segment. Reuses getRegionsScreen // for Backlog by LOB, Top Whitespace Accounts, Revenue Trend, and Market // Tier (identical figures, no duplicate queries) and adds the sections // that don't exist anywhere else yet: Account Concentration, Lowest- // ============================================================ // GROWTH STRATEGY — COMPANY-WIDE (2026-09-10). The other half of the // Growth Strategy nav mockup Misty approved ("this is great, let's // build it") — "By Region" (getGrowthStrategyReport below) got built // first when Growth Strategy was extracted from Regions; this is the // company-wide roll-up tab that mockup also showed, now wired to real // data instead of illustrative placeholders. Three real pieces, same // scope as the approved mockup's own callouts: // 1. Regions Needing Momentum — every region ranked by $ gap to its // own growth target, reusing the exact same region_growth_targets // + backlog-vs-target math getRegionsScreen already computes per // region, just run for every region and sorted instead of read // one at a time. // 2. Largest Accounts, Company-Wide — the same parent_company/ // service-family whitespace logic getRegionsScreen already uses // per region, with the WHERE region_id = ? dropped. // 3. Tiered Market Coverage, Company-Wide — getGrowthOpportunityMarkets // run with no region filter, same distance-to-nearest-coverage // calculation, company-wide instead of one region's cities. // No new upload types, no schema changes — same tables/helpers as the // per-region report and screen. // ============================================================ async function getGrowthStrategyCompanyWide(env, fiscalYear) { const regions = await listRegions(env); const priorYear = fiscalYear - 1; const growthTargetsByRegion = await listRegionGrowthTargets(env, fiscalYear); // ---- 1. Regions Needing Momentum ---- const bookingsRows = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT region_id, revenue_type, ${AWARDED_YEAR_SQL} AS yr, SUM(total_price) AS total FROM bookings_fact WHERE awarded_date IS NOT NULL AND awarded_date != '' AND ${AWARDED_YEAR_SQL} IN (?, ?) AND region_id IS NOT NULL GROUP BY region_id, revenue_type, yr` ).bind(priorYear, fiscalYear).all()).results || []; const backlogRows = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT region_id, revenue_type, SUM(contract_balance) AS total FROM backlog_fact WHERE end_date IS NOT NULL AND end_date != '' AND ${END_YEAR_SQL} = ? AND region_id IS NOT NULL GROUP BY region_id, revenue_type` ).bind(fiscalYear + 1).all()).results || []; const byRegionLob = new Map(); // region_id -> { [lob]: {prior, current, backlogNext} } function ensureRegionLob(regionId) { if (!byRegionLob.has(regionId)) { const init = {}; for (const lobKey of VALID_LOBS) init[lobKey] = { prior: 0, current: 0, backlogNext: 0 }; byRegionLob.set(regionId, init); } return byRegionLob.get(regionId); } for (const r of bookingsRows) { const lob = mapRevenueTypeToLob(r.revenue_type); if (!lob) continue; const d = ensureRegionLob(r.region_id)[lob]; if (r.yr === priorYear) d.prior += r.total || 0; if (r.yr === fiscalYear) d.current += r.total || 0; } for (const r of backlogRows) { const lob = mapRevenueTypeToLob(r.revenue_type); if (!lob) continue; ensureRegionLob(r.region_id)[lob].backlogNext += r.total || 0; } const regionsNeedingMomentum = regions.map(region => { const regionTargets = growthTargetsByRegion.get(region.id) || {}; const lobData = byRegionLob.get(region.id) || (() => { const init = {}; for (const lobKey of VALID_LOBS) init[lobKey] = { prior: 0, current: 0, backlogNext: 0 }; return init; })(); let totalBacklogNext = 0, totalTarget = 0; let worstLob = null, worstLobGap = 0; for (const lobKey of VALID_LOBS) { const d = lobData[lobKey]; const yoyPct = d.prior > 0 ? ((d.current - d.prior) / d.prior) * 100 : null; const growthPct = regionTargets[lobKey] !== undefined ? regionTargets[lobKey] : null; const suggestedPct = suggestGrowthPct(yoyPct); const effectivePct = growthPct !== null ? growthPct : suggestedPct; const targetAmount = d.current * (1 + effectivePct / 100); const gap = d.backlogNext - targetAmount; totalBacklogNext += d.backlogNext; totalTarget += targetAmount; if (gap < worstLobGap) { worstLobGap = gap; worstLob = lobKey; } } const gap = totalBacklogNext - totalTarget; return { regionId: region.id, regionName: region.name, backlogNext: totalBacklogNext, targetAmount: totalTarget, gap, gapPctOfTarget: totalTarget > 0 ? (gap / totalTarget) * 100 : null, onPace: gap >= 0, heldBackBy: worstLob ? LOB_LABELS[worstLob] : null, }; }) // Needing momentum = behind target, worst gap first. Regions already // on pace are still returned (onPace:true) so the frontend can choose // to show them, just sorted after the ones that need attention. .sort((a, b) => a.gap - b.gap); // ---- 2. Largest Accounts, Company-Wide ---- const accountRows = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT parent_company, service_family, service_name, SUM(total_price) AS total FROM bookings_fact WHERE ${AWARDED_YEAR_SQL} = ? AND parent_company IS NOT NULL AND parent_company != '' GROUP BY parent_company, service_family, service_name` ).bind(fiscalYear).all()).results || []; const byAccountCompany = new Map(); const companyFamilyTotalsForRanking = {}; for (const f of ACCOUNT_SERVICE_FAMILIES) companyFamilyTotalsForRanking[f.key] = 0; for (const r of accountRows) { if (!byAccountCompany.has(r.parent_company)) byAccountCompany.set(r.parent_company, { total: 0, families: new Set() }); const acct = byAccountCompany.get(r.parent_company); acct.total += r.total || 0; const bucket = mapServiceToAccountFamily(r.service_family, r.service_name); if (bucket) { acct.families.add(bucket); companyFamilyTotalsForRanking[bucket] += r.total || 0; } } function rankMissingFamiliesCompanyWide(presentFamilies) { return ACCOUNT_SERVICE_FAMILIES .filter(f => !presentFamilies.has(f.key)) .sort((a, b) => (companyFamilyTotalsForRanking[b.key] || 0) - (companyFamilyTotalsForRanking[a.key] || 0)) .slice(0, 3) .map(f => f.label); } // Ranked pool extended from 10 to 20 (Open Items 3(f), 2026-09-11: "I // also want an option to see 'the next 10'"). Same ranking, same real // total-bookings figure, just not truncated before rank 10 anymore — a // real, available superset, not new data. largestAccountsCompanyWide // (top 10, unchanged name/shape) still drives every existing tile; // largestAccountsCompanyWideExtended (top 20) backs the "next 10" panel. const accountRegion = await loadTopRegionByName(env, 'bookings_fact', 'parent_company', 'total_price', [`${AWARDED_YEAR_SQL} = ?`, `parent_company IS NOT NULL`, `parent_company != ''`], [fiscalYear]); const regionsById = new Map((await listRegions(env)).map(r => [r.id, r.name])); const largestAccountsCompanyWideExtended = [...byAccountCompany.entries()] .map(([name, d]) => { const topRegion = accountRegion.get(name); return { name, total: d.total, coverage: ACCOUNT_SERVICE_FAMILIES.map(f => d.families.has(f.key)), coverageCount: d.families.size, missingFamilyKeys: ACCOUNT_SERVICE_FAMILIES.filter(f => !d.families.has(f.key)).map(f => f.key), // Region shown is this account's largest-dollar region (Misty's // confirmed tiebreak, 2026-09-11, same convention as the Top // Sales Performers/Top Billers region sublines built earlier). regionName: topRegion ? (regionsById.get(topRegion.regionId) || null) : null, // Company-wide view has no separate "region" basis to rank against // (it IS the company basis), so whitespaceCompany is the only ranking // — matching getRegionsScreen's field name so the frontend can reuse // the same rendering code for both, just without whitespaceRegion. whitespaceCompany: rankMissingFamiliesCompanyWide(d.families), }; }) .sort((a, b) => b.total - a.total) .slice(0, 20); const largestAccountsCompanyWide = largestAccountsCompanyWideExtended.slice(0, 10); const totalAccountRevenue = [...byAccountCompany.values()].reduce((s, d) => s + d.total, 0); const top10Revenue = largestAccountsCompanyWide.reduce((s, a) => s + a.total, 0); const concentrationPct = totalAccountRevenue > 0 ? (top10Revenue / totalAccountRevenue) * 100 : null; const missingFamilyCounts = {}; for (const f of ACCOUNT_SERVICE_FAMILIES) missingFamilyCounts[f.key] = 0; for (const a of largestAccountsCompanyWide) { for (const f of ACCOUNT_SERVICE_FAMILIES) { if (!a.coverage[ACCOUNT_SERVICE_FAMILIES.indexOf(f)]) missingFamilyCounts[f.key]++; } } const fullCoverageCount = largestAccountsCompanyWide.filter(a => a.coverageCount === ACCOUNT_SERVICE_FAMILIES.length).length; // ---- 3. Tiered Market Coverage, Company-Wide ---- // Exactly getGrowthOpportunityMarkets's own query and distance logic, // with the "WHERE srm.region_id = ?" dropped — every Tier 1/2 city in // the country ranked by distance to the nearest counted coverage // point, same as that function returns per region. Same coverageAvailable // gate as getRegionsScreen, so an empty coverage_roster (before either // roster file has ever been uploaded) skips the full-table geocode scan // instead of just returning it empty. const coverageRowCount = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM coverage_roster WHERE counts_toward_coverage = 1` ).first())?.n || 0; const coverageAvailable = coverageRowCount > 0; let companyWideMarkets = []; let geocodeMissing = []; if (coverageAvailable) { const tierCities = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT cmt.state, cmt.city, cmt.tier, cg.lat, cg.lng FROM city_market_tiers cmt JOIN state_region_map srm ON srm.state = cmt.state LEFT JOIN city_geocodes cg ON cg.state = cmt.state AND cg.city = cmt.city WHERE cmt.tier IN ('tier_1', 'tier_2')` ).all()).results || []; const rosterPoints = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT DISTINCT cr.city, cr.state, cr.role, cr.source_file, cg.lat, cg.lng FROM coverage_roster cr JOIN city_geocodes cg ON cg.state = cr.state AND cg.city = cr.city WHERE cr.counts_toward_coverage = 1` ).all()).results || []; for (const tc of tierCities) { if (tc.lat == null || tc.lng == null) { geocodeMissing.push(`${tc.city}, ${tc.state}`); continue; } let nearestMiles = Infinity; let nearestPoint = null; for (const rp of rosterPoints) { const dist = haversineMiles(tc.lat, tc.lng, rp.lat, rp.lng); if (dist < nearestMiles) { nearestMiles = dist; nearestPoint = rp; } } companyWideMarkets.push({ city: tc.city, state: tc.state, tier: tc.tier, nearestMiles: Number.isFinite(nearestMiles) ? Math.round(nearestMiles) : null, nearestCity: nearestPoint ? nearestPoint.city : null, nearestRole: nearestPoint ? nearestPoint.role : null, nearestSourceFile: nearestPoint ? nearestPoint.source_file : null, }); } // Farthest (least-served) first, same convention as the per-region version. companyWideMarkets.sort((a, b) => (b.nearestMiles ?? -1) - (a.nearestMiles ?? -1)); } return { fiscalYear, regionsNeedingMomentum, largestAccounts: { rows: largestAccountsCompanyWide, // Ranks 11-20, real superset backing the "see the next 10" option // (Open Items 3(f)) and the missing-service slide-out panel, which // both need names + region beyond the top-10 tile view. rowsExtended: largestAccountsCompanyWideExtended, concentrationPct, missingFamilyCounts, fullCoverageCount, accountFamilies: ACCOUNT_SERVICE_FAMILIES.map(f => f.label), accountFamilyKeys: ACCOUNT_SERVICE_FAMILIES.map(f => f.key), }, coverageAvailable, growthOpportunityMarkets: companyWideMarkets, coverageGeocodeMissing: geocodeMissing, }; } // Selling Services, Proposal Win Rate, Sales Volume Leaderboard, Largest // Accounts One Service Only, Client Retention Rate, Account Tenure Mix, // Next $1M scoring, and Where to Focus. No new upload types, no schema // changes — every number here comes from bookings_fact, proposal_volume_ // fact, or figures getRegionsScreen already computes. // // Growth target: reuses the SAME per-region-per-LOB region_growth_targets // already set on the live Regions screen (confirmed with Misty, no // separate blended target for this report) — getRegionsScreen's own // backlogLines already reads these, so Backlog by LOB's gap/on-pace here // is identical to the live screen, not a second parallel calculation. // // Retention and Tenure: both real, from bookings_fact.parent_company + // awarded_date, no new upload. As of this build bookings_fact only has // live weekly-upload history starting 2026-09-03 (§38), so Account // Tenure Mix will honestly show almost everything as "1 year" until // older bookings data (e.g. a real 2024/2025 file) is uploaded — the // query itself is written for however many years actually exist, no // hardcoded window, no faked history (standing rule 7). async function getGrowthStrategyReport(env, fiscalYear, regionId) { const base = await getRegionsScreen(env, fiscalYear, regionId); if (base.error) return base; const priorYear = fiscalYear - 1; // ---- Re-derive byAccount / family totals ---- // getRegionsScreen doesn't return these intermediate maps (only the // top-5 largestAccounts slice), so they're recomputed here from the // same query rather than changing getRegionsScreen's return shape and // risking the live Regions screen. Same SQL, same grain, one extra // round trip — acceptable, this report is a separate, less-frequently // loaded screen. const accountRows = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT parent_company, service_family, service_name, SUM(total_price) AS total FROM bookings_fact WHERE region_id = ? AND ${AWARDED_YEAR_SQL} = ? AND parent_company IS NOT NULL AND parent_company != '' GROUP BY parent_company, service_family, service_name` ).bind(regionId, fiscalYear).all()).results || []; const byAccount = new Map(); // parent_company -> { total, families: Set, priorTotal } const regionFamilyTotals = {}; for (const f of ACCOUNT_SERVICE_FAMILIES) regionFamilyTotals[f.key] = 0; for (const r of accountRows) { if (!byAccount.has(r.parent_company)) byAccount.set(r.parent_company, { total: 0, families: new Set() }); const acct = byAccount.get(r.parent_company); acct.total += r.total || 0; const bucket = mapServiceToAccountFamily(r.service_family, r.service_name); if (bucket) { acct.families.add(bucket); regionFamilyTotals[bucket] += r.total || 0; } } const priorAccountRows = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT parent_company, service_family, service_name, SUM(total_price) AS total FROM bookings_fact WHERE region_id = ? AND ${AWARDED_YEAR_SQL} = ? AND parent_company IS NOT NULL AND parent_company != '' GROUP BY parent_company, service_family, service_name` ).bind(regionId, priorYear).all()).results || []; const priorRegionFamilyTotals = {}; for (const f of ACCOUNT_SERVICE_FAMILIES) priorRegionFamilyTotals[f.key] = 0; for (const r of priorAccountRows) { const bucket = mapServiceToAccountFamily(r.service_family, r.service_name); if (bucket) priorRegionFamilyTotals[bucket] += r.total || 0; } const companyRows = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT service_family, service_name, SUM(total_price) AS total FROM bookings_fact WHERE ${AWARDED_YEAR_SQL} = ? GROUP BY service_family, service_name` ).bind(fiscalYear).all()).results || []; const companyFamilyTotals = {}; for (const f of ACCOUNT_SERVICE_FAMILIES) companyFamilyTotals[f.key] = 0; for (const r of companyRows) { const bucket = mapServiceToAccountFamily(r.service_family, r.service_name); if (bucket) companyFamilyTotals[bucket] += r.total || 0; } // Distinct company-wide accounts buying each family, so Next $1M's // whitespace $ can use a real "typical account's spend in this family" // average (companyFamilyTotals ÷ this count) rather than an unrelated // account's whole-account revenue (see continuity brief: whitespace $ // duplication bug, caught by Misty checking Next $1M against the live // report — three families with overlapping missing-account sets were // showing the identical, inflated $ figure). const companyAccountFamilyRows = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT parent_company, service_family, service_name FROM bookings_fact WHERE ${AWARDED_YEAR_SQL} = ? AND parent_company IS NOT NULL AND parent_company != '' GROUP BY parent_company, service_family, service_name` ).bind(fiscalYear).all()).results || []; const companyFamilyAccountCounts = {}; for (const f of ACCOUNT_SERVICE_FAMILIES) companyFamilyAccountCounts[f.key] = new Set(); for (const r of companyAccountFamilyRows) { const bucket = mapServiceToAccountFamily(r.service_family, r.service_name); if (bucket) companyFamilyAccountCounts[bucket].add(r.parent_company); } const companyFamilyAvgPerAccount = {}; for (const f of ACCOUNT_SERVICE_FAMILIES) { const n = companyFamilyAccountCounts[f.key].size; companyFamilyAvgPerAccount[f.key] = n > 0 ? companyFamilyTotals[f.key] / n : 0; } function rankMissingFamilies(presentFamilies, totals) { return ACCOUNT_SERVICE_FAMILIES .filter(f => !presentFamilies.has(f.key)) .sort((a, b) => (totals[b.key] || 0) - (totals[a.key] || 0)) .slice(0, 3) .map(f => f.label); } const accountsRanked = [...byAccount.entries()] .map(([name, d]) => ({ name, total: d.total, familyCount: d.families.size, families: d.families })) .sort((a, b) => b.total - a.total); const regionTotal = accountsRanked.reduce((s, a) => s + a.total, 0); // ---- Account Concentration ---- const top3Total = accountsRanked.slice(0, 3).reduce((s, a) => s + a.total, 0); const top10Total = accountsRanked.slice(0, 10).reduce((s, a) => s + a.total, 0); const concentration = { top3Pct: regionTotal > 0 ? (top3Total / regionTotal) * 100 : null, top10Pct: regionTotal > 0 ? (top10Total / regionTotal) * 100 : null, restPct: regionTotal > 0 ? ((regionTotal - top10Total) / regionTotal) * 100 : null, }; // ---- Lowest-Selling Services (region vs. company, ascending) ---- // Standing exception preserved: Inspections excluded from this ranking // in West and Northwest (existing real rule, not new to this report). const EXCLUDE_INSPECTIONS_REGIONS = new Set(['west', 'northwest']); const excludeInspections = EXCLUDE_INSPECTIONS_REGIONS.has(regionId); const lowestSellingServices = ACCOUNT_SERVICE_FAMILIES .filter(f => !(excludeInspections && f.key === 'inspections')) .map(f => ({ key: f.key, label: f.label, region: regionFamilyTotals[f.key] || 0, company: companyFamilyTotals[f.key] || 0 })) .sort((a, b) => a.region - b.region) .slice(0, 3); // ---- Sales Volume Leaderboard + Largest Accounts, One Service Only ---- const topAccountsByVolume = accountsRanked.slice(0, 3).map(a => ({ name: a.name, current: a.total, prior: (priorAccountRows.filter(r => r.parent_company === a.name).reduce((s, r) => s + (r.total || 0), 0)) || 0, })); const topServicesByVolume = ACCOUNT_SERVICE_FAMILIES .map(f => ({ label: f.label, current: regionFamilyTotals[f.key] || 0, prior: priorRegionFamilyTotals[f.key] || 0 })) .sort((a, b) => b.current - a.current) .slice(0, 3); const oneServiceOnlyAccounts = accountsRanked .filter(a => a.familyCount === 1) .slice(0, 3) .map(a => { const boughtKey = [...a.families][0]; const bought = ACCOUNT_FAMILY_LABELS[boughtKey] || boughtKey; const next = rankMissingFamilies(a.families, regionFamilyTotals); return { name: a.name, bought, natural2nd: next[0] || null, natural3rd: next[1] || null }; }); // ---- Proposal Win Rate (proposal_volume_fact, region vs. company) ---- // proposal_pipeline_fact has no service breakdown at all (opportunity // grain, no service_name column) — win rate has to come from // proposal_volume_fact (line-item grain), which carries service_name // and a real Closed Won/Closed Lost stage split. proposal_volume_fact // has NO service_family column (confirmed against sales/schema.sql), // so this groups by service_name directly rather than the 9-family // rollup the rest of this report uses — a real, deliberate difference, // not an oversight (see continuity brief). Uses the real // proposal_issued_date_year column (from the source's own "Proposal // Issued By Year" field), not a parsed date string. // // CAVEAT, flagged not fixed: proposal_volume_fact has never received a // real upload (still blocked on the 64MB file, per §42/§44), so this // column's real stored format is unverified — assumed here to be a // plain year string ("2026"), matching the raw header name. Worth a // quick `SELECT DISTINCT proposal_issued_date_year FROM // proposal_volume_fact` check the first time a real file lands, rather // than trusting this blind. proposalWinRateAvailable below reads false // with zero rows either way, so a format mismatch fails safe (shows // "not yet available"), not silently wrong. const winRateRows = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT region_id, service_name, stage, SUM(total_price) AS total, COUNT(*) AS n FROM proposal_volume_fact WHERE proposal_issued_date_year = ? AND stage IN ('Closed Won', 'Closed Lost') AND service_name IS NOT NULL AND service_name != '' GROUP BY region_id, service_name, stage` ).bind(String(fiscalYear)).all()).results || []; const winRateByService = new Map(); // service_name -> { region: {won,lost}, company: {won,lost} } for (const r of winRateRows) { if (!winRateByService.has(r.service_name)) winRateByService.set(r.service_name, { region: { won: 0, lost: 0 }, company: { won: 0, lost: 0 } }); const entry = winRateByService.get(r.service_name); const wonLost = r.stage === 'Closed Won' ? 'won' : 'lost'; entry.company[wonLost] += r.total || 0; if (r.region_id === regionId) entry.region[wonLost] += r.total || 0; } const proposalWinRate = [...winRateByService.entries()] .map(([serviceName, e]) => { const regionDenom = e.region.won + e.region.lost; const companyDenom = e.company.won + e.company.lost; return { serviceName, regionWinRatePct: regionDenom > 0 ? (e.region.won / regionDenom) * 100 : null, companyWinRatePct: companyDenom > 0 ? (e.company.won / companyDenom) * 100 : null, regionVolume: regionDenom, companyVolume: companyDenom, }; }) .filter(r => r.regionVolume > 0) .sort((a, b) => b.regionVolume - a.regionVolume) .slice(0, 4); const proposalWinRateAvailable = winRateRows.length > 0; // ---- Client Retention Rate ---- // Accounts billing in BOTH priorYear and fiscalYear ÷ accounts billing // in priorYear. A pure year-over-year check — an account's longer // history doesn't change this number, see continuity brief for the // tenure-vs-retention discussion. const currentAccountNames = new Set(accountsRanked.map(a => a.name)); const priorAccountNames = new Set(priorAccountRows.map(r => r.parent_company).filter(Boolean)); const retainedCount = [...priorAccountNames].filter(n => currentAccountNames.has(n)).length; const retentionRatePct = priorAccountNames.size > 0 ? (retainedCount / priorAccountNames.size) * 100 : null; // Company-wide retention, same calculation with no region filter, as // the benchmark Next $1M's retention gate compares against. const companyCurrentRows = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT DISTINCT parent_company FROM bookings_fact WHERE ${AWARDED_YEAR_SQL} = ? AND parent_company IS NOT NULL AND parent_company != ''` ).bind(fiscalYear).all()).results || []; const companyPriorRows = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT DISTINCT parent_company FROM bookings_fact WHERE ${AWARDED_YEAR_SQL} = ? AND parent_company IS NOT NULL AND parent_company != ''` ).bind(priorYear).all()).results || []; const companyCurrentNames = new Set(companyCurrentRows.map(r => r.parent_company)); const companyPriorNames = new Set(companyPriorRows.map(r => r.parent_company)); const companyRetained = [...companyPriorNames].filter(n => companyCurrentNames.has(n)).length; const companyRetentionRatePct = companyPriorNames.size > 0 ? (companyRetained / companyPriorNames.size) * 100 : null; // ---- Account Tenure Mix ---- // Consecutive-year count per account, walking backward from fiscalYear // only as far as bookings_fact actually has data — no hardcoded window. // Real today (as of this build): bookings_fact's live weekly-upload // history starts 2026-09-03 (continuity brief §38), so most accounts // will show as "1 year" until an older bookings file is uploaded; this // query re-computes correctly the moment that happens, no code change. const allYearsRows = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT DISTINCT ${AWARDED_YEAR_SQL} AS yr FROM bookings_fact WHERE region_id = ? AND awarded_date IS NOT NULL AND awarded_date != '' AND ${AWARDED_YEAR_SQL} <= ? ORDER BY yr DESC` ).bind(regionId, fiscalYear).all()).results || []; const availableYears = allYearsRows.map(r => r.yr); const yearAccountRows = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT DISTINCT parent_company, ${AWARDED_YEAR_SQL} AS yr FROM bookings_fact WHERE region_id = ? AND parent_company IS NOT NULL AND parent_company != '' AND ${AWARDED_YEAR_SQL} <= ?` ).bind(regionId, fiscalYear).all()).results || []; const yearsByAccount = new Map(); // parent_company -> Set(years) for (const r of yearAccountRows) { if (!yearsByAccount.has(r.parent_company)) yearsByAccount.set(r.parent_company, new Set()); yearsByAccount.get(r.parent_company).add(r.yr); } function consecutiveYearsEndingAt(years, endYear) { let count = 0, y = endYear; while (years.has(y)) { count++; y--; } return count; } const tenureBuckets = { oneYear: 0, twoYear: 0, threePlusYear: 0 }; const tenureRevenue = { oneYear: 0, twoYear: 0, threePlusYear: 0 }; for (const a of accountsRanked) { const years = yearsByAccount.get(a.name) || new Set([fiscalYear]); const consecutive = consecutiveYearsEndingAt(years, fiscalYear); const bucketKey = consecutive >= 3 ? 'threePlusYear' : consecutive === 2 ? 'twoYear' : 'oneYear'; tenureBuckets[bucketKey] += 1; tenureRevenue[bucketKey] += a.total; } const accountTenureMix = { yearsOfHistoryAvailable: availableYears.length, counts: tenureBuckets, revenuePct: { oneYear: regionTotal > 0 ? (tenureRevenue.oneYear / regionTotal) * 100 : null, twoYear: regionTotal > 0 ? (tenureRevenue.twoYear / regionTotal) * 100 : null, threePlusYear: regionTotal > 0 ? (tenureRevenue.threePlusYear / regionTotal) * 100 : null, }, }; // ---- Line of Business Detail (Slide 3, §74/§84) ------------------------ // Real Inspections-vs-Program-Advisory attach/retention metrics, scoped // in continuity brief §74, built here for the first time using // resolveLineOfBusiness (see that function's own comment) since // service_alignment is confirmed empty and every prior report section // used the finer 9-family taxonomy instead of real LOB. Reuses // accountRows/priorAccountRows (already queried above for byAccount) — // no new bookings_fact query needed, just a different rollup of rows // already in hand. const alignmentRowsForYear = await listServiceAlignments(env, { effectiveFiscalYear: fiscalYear }); // Separate fetch for priorYear (2026-09-09 fix): resolveLineOfBusiness // matches on an EXACT effective_fiscal_year, so passing this same // fiscalYear-only row set into the priorYear call below (as this code // used to do) meant no override could ever match there — every // service_alignment override, once one exists, would silently apply to // the current year's accountLob but never the prior year's // priorAccountLob, making the two years' LOB buckets inconsistent for // any account touching a realigned service.
