Real data // sourced from the corrected Elevator Market Prioritization workbook, // added as six columns on the existing city_market_tiers table (not a // new table — this is the same real 119-city list already seeded // there, confirmed row-for-row against the workbook before this build). // // Two confirmed decisions shape everything here: // 1. Priority Score / Recommended Priority are context only. They // never update city_market_tiers.tier, and this function never // writes to that column — it's a pure read alongside it. // 2. Market Coverage's Top 5 ranking (getTopGrowthOpportunityMarkets, // above) stays distance-from-coverage only. This new function is // a SEPARATE, fuller view (every scored Tier 1/2/3 city in a // region, or company-wide), additive to the curated Top 5, not a // replacement or a re-ranking of it — same relationship Pipeline & // Backlog's Regional Density table has to a curated summary card. // // regionId is optional: null returns every scored city company-wide // (for a future company-wide detail view); a real region_id scopes to // that region's states via state_region_map, the same join every other // per-region screen in this app already uses. async function getMarketPrioritizationDetail(env, regionId) { const whereRegion = regionId ? `JOIN state_region_map srm ON srm.state = cmt.state WHERE srm.region_id = ?` : ''; const rows = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT cmt.state, cmt.city, cmt.tier, cmt.priority_score, cmt.recommended_priority, cmt.estimated_vt_installed_base, cmt.indicative_market_alloc_m, cmt.major_market_verticals, cmt.data_confidence, cmt.scoring_source_date FROM city_market_tiers cmt ${whereRegion} ORDER BY cmt.priority_score DESC` ).bind(...(regionId ? [regionId] : [])).all()).results || []; // Real count of unscored cities (a re-score gap, or a future new city // added to city_market_tiers before it's been scored) — surfaced // rather than silently sorted to the bottom with a blank score, since // "no score yet" and "scored zero" are different, real states. const scored = rows.filter(r => r.priority_score != null); const unscoredCount = rows.length - scored.length; return { markets: scored.map(r => ({ state: r.state, city: r.city, tier: r.tier, priorityScore: r.priority_score, recommendedPriority: r.recommended_priority, estimatedVtInstalledBase: r.estimated_vt_installed_base, indicativeMarketAllocM: r.indicative_market_alloc_m, majorMarketVerticals: r.major_market_verticals, // Frontend-facing label per the approved mockup: describes how the // estimate was built, never a grade on the market itself. underlyingData: r.data_confidence === 'Medium' ? 'Multi-Source Estimate' : r.data_confidence === 'Low' ? 'Modeled Estimate' : r.data_confidence || null, scoringSourceDate: r.scoring_source_date, })), unscoredCount, totalCities: rows.length, }; } // ---- Scenario Modeling (Supported Budget), Batch 4 (2026-09-12) ---- // Per PULSE-scenario-modeling-scoping-v2.md, approved by Misty. Named // deliberately to avoid any confusion with the pre-existing, unrelated // "Apply Growth Scenario" mechanic in the Setup Baseline wizard // (applyGrowthScenario above, which overwrites a budget table row from // a chosen growth scenario) -- this feature is a separate, additive // planning view, not a variant of that mechanic. // // Answers a different question than the Revenue Bridge: not "what will // actually land this year" (run-rate, already-won business) but "what // could next year's Backlog + Retention + Organic Growth + Weighted // Pipeline realistically support," given five named assumption rates, // at region x LOB grain -- matching the source workbook // (2025-11-20 Region BD Plans Waterfall.xlsx) "Waterfall (VDA)" sheet's // own methodology exactly, not an invented model. const SCENARIO_MODELING_DEFAULT_ASSUMPTIONS = { backlogActualizationPct: 95, inspectionRetentionPct: 85, inspectionHitRatePct: 85, eiHitRatePct: 43, ndHitRatePct: 27, }; async function getScenarioModelingAssumptions(env, fiscalYear) { const row = await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT backlog_actualization_pct, inspection_retention_pct, inspection_hit_rate_pct, ei_hit_rate_pct, nd_hit_rate_pct, updated_at FROM scenario_modeling_assumptions WHERE fiscal_year = ?` ).bind(fiscalYear).first(); if (!row) { // No saved row yet for this fiscal year -- return the real Waterfall // (VDA) starting values, plainly labeled as defaults, never silently // treated as if they were already an admin's own saved choice // (standing rule 7). return { ...SCENARIO_MODELING_DEFAULT_ASSUMPTIONS, isDefault: true, updatedAt: null }; } return { backlogActualizationPct: row.backlog_actualization_pct, inspectionRetentionPct: row.inspection_retention_pct, inspectionHitRatePct: row.inspection_hit_rate_pct, eiHitRatePct: row.ei_hit_rate_pct, ndHitRatePct: row.nd_hit_rate_pct, isDefault: false, updatedAt: row.updated_at, }; } async function upsertScenarioModelingAssumptions(env, { fiscalYear, backlogActualizationPct, inspectionRetentionPct, inspectionHitRatePct, eiHitRatePct, ndHitRatePct }, actorUserId) { if (!isValidFiscalYear(fiscalYear)) return { error: 'Invalid fiscal year.', status: 400 }; const vals = { backlogActualizationPct, inspectionRetentionPct, inspectionHitRatePct, eiHitRatePct, ndHitRatePct }; for (const [key, v] of Object.entries(vals)) { if (!Number.isFinite(Number(v))) return { error: `Invalid ${key}.`, status: 400 }; } await env.DB.prepare( `INSERT INTO scenario_modeling_assumptions (fiscal_year, backlog_actualization_pct, inspection_retention_pct, inspection_hit_rate_pct, ei_hit_rate_pct, nd_hit_rate_pct, updated_by, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?) ON CONFLICT(fiscal_year) DO UPDATE SET backlog_actualization_pct = excluded.backlog_actualization_pct, inspection_retention_pct = excluded.inspection_retention_pct, inspection_hit_rate_pct = excluded.inspection_hit_rate_pct, ei_hit_rate_pct = excluded.ei_hit_rate_pct, nd_hit_rate_pct = excluded.nd_hit_rate_pct, updated_by = excluded.updated_by, updated_at = excluded.updated_at` ).bind( fiscalYear, Number(backlogActualizationPct), Number(inspectionRetentionPct), Number(inspectionHitRatePct), Number(eiHitRatePct), Number(ndHitRatePct), actorUserId, new Date().toISOString() ).run(); return { ok: true }; } // Company-wide Weighted Pipeline, reusing the Revenue Bridge's own Step // 5 methodology exactly (open-stage only, FY-filtered // estimated_award_date, 60-day past-due exclusion, admin-set stage- // default-probability override) rather than recomputing a second, // possibly-divergent version of the same real logic. Unlike the // Revenue Bridge, this is never region-scoped here -- region/LOB // splitting happens afterward, in allocateScenarioPipeline below, by // real trailing bookings mix, since proposal_pipeline_fact itself has // no LOB column and (for this feature's company-wide starting figure) // is intentionally not filtered by region either. async function getScenarioModelingCompanyWeightedPipeline(env, fiscalYear) { const asOf = await resolveForecastAsOf(env); const pipelineRows = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT total_vda_fees, probability_pct, estimated_award_date, stage FROM proposal_pipeline_fact WHERE total_vda_fees IS NOT NULL AND probability_pct IS NOT NULL AND estimated_award_date IS NOT NULL AND estimated_award_date != '' AND ${ESTIMATED_AWARD_YEAR_SQL} = ? AND stage NOT IN ('Closed Won', 'Closed Lost')` ).bind(fiscalYear).all()).results || []; const defaultProbabilitySetting = await getRegionPipelineDefaultProbability(env, fiscalYear); const STAGE_DEFAULT_PROBABILITY = 75; const STAGE_DEFAULT_OVERRIDE_STAGES = new Set(['Negotiation/Review', 'Proposal/Price Quote']); const PIPELINE_PAST_DUE_GRACE_DAYS = 60; const asOfDateForPipeline = new Date(Date.UTC(asOf.year, asOf.month - 1, asOf.day)); let weightedPipeline = 0; for (const r of pipelineRows) { const parts = r.estimated_award_date.split('/'); if (parts.length !== 3) continue; const m = parseInt(parts[0], 10), d = parseInt(parts[1], 10), y = parseInt(parts[2], 10); if (!m || !d || !y) continue; const awardDate = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d)); const daysPastDue = (asOfDateForPipeline - awardDate) / (24 * 60 * 60 * 1000); if (daysPastDue > PIPELINE_PAST_DUE_GRACE_DAYS) continue; let probability = r.probability_pct; const isStageDefault = probability === STAGE_DEFAULT_PROBABILITY && STAGE_DEFAULT_OVERRIDE_STAGES.has(r.stage); if (isStageDefault) probability = defaultProbabilitySetting.probabilityPct; weightedPipeline += r.total_vda_fees * probability / 100.0; } return weightedPipeline; } // Real trailing-12-month bookings mix, by region and by LOB, used only // to allocate the one company-wide Weighted Pipeline figure above -- // proposal_pipeline_fact has no LOB column and this feature does not // filter it by region, so this is a real, disclosed allocation // (clearly labeled as such in the UI), not a per-opportunity region/LOB // tag PULSE's pipeline data doesn't have. Trailing 12 months (not just // the fiscal year to date) matches the source workbook's own "recent // activity" framing and gives a stable mix even early in a fiscal year. async function getScenarioModelingBookingsMix(env, fiscalYear) { const rows = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT region_id, revenue_type, ${AWARDED_YEAR_SQL} AS yr, SUM(total_price) AS total FROM bookings_fact WHERE awarded_date IS NOT NULL AND awarded_date != '' AND region_id IS NOT NULL AND ${AWARDED_YEAR_SQL} IN (?, ?) GROUP BY region_id, revenue_type, yr` ).bind(fiscalYear - 1, fiscalYear).all()).results || []; const byRegion = new Map(); const byLob = { program_advisory: 0, design_services: 0, inspections: 0 }; let total = 0; for (const r of rows) { const lob = mapRevenueTypeToLob(r.revenue_type); if (!lob) continue; byRegion.set(r.region_id, (byRegion.get(r.region_id) || 0) + (r.total || 0)); byLob[lob] += r.total || 0; total += r.total || 0; } return { byRegion, byLob, total }; } // The full region x LOB Supported Budget build. Company-wide assumption // rates (confirmed granularity: one shared set, not per region x LOB) // applied to real region x LOB data (confirmed calc granularity), // matching the source workbook's own Summary_Rev/Summary_Sales shape. async function getScenarioModeling(env, fiscalYear) { const asOf = await resolveForecastAsOf(env); const assumptions = await getScenarioModelingAssumptions(env, fiscalYear); const regions = await listRegions(env); // Current-year Sales (bookings) by region x LOB x month, for // Historical Growth Trend and "{fiscalYear} Actual + Forecast" -- // same source and grouping shape as getForecastRegionalDrivers/ // getCompanyLobBacklog, just kept split by both region and LOB here. const bookingsRows = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT region_id, revenue_type, ${AWARDED_MONTH_SQL} AS month, SUM(total_price) AS total FROM bookings_fact WHERE ${AWARDED_YEAR_SQL} = ? AND awarded_date IS NOT NULL AND awarded_date != '' AND region_id IS NOT NULL GROUP BY region_id, revenue_type, month` ).bind(fiscalYear).all()).results || []; const priorYearBookingsRows = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT region_id, revenue_type, SUM(total_price) AS total FROM bookings_fact WHERE ${AWARDED_YEAR_SQL} = ? AND awarded_date IS NOT NULL AND awarded_date != '' AND region_id IS NOT NULL GROUP BY region_id, revenue_type` ).bind(fiscalYear - 1).all()).results || []; // 2026 Backlog by region x LOB -- same query shape as the Revenue // Bridge's Steps 3/4, extended here to all three LOBs (the Bridge // itself only needs PA/DS since Inspections' Bridge contribution // comes from run-rate, not backlog; this feature needs Inspections' // backlog too, matching the source workbook). const backlogRows = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT region_id, revenue_type, SUM(contract_balance) AS total FROM backlog_fact WHERE end_date IS NOT NULL AND end_date != '' AND ${END_YEAR_SQL} = ? AND region_id IS NOT NULL GROUP BY region_id, revenue_type` ).bind(fiscalYear + 1).all()).results || []; // Prior fiscal year's real Inspections revenue by region, for the // Retention input (Inspections rows only). const priorInspectionsBilledRows = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT region_id, revenue_type, SUM(amount) AS total FROM billed_fact WHERE fiscal_year = ? AND region_id IS NOT NULL GROUP BY region_id, revenue_type` ).bind(fiscalYear - 1).all()).results || []; const salesBudgetRows = await listBudgets(env, 'sales_budgets', fiscalYear); const companyWeightedPipeline = await getScenarioModelingCompanyWeightedPipeline(env, fiscalYear); const bookingsMix = await getScenarioModelingBookingsMix(env, fiscalYear); // Index everything by region_id, then by lob, matching the pattern // getForecastRegionalDrivers already uses (fetch all regions, then // .filter()/lookup per region rather than a per-region query). const byRegionLob = () => ({ program_advisory: new Map(), design_services: new Map(), inspections: new Map() }); const currentYearMonthly = new Map(); // region_id -> lob -> Map for (const region of regions) currentYearMonthly.set(region.id, byRegionLob()); for (const r of bookingsRows) { const lob = mapRevenueTypeToLob(r.revenue_type); if (!lob || !currentYearMonthly.has(r.region_id)) continue; const m = currentYearMonthly.get(r.region_id)[lob]; m.set(r.month, (m.get(r.month) || 0) + (r.total || 0)); } const priorYearTotal = new Map(); // region_id -> lob -> $ for (const region of regions) priorYearTotal.set(region.id, { program_advisory: 0, design_services: 0, inspections: 0 }); for (const r of priorYearBookingsRows) { const lob = mapRevenueTypeToLob(r.revenue_type); if (!lob || !priorYearTotal.has(r.region_id)) continue; priorYearTotal.get(r.region_id)[lob] += r.total || 0; } const backlogByRegionLob = new Map(); for (const region of regions) backlogByRegionLob.set(region.id, { program_advisory: 0, design_services: 0, inspections: 0 }); for (const r of backlogRows) { const lob = mapRevenueTypeToLob(r.revenue_type); if (!lob || !backlogByRegionLob.has(r.region_id)) continue; backlogByRegionLob.get(r.region_id)[lob] += r.total || 0; } const priorInspectionsByRegion = new Map(); for (const region of regions) priorInspectionsByRegion.set(region.id, 0); for (const r of priorInspectionsBilledRows) { if (mapRevenueTypeToLob(r.revenue_type) !== 'inspections' || !priorInspectionsByRegion.has(r.region_id)) continue; priorInspectionsByRegion.set(r.region_id, priorInspectionsByRegion.get(r.region_id) + (r.total || 0)); } const salesBudgetByRegionLob = new Map(); for (const region of regions) salesBudgetByRegionLob.set(region.id, { program_advisory: 0, design_services: 0, inspections: 0 }); for (const b of salesBudgetRows) { if (!salesBudgetByRegionLob.has(b.region_id) || salesBudgetByRegionLob.get(b.region_id)[b.line_of_business] === undefined) continue; salesBudgetByRegionLob.get(b.region_id)[b.line_of_business] += b.amount || 0; } const HIT_RATE_BY_LOB = { inspections: assumptions.inspectionHitRatePct, program_advisory: assumptions.eiHitRatePct, design_services: assumptions.ndHitRatePct, }; const rows = []; const companyTotals = { actualForecast: 0, backlog: 0, retention: 0, organicGrowth: 0, openOpportunity: 0, weightedFunnel: 0, supportedBudgetBeforeActualization: 0, budget: 0, }; for (const region of regions) { const regionBookingsShare = bookingsMix.total > 0 ? (bookingsMix.byRegion.get(region.id) || 0) / bookingsMix.total : 0; for (const lobKey of VALID_LOBS) { const forecast = computeBlendedForecast(currentYearMonthly.get(region.id)[lobKey], asOf); const actualForecast = forecast.totalYearForecast; const priorActual = priorYearTotal.get(region.id)[lobKey]; const historicalGrowthTrendPct = priorActual > 0 ? ((actualForecast - priorActual) / priorActual) * 100 : null; const backlog = backlogByRegionLob.get(region.id)[lobKey] || 0; const retention = lobKey === 'inspections' ? (priorInspectionsByRegion.get(region.id) || 0) * (assumptions.inspectionRetentionPct / 100) : 0; // Organic Growth: the region x LOB's own Historical Growth Trend // rate applied to its own Actual + Forecast base -- a trend-based // add-on, matching the source workbook's own "Local Field Growth" // derivation (that file has no separately-sourced organic-growth // input column either). A null/negative trend contributes 0, not // a fabricated shrinkage figure subtracted elsewhere in the // roll-up -- Organic Growth is additive-only here, matching the // workbook's own presentation. const organicGrowth = historicalGrowthTrendPct != null && historicalGrowthTrendPct > 0 ? actualForecast * (historicalGrowthTrendPct / 100) : 0; const lobShare = (() => { const lobTotal = bookingsMix.byLob[lobKey] || 0; const allLobTotal = bookingsMix.byLob.program_advisory + bookingsMix.byLob.design_services + bookingsMix.byLob.inspections; return allLobTotal > 0 ? lobTotal / allLobTotal : 0; })(); // Open Opportunity: one real company-wide Weighted Pipeline figure // allocated by this region's share of trailing bookings x this // LOB's share of trailing bookings -- a disclosed allocation, not // a per-opportunity region/LOB tag (see scoping doc; the two // shares are applied together as a real, if approximate, joint // split, and the UI's footnote says so explicitly). const openOpportunity = companyWeightedPipeline * regionBookingsShare * lobShare; const hitRatePct = HIT_RATE_BY_LOB[lobKey]; const weightedFunnel = openOpportunity * (hitRatePct / 100); const supportedBudgetBeforeActualization = backlog + retention + organicGrowth + weightedFunnel; const budget = salesBudgetByRegionLob.get(region.id)[lobKey] || 0; rows.push({ regionId: region.id, regionName: region.name, lob: lobKey, historicalGrowthTrendPct, actualForecast, backlog, retention: lobKey === 'inspections' ? retention : null, organicGrowth, openOpportunity, avgHitRatePct: hitRatePct, weightedFunnel, supportedBudgetBeforeActualization, budget, // Variance/RequiredProposalVolume computed after Actualization is // applied at the company level below is NOT what we want here -- // per the scoping doc, Actualization is a single company-wide // haircut applied once to the company total only, never per-cell // (matching the workbook's own roll-up structure). Each row's own // Variance therefore compares its pre-Actualization Supported // Budget to its own Budget, consistent with how the workbook's // per-region/LOB tabs also show pre-Actualization figures. variance: supportedBudgetBeforeActualization - budget, variancePct: budget > 0 ? (supportedBudgetBeforeActualization - budget) / budget : null, requiredProposalVolume: (supportedBudgetBeforeActualization - budget) < 0 && hitRatePct > 0 ? (budget - supportedBudgetBeforeActualization) / (hitRatePct / 100) : null, }); companyTotals.actualForecast += actualForecast; companyTotals.backlog += backlog; companyTotals.retention += retention; companyTotals.organicGrowth += organicGrowth; companyTotals.openOpportunity += openOpportunity; companyTotals.weightedFunnel += weightedFunnel; companyTotals.supportedBudgetBeforeActualization += supportedBudgetBeforeActualization; companyTotals.budget += budget; } } // Backlog Actualization: a single realism haircut on the company-wide // Supported Budget total, applied once at the very end -- matching // the source workbook's own waterfall structure, where Actualization // is the last roll-up step, not a per-cell multiplier. Stated // explicitly in the UI (see mockup/frontend) so it's clear why the // per-region/LOB rows above don't individually reflect it. const companySupportedBudgetFinal = companyTotals.supportedBudgetBeforeActualization * (assumptions.backlogActualizationPct / 100); const companyVariance = companySupportedBudgetFinal - companyTotals.budget; return { fiscalYear, assumptions, rows, companyTotals: { ...companyTotals, supportedBudgetFinal: companySupportedBudgetFinal, variance: companyVariance, variancePct: companyTotals.budget > 0 ? companyVariance / companyTotals.budget : null, }, companyWeightedPipeline, }; } async function getCoverageUploadStatus(env) { const rows = (await env.DB.prepare( `SELECT source_file, COUNT(*) AS n, SUM(counts_toward_coverage) AS counting FROM coverage_roster GROUP BY source_file` ).all()).results || []; const bySource = { consultants: { rowCount: 0, countingRowCount: 0 }, inspectors: { rowCount: 0, countingRowCount: 0 } }; for (const r of rows) { if (bySource[r.source_file]) bySource[r.source_file] = { rowCount: r.n, countingRowCount: r.counting || 0 }; } return bySource; } // ---- CORS ------------------------------------------------------------- function corsHeaders(origin, env) { const allowed = (env.ALLOWED_ORIGINS || 'https://elevatorpulse.com,https://www.elevatorpulse.com').split(','); const allowOrigin = allowed.includes(origin) ? origin : allowed[0]; return { 'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': allowOrigin, 'Access-Control-Allow-Credentials': 'true', 'Access-Control-Allow-Methods': 'GET, POST, PATCH, DELETE, OPTIONS', 'Access-Control-Allow-Headers': 'Content-Type, Authorization', };
